# Wave digest: 10 slices, 190 findings

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-01 [high] A dispute follow-up recorded with the form's default decision «بلا إجراء» silently lifts a «إيقاف الشحن» stop, so the stopped line goes back to «للشحن» during an open chargeback
src/components/admin/DisputeForm.tsx:63 fix:small conf:high prior:New: the latest-row semantics come from FABLE-AUDIT round M1b (DB-OPS-02 fix, uncommitted); brief F2b-4 adds the «موقوف بنزاع» badge but not the follow-up default.
EVIDENCE: DisputeForm.tsx:63 `const [decision, setDecision] = useState<string>('none')` is the default for every mode, «إضافة متابعة» included (ReconciliationView.tsx:455-468 opens it with `follows: latest.seq` and nothing about the latest row's decision). Since round M1b the stop is the reference's LATEST row only: finance.order_stopped_items (supabase/migrations/20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:39-56) keeps an item only while `not exists (select 1 from finance.disputes n where n.kind = d.kind and n.provider_ref = d.provider_ref and n.environment = d.environment and n.seq > d.seq)`, and fulfillment_update refuses FULFILLMENT_STOPPED only for items in that set (same file :605-614). A follow-up row with decision 'none' therefore ends the stop; the form says nothing about it (no sentence, no pre-selected decision, no item preselection), while `entitlement_revoked` is sticky (only `entitlement_kept` undoes it, :1894-1907), so the two decisions behave in opposite ways in the same form.
IMPACT: The owner records an interim follow-up of a chargeback he stopped shipping for (for example «طلبت Moyasar مستندات وأُرسلت», amount and day filled, decision left at its default): the stop is lifted with no warning, the line reappears in «للشحن» and in the home's toShip count (orders_list/orders_alerts use the same function), and operations ship the goods while the bank still holds the dispute. This is the outcome DB-OPS-02/P08-CKPT-10 (ruled high) was fixed to prevent.
FIX: In «إضافة متابعة» start the decision (and the picked items) from the reference's latest row (`latest.decision`, `latest.itemIds`, passed in the follow mode next to `follows`), and under the select say «قرار هذا السجل يحل محل القرار السابق للمرجع؛ «بلا إجراء» يرفع إيقاف الشحن.» when the latest row is `fulfillment_stopped`. Add an e2e: a follow-up opened on a stopped reference shows «إيقاف الشحن» selected.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-04 [high] «إكمال الطلب» becomes impossible after the owner refunds the missing line, as the screen tells him to: the uncommitted order_resolve reads Moyasar's status 'refunded', which Moyasar sets after a PARTIAL refund, as a full reversal
supabase/migrations/20261007100000_fable_audit_payments.sql:1002 RUNTIME fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-12 (confirmed). Introduced by FABLE-AUDIT M1a-7 (DB-PAY-06 fix); wave B VENDOR-PAY and wave A EF-MONEY-07 record the partial-refund status fact without tracing it to order_resolve.
EVIDENCE: order_resolve (working tree, round M1a-7): `if v_attempt.provider_status in ('refunded', 'voided') or v_attempt.provider_refunded_halalas >= v_attempt.captured_halalas then return ... 'PAYMENT_REVERSED'` (:1002-1006). apply_verified_payment writes the fetched status onto the attempt's own payment whatever follows (`set fetched_at = now(), provider_status = v_status, ...`, same file :366-372); every `payment_*` webhook (payments.ts:485-488, settlePayment) and the owner's «أعد الفحص» (admin.ts paymentRecheck -> settleInvoice, which treats 'refunded' as charged: payments.ts:53) run it. Moyasar's guide, under «Partial Refund»: 'The payment status changes to `refunded`.' (https://docs.moyasar.com/docs/guides/payment-operations/index.md, fetched 2026-10-07) and it sends `payment_refunded` webhooks (https://docs.moyasar.com/webhook-reference); the project's own emulator does the same by default (`partialRefundKeepsPaid: false`, tests/support/moyasar-emulator.ts:220, applied at :753). The screen's own refusal sends the owner down this path: STOCK_UNAVAILABLE «... عدّل المخزون أو أعد مبلغ العنصر أولًا.» (admin-orders.ts:619). The integration case 'A part refunded at the provider is no reversal' (tests/integration/order-operations.test.ts:1384-1389) passes only because its `fetched` helper keeps `status: 'paid'` with `refunded: r.total - 1` (:177-185), a payment Moyasar does not produce.
IMPACT: A paid order lands paid_needs_resolution because one line's stock was gone. The owner refunds that line (a partial refund), Moyasar marks the payment 'refunded', the payment_refunded webhook (or his own «أعد الفحص») records it, and every «تأكيد إكمال الطلب» on the line the buyer still paid for answers PAYMENT_REVERSED (shown as «تعذّر الحفظ. حاول مرة أخرى.» until F2b-4 maps it). The paid line is never committed, receipted, shipped or (for a digital line) granted through the system; the order stays in «تحتاج حلًا» until the owner refunds everything, i.e. the buyer cannot be served once any part was refunded.
FIX: Decide reversal by money and by the one status that means it, not by the word Moyasar also uses for a partial refund: `if v_attempt.provider_status = 'voided' or v_attempt.provider_refunded_halalas >= v_attempt.captured_halalas then PAYMENT_REVERSED`. Change the test's partial case to `status: 'refunded', refunded: r.total - 1` (and keep E02 recording the status after a real partial refund).
PROOF: On the local stack with M1a applied: make a two-line paid_needs_resolution order (one variant at stock 0 when the payment lands, as tests/integration/order-operations.test.ts `stuck` does); refund the out-of-stock line through refund_request/refund_result; then call apply_verified_payment for the order's own payment with p_payment {status: 'refunded', refunded: <that line's total>, amount: <order total>, ...} (what Moyasar returns after a partial refund); raise the other variant's stock; call order_resolve(p_order) as the owner. Wrong result today: {ok:false, code:'PAYMENT_REVERSED'}; expected after the fix: {ok:true, status:'paid'}.

### DSN-PAGES-08 [high] /book never leads to the purchase: the editions section hard-codes «قريباً» and «السعر يُعلن قريباً» and has no link to the store, so the day checkout opens the book page still says the book cannot be bought yet
src/components/public/book/BookView.tsx:233 fix:medium conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-08 (found independently; raised to high: the main sale has no path once checkout opens); P07 file list «book purchase wiring» never built
EVIDENCE: BookView.tsx:233-260: the «الطلب» section (SECTIONS id editions, reached by the hero's «النسخ ←» and the reader's last leaf, PdfBookReader.tsx:607) renders <h2>قريباً</h2>, the CMS status lines, and for each CMS edition <span>السعر</span><span>يُعلن قريباً</span>; nothing reads the store (getProducts in src/lib/store.ts has the variants and price_halalas at build time) or the checkout state, and no element links out: book.mjs lists every href in <main> of /book and finds only #anchors (links: []). No public page links to /store at all (grep of src outside the admin: only CartView and CheckoutForm link back to /store; the seeded nav has no store item). WORK-PACKAGES.md P07 lists «book purchase wiring» in its files; P07 was accepted (EXECUTION-STATUS ledger) without it, and nothing in P08-CONTRACT or P09-P11 takes it up. The book page lists two CMS editions (رقمية، ورقية موقّعة) while the demo catalog (D37) sells three (e-book, paper, signed): the two lists are edited in different places and can disagree.
IMPACT: D34 launches with the store «قريباً», which the page matches. Once E02/E03 close and the owner turns the «الشراء» switch on, the buyer who comes to the book from the home door, the nav or the reader's closing leaf is told the price is still to be announced and has no way to the product page; the only way to buy is to type /store. That is the site's main sale, and it would embarrass the commerce launch; the stale «يُعلن قريباً» also contradicts the live prices (DESIGN.md section 8 says the words hold only until Anas sets the prices).
FIX: Link each edition card to its store variant: give the book document (or the editions) the product slug, read getProducts() in BookPage at build time, and render the store price and an ActionLink to /store/<slug> when the variant is priced and sellable, keeping «يُعلن قريباً» only for an unpriced one; until checkout opens the store page itself says «قريباً». Record the wiring as an explicit item (P07 left it), and decide with Anas whether the nav gets «المتجر».

### DSN-PAGES-10 [high] The scenes lightbox does not fit the photo to the screen: on desktop, tablet and a phone held sideways most photos spill far past the stage, show only their top and cover the caption
src/components/public/scenes/scenes.module.css:199 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-07 (confirmed with my own measurement)
EVIDENCE: .photo { max-inline-size: 100%; max-block-size: 100%; inline-size: auto; block-size: auto; object-fit: contain } (lines 199-205) sits in .stage (lines 192-198: display grid, place-items center, no grid-template-rows), so the img lives in one auto row sized by its own content and the percentage max-block-size never caps it. lightfit.mjs stepped through all 19 photos on the export: does not fit the stage in 17/19 at 1440x900 (the first, a 1440x2560 portrait, is drawn 1267x2253 in a 758px-high stage, top 68 to bottom 2321), 19/19 at 1280x720, 16/19 at 1024x768, 12/19 at 768x1024, 19/19 at 844x390 (phone landscape), 0/19 only at 360x740; elementFromPoint at the caption hits the photo, not the caption, on 16/19 at 1440x900. shots/lightfit-1440x900.png: blue sky fills the screen, the street sign itself is at the bottom edge, the caption and category are hidden under the photo. A single-photo check (my own first scenes.mjs, and public.spec.ts:109-122 which only reads the counter) opens a landscape poster that happens to fit.
IMPACT: Every visitor on a computer, a tablet or a phone turned sideways who opens a photo in المَشاهد sees a cropped strip of it (for portraits mostly sky or floor) instead of the picture, and cannot read Anas's caption: the gallery's one interaction is broken everywhere except a portrait phone, on a page linked from the header and the footer of every page.
FIX: Give the image a definite box: .stage { grid-template: minmax(0, 1fr) / minmax(0, 1fr) } and .photo { inline-size: 100%; block-size: 100%; object-fit: contain } (or position the img absolutely inside .stage with inset 0), then add a lightbox check that steps every photo at 1440x900 and 844x390 and asserts the img rect is inside the stage.

### A11Y-RTL-01 [medium] At 320 CSS px the header does not fit: «القائمة» runs 22px past the left edge, its label is clipped, and on a phone the whole page pans sideways (every public page)
src/components/site/site.module.css:39 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-01
EVIDENCE: Measured on the export at 320x640 (hdr.mjs): the header bar holds the brand (191px: Mark 36 + «أنس» 48 + «anas.studio» 83), a 20px gap and the 111px button inside 20px gutters. The button rect is x=-22..89 and the text node «القائمة» spans x=-4..41, so body overflow-x:clip cuts its last letter. documentElement.scrollWidth is 342 against clientWidth 320 on all 25 public pages at 320 (crawl.mjs json/*-320.json; the only offscreen element is button «القائمة» [-22,89]). At 340 the button starts at x=-2, at 360 at x=8. Screenshot: shots/hdr-320.png. On a 320x568 phone (isMobile, hasTouch; leadcheck.mjs) the layout viewport widens to innerWidth 342 against a 320 visual viewport and scrollX starts at -22. A right-to-left swipe moves visualViewport.offsetLeft to 22: the page pans sideways and the reading start of every line (the title «بدأتُ من هنا», the tagline) is cut at the right edge (shots/mobile320-after-swipe.png). No suite runs 320: tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts:31 has WIDTHS = [360, 768, 1024, 1440].
IMPACT: Fails WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow at 320 CSS px (a 320px phone, or 1280px at 400% zoom). Every page scrolls in two directions, a sideways pan hides the start of each Arabic line, and below 1024px the only navigation control is clipped («القائم…»).
FIX: Let the brand give way at the narrowest widths, e.g. `@media (max-width: 359px) { .brandDomain { display: none } }` (the link's aria-label already names it), or let the bar wrap.

### A11Y-RTL-02 [medium] Shift+Tab lands controls under the sticky header: the focused «أضف إلى السلة» and its quantity field are fully hidden (WCAG 2.4.11)
src/styles/globals.css:130 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-02
EVIDENCE: html has no scroll-padding: grep finds scroll-padding nowhere in src. SiteHeader.tsx:81-82 brings the header back on any upward scroll, including the browser's own focus scroll. obscured2.mjs on /store/demo-khous at 1440x900 with reduced motion, walking back with Shift+Tab from the footer: the first variant's button and its quantity input land at top=0 while the header spans 0..68, covered=1.00. shots/obscured-store_demo-khous-1440-no-preference-1.png (from the motion-on run) shows the focused row under the header. At 360 the input lands at top=25, 78% covered. On /orders at 1440 (reduced motion) the recovery email input lands at top=0, 0% visible. With `html{scroll-padding-block-start:var(--header-h)}` injected, the same steps give top=456 and top=68, covered 0.
IMPACT: A keyboard user moving backwards on a long page (the product page's buy controls, the cart, the forms) cannot see the focused control at all. The sticky header is author content, so this fails WCAG 2.2 SC 2.4.11 Focus Not Obscured (Minimum, AA).
FIX: Add `scroll-padding-block-start: var(--header-h);` to the `html` rule in globals.css. Use the constant, not --header-offset, which is 0 at the moment of the focus scroll because the header is hidden then. /book also sticks its 54px section bar under the header, so that page needs the bar added, for example `:global(html):has(.bar) { scroll-padding-block-start: calc(var(--header-h) + 54px) }` in section-nav.module.css.

### A11Y-RTL-03 [medium] The scenes lightbox does not fit photos to the screen on desktop and tablet: portrait photos overflow by up to 1,495px and cover the caption and category
src/components/public/scenes/scenes.module.css:192 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-03
EVIDENCE: `.stage { display: grid; place-items: center; min-block-size: 0 }` has an implicit auto row, so `.photo { max-block-size: 100% }` (lines 199-205) resolves against a row that grows with the image. lightbox2.mjs stepped through all 19 photos with ArrowLeft. At 1440x900, 17/19 photos overflow the 758px stage: photo 1 is drawn 1267x2253 from y=68, overflows by 1495px, the dialog scrolls 1421px, and elementFromPoint at the caption's centre hits the <img>. Counts at other sizes: 1024x768 16/19, 768x1024 12/19, 320x568 7/19, 360x720 0/19. Screenshots: shots/lightbox-first-1440x900.png (only the sky of «لوحة شارع رقم 4، تبوك» shows, the caption is a sliver at the right edge) and shots/lightbox-1440.png. With `grid-template-rows: minmax(0,1fr); grid-template-columns: minmax(0,1fr)` injected on the stage, 0/19 overflow at every size (shots/lightbox-fixed-1440x900.png). The rule is unchanged since 2f54f85 (DESIGN-B), and tests/e2e/public.spec.ts:109 checks only the counter. dialogs.mjs at 1440: the Tab cycle inside the open viewer includes the <dialog> «عارض الصور» itself, a keyboard-focusable scroller; at 360, where the photos fit, it does not.
IMPACT: On a laptop or tablet the viewer shows only the top third of most photos and hides the caption and category that name them. The user has to scroll inside the modal (the step arrows scroll away with it), and at 1440 the scrollable dialog itself becomes an extra Tab stop whose focus ring is drawn off screen.
FIX: In `.stage` add `grid-template-rows: minmax(0, 1fr); grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);` so the photo's 100% max sizes resolve against the stage.

### A11Y-RTL-04 [medium] «تأكيد الطلب» (and the hold view's «إلغاء الطلب» and «أعد المحاولة») become `disabled` while their request runs, which drops keyboard focus to <body>; a failed attempt never restores it
src/components/store/CheckoutForm.tsx:790 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-04; AUDIT-1 S14.5 (fixed for the success paths only)
EVIDENCE: Line 790 sets `disabled={!turnstileAvailable || submitting || waiting || stale || noCities || policiesStale}`; HoldView.tsx:224, 262 and 267 set `disabled={submitting}`. Chromium 153 moves activeElement to BODY when the focused button is disabled and does not restore it when the button is enabled again (disabledfocus.mjs: before=b, during=BODY, afterReenable=BODY). Real flows with a mocked API (flows.mjs, mockapi.mjs; nothing left the browser): a checkout submit whose `create` fails on the network gives during=BODY and after=BODY, with only the alert «تعذّر الاتصال بالخدمة؛ أعد المحاولة.»; the hold view's «إلغاء الطلب» with a failing cancel also gives during=BODY and after=BODY. The team's own NotifyAction.tsx:114 says 'aria-disabled, not disabled: the button keeps the focus while its own request runs', and AvailabilityForm.tsx:189 does the same. The owner's step-up «تحقق» (StepUp.tsx:88) follows the same pattern.
IMPACT: On the money path, a keyboard or screen-reader buyer whose order attempt fails (network, QUOTE_CHANGED, POLICY_CHANGED, ACTIVE_HOLD without the order, any 4xx) is left on <body> with no visible focus, and a screen reader may restart from the top of the page. The same happens on a failed cancel or retry in the hold view and on the owner's step-up.
FIX: Follow the NotifyAction pattern: keep these buttons enabled during the request with `aria-disabled={submitting || undefined}` (the handlers already return early on `submitting`), and keep `disabled` only for the static reasons (no Turnstile, stale quote, no cities, policies updating).

### ADMIN-CMS-01 [medium] Two tabs of one document share one local-copy key: a save, an edit or «تجاهل» in one tab deletes or overwrites the other tab's unsaved copy, so the conflict path loses the text it says is kept
src/components/admin/CollectionForm.tsx:209 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-01; AUDIT-1 S08.2/S08.3 and AUDIT-2 ADMIN-editor-2/-3 fixed the single-tab and per-user cases only
EVIDENCE: The key is per document only: `return `anasaq:draft:${collection}:${docId}`` (CollectionForm.tsx:54). Every change runs `draftStorageAction(localOffer !== null, data, initialData)` then `window.localStorage.removeItem(draftKey(collection, docId))` when the form equals its saved version, or `setItem` with this tab's data (205-218); after a successful save the saving tab's form equals its saved version (266-267), so it removes the shared key; «تجاهل» in a tab that was offered another tab's copy calls clearStoredDraft (244-248). No 'storage' listener or beforeunload exists in src/components/admin (grep). The conflict reply then says «تغيّر هذا المستند منذ فتحته. نصّك محفوظ هنا؛ حمّل آخر نسخة ثم أعد التعديل.» (35, 263), and the only way to load the latest version is a page reload, whose load() reads that same key (176). The e2e 'a stale save is rejected with a conflict message and keeps the typed text' (tests/e2e/cms.spec.ts:659-693) uses two separate browser contexts (no shared localStorage) and only checks the in-memory input.
IMPACT: Anas has the same post open in two tabs, types a paragraph in tab B, then saves a small fix in tab A: tab A's save removes the stored copy. Tab B's «حفظ» answers that his text is kept; he reloads as told and the paragraph is gone with no offer. The same loss happens if tab B is closed or crashes after tab A saved (the S08.3 protection depends on this copy), and if tab A only typed after tab B, tab B's reload offers tab A's text as its own.
FIX: Key the copy per tab as well: a random tab id kept in sessionStorage (it survives that tab's reloads) appended to the key; the autosave writes and removes only its own tab's key; load() offers this tab's copy, else the newest same-user copy of another tab. clearDrafts() already removes by prefix.
PROOF: Playwright, ONE browser context, two pages signed in as an owner on /admin/content/posts/edit?id=<saved post>. Page B: fill «مقتطف» with 'B text' (no save). Page A: change «العنوان», click «حفظ» (expect «تم الحفظ.»). Page B: localStorage.getItem('anasaq:draft:posts:<id>') -> null (wrong: B's text had no other copy). Click «حفظ» on page B -> «...نصّك محفوظ هنا...». Reload page B -> no «يوجد تعديل غير محفوظ محليًا لهذا المستند.» heading and «مقتطف» lacks 'B text' (wrong: text lost).

### ADMIN-CMS-02 [medium] A new post that was never saved is autosaved only under its random id and no screen lists it: leaving the page (for example to upload its cover in «المكتبة») orphans the whole draft
src/components/admin/CollectionList.tsx:90 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-10; AUDIT-1 S08.3 protects existing documents only
EVIDENCE: «جديد» opens a fresh uuid: `router.push(documentHref('posts', crypto.randomUUID().toLowerCase()))` (CollectionList.tsx:95). Until the first «حفظ» nothing exists in the database; the autosave keeps the text only as `anasaq:draft:posts:<uuid>` (CollectionForm.tsx:54, 205-218). The list is built only from database rows: `: rows ? Array.from(rows.keys()) : []` over content_documents (CollectionList.tsx:64-74, 90-92). The image picker only browses the library (MediaPicker.tsx:41), so uploading a new cover means leaving for /admin/media. No code reads draft keys except AdminShell's sign-out check hasDrafts() (AdminShell.tsx:42-48), which warns «لديك تعديلات غير محفوظة على هذا الجهاز...» and offers no way to open them. No beforeunload or route guard exists. The header promises «Autosave keeps an unsaved local copy in localStorage so a conflict or a closed tab never loses text» (CollectionForm.tsx:7-8).
IMPACT: Anas starts an article with «جديد», writes for an hour and closes the tab, or goes to «المكتبة» to upload its cover, without pressing «حفظ» (the form has no unsaved indicator): the text sits in localStorage under a uuid no list or link shows; «جديد» makes another empty post, and the next sign-out deletes the orphan after a warning he cannot act on.
FIX: List this user's local copies of documents with no database row (keys `anasaq:draft:posts:*` and `anasaq:draft:taxonomies:*`) at the top of CollectionList as «مسودة غير محفوظة على هذا الجهاز» with links to their edit pages; or save version 1 on the first edit of a new document.

### ADMIN-CMS-04 [medium] Publishing or scheduling an approved policy closes the store's checkout until the owner re-approves, but the policy editor never says so and reports plain success
src/components/admin/PublishBar.tsx:93 fix:trivial conf:high prior:FABLE wave A DB-CORE-01 (owner-side alert, M1b-7/F2b-2); AUDIT-1 X1.1 (the reset itself); the publisher-side warning is not covered
EVIDENCE: «نشر» and «جدولة» run publishDocument/scheduleDocument for every collection alike and answer «نشر: تم بنجاح. يظهر التعديل على الموقع خلال دقائق.» (PublishBar.tsx:63, 81, 93). The trigger on published_documents clears finance.commerce_settings.policy_revisions whenever a policy goes live at a seq other than the approved one, from publish_version and publish_due alike (20260930120000_audit_fixes.sql:119-158, kept by 20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:1317-1360), which closes checkout ('POLICIES_UNAPPROVED', same file :1294-1298). content_assert_publisher lets an editor publish policies (20260925120000_content_versions_and_publishing.sql:116-131). The only text about approval is on the owner-only settings screen (CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:229-243); nothing in CollectionForm, PublishBar or the policy collection mentions it.
IMPACT: After launch an editor (or Anas) fixes a typo in «سياسة الاسترجاع» and publishes, or schedules it for midnight: every buyer's checkout is refused from that moment until the owner opens the settings and approves again. The publisher is told the change succeeded; the owner learns from the alert mail (FABLE M1b-7, only while the switch is on) or from buyers, possibly hours later.
FIX: For collection 'policies', show above «نشر»/«جدولة» and append to the success message: «نشر تعديل على سياسة معتمدة يوقف الشراء حتى يعيد المالك اعتماد السياسات من الإعدادات.»

### ADMIN-CMS-08 [medium] A new article starts with «ظاهر» unticked, so publishing it reports success and «منشور» while the journal never shows it
src/admin/collections/posts.ts:16 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-13
EVIDENCE: `{ name: 'visible', label: 'ظاهر', type: 'boolean' }` (posts.ts:16) defaults through `case 'boolean': return false` (src/admin/fields.ts:168-169), which a new post opens with (CollectionForm.tsx:165-167). postSchema accepts false; PublishBar answers «نشر: تم بنجاح. يظهر التعديل على الموقع خلال دقائق.» (PublishBar.tsx:63); CollectionList says «منشور» from seq numbers alone (CollectionList.tsx:41-52). The site drops it: anon reads a post only when `data -> 'visible' = 'true'` (20260930120000_audit_fixes.sql:49-51) and the build keeps `posts.filter(({ post }) => post.visible)` (src/lib/journal.ts:81). Every e2e that publishes a post ticks the box first (tests/e2e/cms.spec.ts:112, 160).
IMPACT: Anas writes and publishes his first article without noticing the last checkbox: the bar promises it on the site within minutes, the list says «منشور», the preview shows it, yet /journal never lists it and its link is a 404, with nothing on screen to explain why.
FIX: Start new posts with visible true (set it in the new-post defaults), and make statusFor and the publish message say «منشور ومخفي عن الموقع» when the live data has visible false.

### ADMIN-CMS-09 [medium] Gallery, book and portrait photos publish with an empty alt: the CMS alt field starts blank, the publish gate accepts it, and those views never fall back to the alt the library required
src/admin/collections/rooms.ts:43 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-04; AUDIT-2 4.8 (PUBLIC-data-3) covered StoryFigure only
EVIDENCE: galleryPhotoFields' `{ name: 'alt', label: 'الوصف البديل', type: 'text' }` (rooms.ts:41-44) is reused by the passed gallery, the shelf photos and images, and the book's cover, standing, spine, bookmark and photos (rooms.ts:158, 185, 194, 240-245, 258-261); the home has portraitAlt (site-settings.ts:60). None is nonBlank and no publish rule checks them (rooms.ts:321-346, site-settings.ts:156-212). «أضف عنصرًا» builds items from defaultsForFields (FieldInput.tsx:744-751), so alt is ''; the picker sets only the id (FieldInput.tsx:221-227). The views pass the CMS value to <img alt>: PassedRoomView.tsx:117, ShelfRoomView.tsx:138, BookView.tsx:191 and 219-227, HomeView.tsx:49, through Picture.tsx:37; only StoryFigure falls back (`alt={alt || note.alt}`, story/Story.tsx:97), and media-notes.ts:8-9 says lists with their own alt never read the library alt.
IMPACT: Every product, book or gallery photo Anas adds is published as alt="" (decorative) unless he notices the empty «الوصف البديل» box, although the library made him write an alt for that image at upload: screen-reader visitors of /passed, /shelf, /book and the home portrait get nothing (WCAG 1.1.1), the defect AUDIT-2 4.8 fixed for story figures only.
FIX: Add a publish-only rule in roomPublishSchemas and siteSettingsSchema refusing a blank alt where an image id is set («اكتب الوصف البديل للصورة.»), and prefill an empty sibling alt from the library row's alt_ar when the picker returns an image.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-05 [medium] A stale order view (a second tab, or a second staff device) can refund the same money again: the refund carries nothing of the ledger state its form was built from, while every catalog form has a version check
src/lib/admin-money.ts:195 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-16 (confirmed). Round 11b accepted 'remainders shown meanwhile can be stale', relying on REFUND_IN_FLIGHT and EXCEEDS_BALANCE, which do not stop a sequential partial refund.
EVIDENCE: refundBody sends only `{action, orderId, attemptId, amount, reason, allocation[, returnId]}` (admin-money.ts:180-204); the remainders and the refunds table the owner saw (`item.total - item.refunded`, :105; OrderView.tsx:998-1057) are not sent. refundCreateSchema is a strictObject of exactly those keys (refunds.ts:61-74) and refund_request decides only by the key, REFUND_IN_FLIGHT, the provider total, CHARGEBACK_RECORDED, EXCEEDS_BALANCE and refund_allocation_ok against the ledger as it is now (20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:857-952). A screen re-reads only after its own actions (OrderView.tsx:158-162, :221); nothing refreshes a view opened earlier. By contrast the catalog forms send `.eq('version', <read>)` and stop a stale save (TableForm.tsx:217-222).
IMPACT: The owner refunds 30 SAR of a 100 SAR line from his phone after a complaint; later, on the laptop tab he opened before, the refunds table shows no refund and the line still offers 100, so he refunds the complaint again: the server sees 70 left and sends a second 30 SAR to the buyer's card. The same happens with two tabs of one session, or after the «حدّث الصفحة» advice of ADMIN-COMMERCE-07. Money sent to a card is not recoverable from the admin.
FIX: Send the ledger state the form was built from (the paying attempt's confirmed refunded total, or the review payment's `refunded`) as `expectedRefunded` in the body and in the request hash; refund_request answers a new refusal STALE when it differs (checked after the idempotency lookup, so a replay still answers the stored refund); the screen words it «تغيّر المسترد منذ فتحت هذه الصفحة؛ راجع جدول الاستردادات ثم أعد المحاولة.» and re-reads.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-18 [medium] «تم الشحن» from a stale order view silently rewrites another staff member's carrier and tracking: the uncommitted fulfillment_update infers a 'correction' from the line's state, so a second shipment of the same line is no longer refused
supabase/migrations/20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:536 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:New; introduced by FABLE-AUDIT M1b section 3 (the DB-POST-10 correction). Brief F2b-4(d) adds the correction UI but not an explicit intent.
EVIDENCE: fulfillment_update (working tree, M1b-3/DB-POST-10 fix): `v_correct := p_state = 'shipped' and not exists (select 1 from finance.fulfillments f where ... f.state <> 'shipped')` (:536-539); with v_correct a shipped line passes the transition check (:547) and the call overwrites carrier and tracking, mails nothing and answers `{ok:true, changed, itemIds, corrected:true}` (:586-603). Before M1b the same call was BAD_TRANSITION. The only call that ships is the screen's «تم الشحن»: `rpc('fulfillment_update', {p_item_ids: sendable, p_state: 'shipped', p_carrier, p_tracking, ...})` (OrderView.tsx:250-261), and the screen has no refresh while drawn, so a line another person shipped still shows «قيد التجهيز» and stays choosable (:689-699). parseActionReply ignores `corrected` (admin-orders.ts:579-593) and the done sentence for any changed > 0 is «تم التحديث.» (OrderView.tsx:262-271).
IMPACT: Staff A ships a line (SMSA, 111) and the buyer is mailed that tracking. Staff B, whose view predates it, packs the same line and presses «تم الشحن» with (Aramex, 222): the server treats it as a correction, the buyer's order page now shows Aramex 222 while his mail says SMSA 111, no one is told that the line was already shipped, and two parcels may be on their way. The conflict signal (BAD_TRANSITION, «لا تنتقل هذه العناصر إلى هذه الحالة.») that used to stop B is gone; F2b-4(d)'s planned «تصحيح بيانات الشحن» button does not restore it, because the server still decides by state, not by intent.
FIX: Make the correction explicit: add `p_correct boolean` to fulfillment_update (or a separate `fulfillment_correct`), correct only when it is true (and only lines already shipped), and keep a shipped line under a plain «تم الشحن» call with another carrier or tracking a BAD_TRANSITION as before; the screen's «تصحيح بيانات الشحن» (F2b-4 d) sends the flag, «تم الشحن» never does.
PROOF: On the local stack with M1b applied, as an operations member: for a paid order with one physical line, call fulfillment_update(p_order, [item], 'shipped', 'SMSA', '111', null) then fulfillment_update(p_order, [item], 'shipped', 'Aramex', '222', null). Wrong result today: the second call answers {ok:true, changed:1, corrected:true} and finance.fulfillments shows Aramex/222; expected after the fix: {ok:false, code:'BAD_TRANSITION'} with SMSA/111 kept.

### DSN-HOME-01 [medium] Header bar needs 342px: below that the «القائمة» button slides off the left edge (cut at 320px, label gone at 280px) and the page overflows sideways
src/components/site/site.module.css:16 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-01
EVIDENCE: hdr.mjs (export at 127.0.0.1:4173, /started, reduced motion): at 320px the bar's scrollWidth is 342, brand spans x 109-300 (mark 36 + «أنس» 48 + «anas.studio» 83 + gaps) and the menu button spans x -22..89; at 280px the button spans -62..49. document.documentElement.scrollWidth = 342 at 280/320/340 (survey.mjs, every slice page at 320). Screenshots shots/hdr-320.png («القائمـ», final letter cut) and shots/hdr-280.png (only the mark shows, no label). .bar is `display:flex; justify-content:space-between; gap:20px` with no wrap or shrink, the brand's three parts cannot shrink, and .menuButton adds `margin-inline-end: calc(var(--gutter) * -1 + 8px)` (line 81). In an RTL document the overflow lies on the scrollable (left) side: with Playwright's mobile emulation (isMobile, hasTouch) /started loads already scrolled to scrollX -22, the h1's right edge at 322px (hdrfocus.mjs, shots/hdrfocus-320-mobile.png); desktop emulation stays at scrollX 0.
IMPACT: On a 320 CSS px screen (iPhone SE 1st gen, small Androids, a 360px phone at 112% page zoom, a desktop at 400% zoom) the only navigation control on a phone is cut off and the page has 22px of sideways overflow (in mobile emulation the page even loads shifted by those 22px, cutting every page's right edge; a real 320px device should confirm); at 280px (Galaxy Fold cover screen) the menu button shows no label. WCAG 2.1 1.4.10 Reflow is measured exactly at 320 CSS px. Every public page shares this header.
FIX: Let the brand give way on narrow screens: hide the Latin domain under 360px, e.g. `@media (max-width: 359.98px) { .brandDomain { display: none; } }` in site.module.css (the bar then needs about 247px), or give `.brand` and `.brandDomain` `min-inline-size: 0; overflow: hidden` so the domain shrinks first. Add 320 to the visual e2e widths.

### DSN-HOME-02 [medium] Home room doors break into an uneven grid at the two commonest desktop widths: 6 doors + 2 triple-width doors at 1920px, 5 + 3 at 1536px, 3 + 3 + 2 at 1024px
src/components/public/home/home.module.css:92 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-04
EVIDENCE: `.doors { display:flex; flex-wrap:wrap }` (line 85) and `.doorItem { flex: 1 1 300px }`, so the last row's doors grow to fill it. grids.mjs on the export (8 doors from the seed): 1920px rows [6 x 320px][2 x 960px]; 1024px [3 x 341][3 x 341][2 x 512]; 1440 and 1280 are 4 + 4. Screenshot shots/el-home-doors-1920.png: «المَشاهد» and «تواصل» become 960px banners three times wider than «بدأتُ من هنا», «بنيتُ هنا» and the other rooms.
IMPACT: At 1920x1080 (the most common desktop screen) and 1536 (the same screen at 125% scaling) the home page's main block gives the two secondary doors (scenes, contact) three times the size of the four rooms: the composition reads as accidental and the hierarchy is inverted; DESIGN-AUDIT item 44 (parallel columns) fails. The visual e2e includes 1024 but nobody judged its 3+3+2 row.
FIX: Lay the doors on a grid whose column count divides the row: `.doors { display:grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr }`, `repeat(2, 1fr)` from 600px and `repeat(4, 1fr)` from 1200px; the door count is CMS-driven (1-8), so a short last row keeps equal widths instead of stretching.

### DSN-PAGES-01 [medium] At 320px the header bar is 42px too wide: «القائمة» is cut 22px past the left edge, and on a phone every page opens panned left with the start of each line cut
src/components/site/site.module.css:39 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-01 (found independently, same cause); none in AUDIT-1/2 or tasmeem (they measured 360+)
EVIDENCE: Measured on the export (pages.mjs, w320.mjs, w320b.mjs): at 320px every page in the slice (and every page, the header is shared) reports documentElement.scrollWidth 342 > clientWidth 320; the only element outside is BUTTON.menuButton at [-22,89]. The bar content box is 280px but needs brand 191px (mark 36 + أنس 48 + «anas.studio» 83 + gaps) + gap 20 + button 111 - 12 (margin-inline-end: calc(var(--gutter) * -1 + 8px), line 81) = 310px. With isMobile emulation the layout viewport grows to 342 (innerWidth 342, scrollX -22): the page loads showing doc x [-22,298], so a 22px empty strip on the left and the first 22px of every RTL line cut on the right (shots/hdr-320-mobile.png, hdr2-320-mobile.png: «إذا كانت لديك», «الاسم», «المجلس» cut). At 360 it fits (scrollWidth 360).
IMPACT: WCAG 1.4.10 Reflow fails at its own test width (320 CSS px, also a 1280px window at 400% or a 640px window at 200%): on a 320px phone (iPhone SE 1st gen, small Androids) every page opens sideways-scrollable with the beginning of every Arabic line hidden until the visitor pans; on a narrow desktop window the menu button label is clipped. The visual suite only runs 360+, so nothing catches it.
FIX: Drop the domain from the header on the narrowest screens, e.g. @media (max-width: 359.98px) { .brandDomain { display: none } } in site.module.css (saves 95px), or let .brand shrink with min-inline-size: 0 and the domain ellipsized; add 320 to the visual suite widths.

### DSN-PAGES-05 [medium] A journal post title never gets its own size (--size-post-title): .t-title wins the cascade, so titles render at 68-200px with no overflow-wrap, and ordinary long Arabic words overflow on phones
src/components/public/journal/journal.module.css:167 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-02 and lead:DSN-PAGES-03 (merged: one cause, one place)
EVIDENCE: PostView.tsx:40 sets className={`t-title ${styles.postTitle}`}; .postTitle { font-size: var(--size-post-title) } (clamp(56px,10vw,168px)) and .t-title { font-size: var(--size-title) } (clamp(68px,12.4vw,200px)) are both one class. out/journal.html and out/journal/_.html load 37lmkiv9a5z5_.css (every CSS module, journal included) BEFORE 3m_iako6q6ww8.css (globals.css: @font-face and the .t-* roles), so .t-title wins. article.mjs (PostView markup on the export CSS): h1 font-size 68px at 320/360, 95.2px at 768, 178.56px at 1440 (= 12.4vw, --size-title), never 56-168. .postTitle has no overflow-wrap (RoomHero .title has overflow-wrap: anywhere; the store product title got it in 12a). titlewords.mjs, Serif Display 500: at 68px «والمشروبات» 284px, «والاستثمارات» 320, «بالمسؤوليات» 307, «الاستراتيجيات» 336, 'ChatGPT' 298, all wider than the 280px column at 320, and «الاستراتيجيات» also wider than the 320px column at 360; at the intended 56px every Arabic word tested fits (max 277). With «كيف تبدأ مشروعك الأول في الأطعمة والمشروبات» the h1 measured scrollWidth 284 > clientWidth 280 at 320, and 5 lines of one or two words at 360 (shots/article-360-head.png).
IMPACT: On phones a post title is set about 20% larger than designed, one or two words a line, and any long word (common in Arabic with its prefixes) or Latin term runs past the column into the gutter and, at 320, off the screen where body overflow-x: clip cuts it. The token DESIGN.md names for the post title is dead, so changing it does nothing.
FIX: Make the post title independent of .t-title: drop t-title from the h1 and give .postTitle the role's family, weight, line-height 1.25 and text-wrap: balance (or raise specificity, e.g. h1.postTitle), and add overflow-wrap: anywhere as RoomHero .title has. The same post title is printed at 44px in the lead journal card (t-h2) and 36px in the RoomNav door (.name), where only a long Latin run overflows ('Entrepreneurship' is about 382px at 44px in a 234px card body at 320); give .cardBody h2/h3 and .name overflow-wrap: anywhere too.

### DSN-PAGES-06 [medium] In an article a quotation is set exactly like a section heading: same face, size, weight and colour as h2, with no mark, rule or indent
src/components/public/journal/journal.module.css:211 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-04 (found independently; I rate it medium: it changes who seems to be speaking)
EVIDENCE: .prose h2 { font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: var(--size-display) } and .prose blockquote { margin-inline: 0; padding-inline-start: 0; font-family: var(--font-display); font-weight: 500; font-size: var(--size-display); line-height: 1.55 } (lines 197-218). Computed at 1440 (article-report.json): h2 48.96px / 500 / Serif Display / rgb(84,53,59); blockquote 48.96px / 500 / Serif Display / rgb(84,53,59); only line-height (68.5 vs 75.9px) and the top margin differ, and the h3 (40px) is smaller than any quote. shots/article-1440-prose.png: «الطبق باب، والرائحة طريق.» reads as a heading above the h3, and a four-line quote reads as a giant section title.
IMPACT: Whenever Anas quotes someone in a post (the editor offers the quote block, D14), readers see the quotation as his own section heading: the attribution is lost and the outline of the article looks wrong. DESIGN-AUDIT 16 also rules out oversized quote decoration, while the book page sets its quotations with guillemets and a source.
FIX: Give .prose blockquote the site's quotation language at reading scale: the --size-quote size (or the body size), hanging «» or an inline-start rule in saffron-ink, and a margin-inline-start, so it is visibly a quotation and smaller than h3.

### PERF-01 [medium] The two door pictures at the top of /shelf are lazy-loaded although they sit in the first screen; on a phone the first one is the LCP element and is only requested after the first layout
src/components/public/rooms/ShelfRoomView.tsx:77 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:PERF-03 (same defect; its own A/B at DPR 3 gave a median 5584 -> 2696 ms). AUDIT-1 X7.2 fixed the same defect for the journal lead card, the store grid and the product cover; the shelf doors were not covered.
EVIDENCE: ShelfRoomView.tsx:77 `<Picture id={thuraDoor.id} alt="" sizes="(min-width: 1024px) 33vw, 100vw" className={shelf.doorPicture} />` (and :86 for the moon door) pass no `loading`, so Picture.tsx:19 defaults to `loading = 'lazy'`. Measured on out/ (scratch runs/mob/shelf.json, 360x800 DPR 2, CPU 4x, DevTools Slow 4G): LCP = img /images/shelf/thura-81-720.webp at 6920 ms; that request starts at 4951 ms (after the first layout) and ends at 6596 ms. At 1440 (runs/desk/shelf.json) both door images are in the first viewport and still `loading=lazy`. A/B on the same profile with the HTML served through the same route (scratch ab.mjs, 3 runs each): base LCP 5664/6124/4920 ms, with the two doors eager (first one fetchpriority=high) 4264/4812/4088 ms: about 1.4 s earlier at the median. Every other page's hero image passes `loading="eager"` (HomeView.tsx:51, BookView.tsx:63, store/[slug]/page.tsx:80).
IMPACT: Every visitor to /shelf on a phone waits for HTML, CSS, the first full layout and only then the door photo: the largest paint of the page lands 1.4 to 2.9 s later than it needs to on a slow connection (this audit's median and the earlier attempt's), the opposite of the plan goal LCP < 2.5 s.
FIX: In ShelfRoomView.tsx pass loading="eager" fetchPriority="high" to the first door Picture (line 77) and loading="eager" to the second (line 86).

### PERF-04 [medium] The cart, checkout and order pages are static shells only 50vh tall, so the footer starts in the first screen and the client island pushes it away: CLS 0.35 (cart), 0.42 (checkout), 0.12 (orders) on a phone
src/components/store/store.module.css:29 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:PERF-05 (same defect and fix; its numbers reproduced here within 0.001).
EVIDENCE: store.module.css:28-30 `.page { min-block-size: 50vh; }` is the only height of `<main>` on the money pages (cart/page.tsx:14, checkout/page.tsx:23, checkout/return/page.tsx:14, orders/page.tsx:14, notify/confirm/page.tsx:14, notify/unsubscribe/page.tsx:14); each is a client island whose static state is a loading line or an empty band (OrderPage.tsx:476-478), so the footer starts at y 496 at 360x800 and y 561 at 1440x900. Measured with a filled cart and the quote and shipping rates answered inside the browser by route.fulfill (scratch clsmock.mjs, mouse context, nothing reached a server), 360 at 4x CPU and Slow 4G: /cart 0.3465 / 0.3464 (footer pushed out at 5.5 s, 0.161, then the summary panel 457->517 at 7.3 s, 0.186), /checkout 0.415 / 0.415 (the form replaces the loading line), /orders 0.1186 / 0.1186; 1440: 0.109, 0.311, 0.122. Same pages with only `main#main{min-block-size:100dvh}` injected: 360: 0.0846, 0, 0.0064; 1440: 0.0066, 0, 0.0008. Without the mock (API refused) /orders alone measures 0.1186 at 360 and 0.100 at 1440 (runs/mob, runs/desk; screenshot runs/mob/orders.png).
IMPACT: Every buyer with a cart sees the footer and the summary jump while the page fills in, on the three pages of the money path; CLS on the cart and the checkout is in the "poor" band (> 0.25) on phones and on desktop, against the plan goal CLS < 0.1.
FIX: Give the island pages a shell that reaches below the first screen: a modifier on `.page` with `min-block-size: 100dvh` (or calc(100dvh - var(--header-h))) applied in the six island pages (cart, checkout, checkout/return, orders, notify/confirm, notify/unsubscribe); `.page` itself is shared with the policies, the store grid and the product page. Then fix the cart's own late re-layout (PERF-17).

### PERF-05 [medium] The public layout preloads two 80 KB fonts on every page: on a phone they take the bandwidth of the hero picture (LCP 1.4-1.7 s later on /, /journal and /book), while the face every page sets its text in is not among them
src/app/(public)/layout.tsx:34 fix:trivial conf:medium prior:lead:PERF-06 (same defect; its medians / 3440->2444, /journal 3624->2448, /book 4188->2816, text pages unchanged).
EVIDENCE: layout.tsx:34-41 preloads thmanyahserifdisplay-Medium.woff2 (82,552 B) and thmanyahsans-Bold.woff2 (79,160 B) on every public page (the export carries each tag twice; Chrome makes one request). At 360/Slow 4G they start at 0.67-0.95 s and end at 2.7-3.3 s (runs/mob), sharing the link with the LCP image. A/B with only the font preload tags removed from the HTML, both arms served through the same route (scratch ab.mjs, 3 runs each, loaded machine): / LCP 7332/6688/6228 -> 5260/6400/4320 ms; /journal 5316/2392/5068 -> 3380/3428/3612; /book 9684/8128/7288 -> 6396/7224/6192; the text-LCP /policies/store went 2492/2900/3272 -> 3688/3644/3364. The comment at layout.tsx:33 ("The two faces every page's first screen is set in") is wrong: document.fonts shows Thmanyah Sans 400, which is not preloaded, on all 22 public pages measured, and on /admin (body text, globals.css:141-146); it starts at the first layout (1.67-2.4 s, 4.8-5.3 s on /orders and /notify/*) and lands at 3.6-7.8 s.
IMPACT: On a phone the home page, the journal, the book page and the image-led rooms show their main picture well over a second later than they could; the preload list rests on a false premise, so tuning it from the comment makes it worse.
FIX: Remove the two font preloads from the shared layout (the @font-face rules still find the faces, font-display: swap keeps text visible); if a text-led page (a policy, the cart) proves faster with its title face preloaded, preload it in that page only (ReactDOM.preload) and re-measure at 360 on Slow 4G. Correct the comment.

### PERF-13 [medium] The room tagline enters by fading in from opacity 0, so when it is the largest text of the first screen (/started on a phone) the LCP is recorded only after the entrance and the hydration task end: 1.1 to 2.3 s late
src/components/public/RoomHero.tsx:38 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: RoomHero.tsx:38 `<p className={`t-lead ${styles.tagline} ...`} {...enter(360)}>`: enter() without fx gives `data-enter` only, so motion.css:19-21 runs `motion-rise` (from `opacity: 0; transform: translateY(32px)`, motion.css:57-61) for 850 ms after a 360 ms delay. At 360x800, DPR 2, CPU 4x, Slow 4G the LCP element of /started is this paragraph (runs/mob/started.json: `p.t-lead.room-hero-module__SC32Ua__tagline`). LCP via the CDP timeline (scratch modes.mjs): default 6755/6344 ms, reduced motion 4048/4168 ms, JavaScript off 3775/3740 ms. A/B with only the opacity entrances disabled (`[data-enter]:not([data-fx]){animation:none}`, HTML served through the same route in both arms, ab.mjs): LCP 4520/4312/4208 -> 3440/3260/2796 ms, i.e. LCP = FCP. The room titles enter with the clip-path wipe (RoomHero.tsx:34 `enter(80, 'band')`) and are reported at FCP (/passed: h1 at 3888 ms = FCP).
IMPACT: The rooms' lab LCP on phones, which P10 must bring under 2.5 s, carries an extra second or two that no network work can remove, and after launch the field LCP of /started is reported late for the same reason.
FIX: Give the tagline an entrance that keeps it painted, e.g. enter(360, 'band') (the clip-path wipe the title already uses) or a transform-only rise, in RoomHero.tsx:38; keep opacity entrances for elements that cannot be the largest paint of the first screen.

### PERF-16 [medium] The room heroes move when Thmanyah replaces the fallback face: /passed reaches CLS 0.111 on a phone (over the 0.1 goal), /contact 0.060, /started 0.036
src/styles/tokens.css:38 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:PERF-07 (same defect; same numbers).
EVIDENCE: tokens.css:38-39 `--font-display: 'Thmanyah Serif Display', serif; --font-body: 'Thmanyah Sans', system-ui, sans-serif;` and globals.css:5-34 declare the faces with `font-display: swap` only; no fallback face carries size-adjust/ascent-override, so the poster-size title (.t-title, clamp(68px, 12.4vw, 200px)) and the lead re-wrap when the real face lands. Measured at 360, 4x CPU, Slow 4G in a mouse context (scratch clsmock.mjs, 3 runs each, identical every run): /passed 0.1114 (the band under the hero moves 352->437 at 4.4 s, 0.084, then 437->482 at 8.2 s, 0.0274), /contact 0.0598 (title band 382->442), /started 0.0362 (article 397->441). With touch emulation Chrome flagged the first /passed shift hadRecentInput although no input was sent (runs/mob/passed.json), which hides it from lab CLS; a visitor gets no such pass. At 1440 unthrottled the fonts land before first paint and CLS is 0 (runs/desk).
IMPACT: On slow phones the first screen of the rooms jumps by up to 85 px seconds after it appeared, while the visitor starts reading; /passed misses the plan goal CLS < 0.1.
FIX: Add metric-matched fallbacks: an @font-face per family (local() of the platform Arabic fonts, e.g. Noto Naskh Arabic, Geeza Pro, Segoe UI) with size-adjust/ascent-override/descent-override tuned to Thmanyah, listed second in --font-display and --font-body; or reserve the hero title's and lead's block size. Re-measure at 360 on Slow 4G.

### PERF-18 [medium] Once the book is open, page-flip redraws every frame for as long as it stays open, idle or scrolled out of sight: 60 style recalculations a second, half or more of a phone-class CPU
src/components/book/PdfBookReader.tsx:289 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 S15.2 fixed the loop surviving destroy() (still holds: 0 rAF after the switch); the loop while the book is open was left. The earlier attempt of this slice listed the reader's idle CPU as pending (prev/PERF reader-idle.mjs, no finding).
EVIDENCE: PdfBookReader.tsx:289 creates `new PageFlip(block, {...})` and tears it down only in the effect cleanup (:449 `flip.destroy()`, when the reader unmounts or switches to «عرض للقراءة»). page-flip's render loop (node_modules/page-flip/dist/js/page-flip.browser.js as patched by patches/page-flip@2.0.7.patch) is `start(){this.update();const t=e=>{this.stopped||(this.render(e),requestAnimationFrame(t))};requestAnimationFrame(t)}`, and the HTML render's drawFrame() rewrites the leaves' styles every frame whether anything moves or not. Measured on the export with CDP Performance metrics over 5 s windows (scratch readeridle.mjs, rAF counted by a wrapper): closed book 0 rAF, task 0.004-0.009 s; open and idle in view 300-302 rAF, 301 style recalcs, task 0.29-0.78 s (1440 and 360); scrolled to the bottom of /book with the book out of view, the same 301 recalcs and 0.38-0.66 s; the same at CPU 4x: task 2.57-4.96 s per 5 s (the frame rate falls to 138-178 a 5 s window because each frame overruns). After switching to «عرض للقراءة» (flip destroyed): 0 rAF, task 0.001 s.
IMPACT: A phone visitor who opens the book and then keeps reading /book (the photos, the journey, the editions) carries a main thread busy every frame: scrolling and taps answer late (INP) and the battery drains for as long as the page stays open.
FIX: Pause the loop when nothing can change: extend the existing page-flip patch with stop()/resume() (the `stopped` flag is already there) and drive it from an IntersectionObserver on the stage in PdfBookReader (resume when visible), and in the loop call render() only while an animation, a drag or a pending update is active; re-measure with the 5 s idle windows above.

### QUALITY-04 [medium] Every handled 5xx in the Edge Functions leaves no trace: 51 fail(500) paths log nothing, and the requestId each reply carries is recorded nowhere
supabase/functions/_shared/http.ts:28 fix:small conf:high prior:EF-MONEY-02 and EF-MONEY-08 (wave A) covered two specific silent paths (refused webhook, refused creation); the general pattern and the dead requestId were not reported
EVIDENCE: http.ts:28 `{ ok: false, error: {...}, requestId: crypto.randomUUID() }` is the only place requestId exists: `grep -rn requestId supabase/functions src` finds nothing else (no log, no client display). `grep -rn "fail(500\|failWith(500" supabase/functions` = 51 sites (admin 9, staff-admin 8, checkout 5, download 5, notify 4, orders 4, paid-files 4, refunds 4, contact 3, payments 3, disputes 2). Each is either a `catch` that drops the error or an unrecognised SQL reply (an unmapped refusal code or a failed shape check), e.g. checkout.ts:279-283 `catch (error) { if (...code === '54000') ...; return fail(500, 'FAILED', 'تعذّر إكمال الإجراء.') }` and orders.ts:182 `mapped ? fail(mapped.status, ...) : fail(500, 'FAILED', FAILED)`. The only console calls in the functions are 5 warns (payments webhook refusal, turnstile x2, resend-webhook x2). Supabase's invocation log (function_edge_logs) holds request/response headers and status_code only, no body (supabase.com/docs/guides/observability/log-field-reference), and only console output and uncaught exceptions reach function_logs (supabase.com/docs/guides/functions/logging). Counts re-checked at submission.
IMPACT: When a SQL function, a grant or a migration goes wrong after launch, checkout, the order page, downloads and the owner's money actions answer «تعذّر إكمال الإجراء.» and the operator sees only a 500 status in the dashboard: no SQLSTATE, no function name, no way to match a buyer's report to an invocation. The requestId suggests a correlation that does not exist. P10's planned Sentry (D23) would see nothing either, because no error is rethrown.
FIX: In http.ts fail(), when status >= 500, `console.error(JSON.stringify({ requestId, status, code }))`; and in the shared rpc failure helpers (each file's `call`/`sqlFail`/`sqlFailure` and db.ts serviceRpc) log the SQLSTATE and the function name only (never arguments, bodies, tokens or addresses), so the reply's requestId ties to one log line with the cause.

### READER-02 [medium] While the book is open, page-flip's never-idle 60fps loop rewrites every leaf's inline style each frame: a 4x-throttled phone's main thread stays ~85-90% busy, even scrolled away from the reader
patches/page-flip@2.0.7.patch:7 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:READER-03 (same measurement, independently re-run: 4.585 s vs the lead's 2.796 s per 5 s under 4x throttle); AUDIT-1 S15.2 fixed the loop after destroy() only
EVIDENCE: The patched loop still renders every frame while mounted: `start(){this.update();const t=e=>{this.stopped||(this.render(e),requestAnimationFrame(t))};...}`; render() calls drawFrame(), whose clear()/simpleDraw() write `style.cssText` on every leaf (node_modules/page-flip/src/Render/HTMLRender.ts drawFrame/clear, Page/HTMLPage.ts simpleDraw). Measured on the export (scratch t1.mjs, t2.mjs, t3.mjs): at 1440 with the book open and untouched, 61 rAF callbacks/s and 708 style-attribute mutations/s on .stf__item leaves; CDP Performance.getMetrics over 5 s idle: closed book TaskDuration 0.005 s; open book at 390x844 DPR3 with 4x CPU throttle TaskDuration 4.585 s (RecalcStyleCount 241), and 4.431 s after scrolling to #editions; at 1440 unthrottled 0.42 s per 5 s. Blocking only the rAF calls whose stack is in page-flip's chunk (3y7oy_tt71u1t.js) drops the same 5 s window to 0.003 s.
IMPACT: Every visitor who opens the book on a phone pays a continuous main-thread load (battery, heat, janky scrolling of the photos and editions below) for as long as /book stays open in book view, whether or not they are reading or even looking at the book; only leaving the page or switching to «عرض للقراءة» stops it.
FIX: Extend patches/page-flip@2.0.7.patch so the loop keeps scheduling frames but calls this.render(e) only while an animation runs, the flip state is not 'read', or a dirty flag is up (set in setLeftPage/setRightPage/setBottomPage/setFlippingPage/update/setShadowData/clearShadow, cleared after drawFrame). Add one e2e assertion that the open, idle book makes no style mutations on .stf__item over 1 s.

### READER-06 [medium] On touch screens a press on the book held 250 ms or more ends in page-flip's own turn: scrolling the page with a finger that starts on the open book turns a page, and a slow tap turns two
src/components/book/PdfBookReader.tsx:307 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:lead:READER-02 (same root cause and symptoms, independently re-measured); P02 WORK-PACKAGES proof lists touch, but tests/e2e/reader.spec.ts only taps the «التالية» button and drags with page.mouse
EVIDENCE: The reader leaves touch to page-flip (`mobileScrollSupport: true`, PdfBookReader.tsx:307; its own press handling is mouse-only, lines 395-415). page-flip (node_modules/page-flip/src/UI/UI.ts) arms a press 250 ms after touchstart (`setTimeout(() => { if (this.touchPoint !== null) this.app.startUserTouch(pos) }, this.swipeTimeout)`), and at touchend calls `this.app.userStop(pos, isSwipe)`; userStop runs `flipController.flip(pos)` whenever the press is armed and nothing was folded. A vertical scroll never folds (userMove needs |dx| > 10), so its release turns the page, the direction coming from page-flip's split at the lift point (portrait: left 40% of the page = BACK = the reader's next). Emulated on the export with CDP Input.dispatchTouchEvent at 360x780 DPR3 (t27.mjs): a 450 ms upward scroll starting on the left quarter of «المقدمة 2 / 5» scrolled the page 235 px AND turned to «3 / 5»; the same scroll downward on the right quarter scrolled -235 px and turned to «1 / 5»; a shorter scroll also turned. A press held past 250 ms gets page-flip's turn at touchend plus the reader's own turn from the compatibility mouseup, queued behind it (t13.mjs, 600 ms press in emulation: 2 -> 3 -> 4 at 20% of the page; at 45% page-flip turns back and the reader turns forward again). page-flip's own `touch-action: pan-y` on .stf__parent (computed, t31.mjs) does not help, since vertical scrolling is allowed and the turn comes from the release, not from the moves.
IMPACT: On a phone the open book fills about half the screen, so a visitor who scrolls /book with a finger that starts on it (to reach the toolbar, the photos or the editions) turns a page with most deliberate scrolls, and a slightly slow tap skips a page; reading the approved pages on the most common device becomes erratic.
FIX: Take the release away from page-flip unless it is really folding: in a capture-phase touchend listener (like the reader's mouseup one) call `flip.userStop({ x: 0, y: 0 }, true)` when page-flip's state is not 'user_fold' (and the touch was not a swipe), so scrolls and taps never reach flipController.flip; taps keep turning through the reader's halves via the compatibility mouse events. Confirm on a real Android Chrome and iOS Safari.
PROOF: On a real phone (Android Chrome, then iPhone Safari), portrait: open /book, «افتح الكتاب», pick «المقدمة» in «انتقل إلى». (1) Put a finger on the left part of the page and scroll the page up slowly (about half a second), lift: wrong result is the label changing to «3 / 5» while the page scrolled (from the right part: «1 / 5»). (2) Tap the left part of the page holding about 0.3-0.4 s: wrong result is two turns («3 / 5» then «4 / 5»).

### READER-10 [medium] Two pages at a time, screen readers read each spread left page first: the leaves are in page-flip's reversed DOM order
src/components/book/PdfBookReader.tsx:310 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:READER-01
EVIDENCE: `flip.loadFromHTML([...elements].reverse())` puts the leaves in the DOM back to front; each page leaf carries its spoken text (pdf.ts:138-146) and the pdf.js layer is aria-hidden, so the accessibility order is the DOM order. t26.mjs at 1440 on spread «3–4 / 5»: DOM order `back endpaper blank end page5 page4* page3* page2 blank page1 endpaper cover` (* = shown), page 3 drawn at x=720 (right), page 4 at x=158 (left); the group's ariaSnapshot begins «صورة الروضة وبين الذاكرة والصورة والمرة الأخيرة من كل شعور…» (page 4) before page 3's «وكان لكل بيت رائحة…». The [5 | end] spread likewise reads the closing leaf («هنا تنتهي الصفحات المتاحة للقراءة…») before page 5.
IMPACT: A screen-reader visitor reading the open book in browse mode on any screen wide enough for two pages hears half the spreads out of order: the start of «صورة الروضة» before the end of «المقدمة», and the end-of-preview message before the last page (WCAG 1.3.2).
FIX: Give the AT a reading order independent of page-flip's DOM: set ids on the leaves and `aria-owns` on the book host listing them in reading order (0..n-1), or keep the spoken text in one visually hidden region outside page-flip that shows the visible leaves' text in reading order.

### READER-12 [medium] In full screen, «عرض للقراءة» centres a column taller than the screen, so the tools and the first pages sit above the scroll origin and cannot be reached
src/components/book/reader.module.css:522 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:READER-07
EVIDENCE: `.reader:fullscreen { justify-content: center; ... overflow-y: auto; }` on the flex column `.reader`; in the reading view the column (tools, five sheets, closing) is far taller than the screen, and centred overflow spills past the start edge, where a scroll container cannot scroll. t26.mjs at 1440x900: full screen, then «عرض للقراءة»: scrollTop 0, scrollHeight 3547, page 1 top at -2563 px, the «عرض الكتاب» / «الخروج من ملء الشاشة» row at -2631 px, `scrollTo(0, 0)` changes nothing; focus is on the off-screen «عرض الكتاب». Screenshot t26-fs-reading.png opens on the lower half of page 3.
IMPACT: A reader who chooses full screen and then the reading view cannot see the dedication, the first introduction page or the top of page 3, nor the buttons to go back or leave full screen; only Esc gets them out.
FIX: Use `justify-content: safe center` in `.reader:fullscreen` (or centre only in book mode, e.g. `.reader:fullscreen[data-mode='book']`).

### SOON-01 [medium] After the launch, «اعرض صفحة قريباً» answers every page 200 with X-Robots-Tag: noindex, so one press removes the launched site from Google
PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md:18 OWNER fix:small conf:high prior:lead:SOON-01
EVIDENCE: Contract line 18: `| «قريباً» | `soon` | a soon design, 200 |`; line 28 offers «اعرض صفحة قريباً» whenever soon is not the current state, so also after «افتح الموقع». The soon answer is worker.js line 32 `new Response(page.body, { status: maintenance ? 503 : 200, headers: page.headers })`, whose headers come from artifacts/soon/site/_headers `/*` (line 12 `X-Robots-Tag: noindex`); the contract does not change that file. Live 2026-10-07: `curl -sS -D - -o /dev/null https://anas.studio/no-such-page` -> `HTTP/1.1 200 OK` ... `x-robots-tag: noindex`. Google, https://developers.google.com/search/docs/crawling-indexing/pause-online-business : "Don't block the website by returning 403, 404, 410 HTTP status codes, or with a noindex robots meta tag or x-robots-tag HTTP header. This will remove the website's URLs from Google Search."
IMPACT: Once the site is launched and indexed, the owner pressing «اعرض صفحة قريباً» (for example to hide the shop for a few days instead of using «صيانة») makes every gated URL (/, /store/*, /book, /journal/*) answer 200 + noindex with the same soon page; each URL Googlebot recrawls in that window is dropped from Google Search and comes back only after a recrawl once the site is open again (days to weeks). The admin text the contract approves («قريباً: يرى الزائر صفحة «قريباً» بدل الموقع.») gives no hint of this.
FIX: Keep the three states but change the soon answer after the first opening: record that the site has been opened (a launched_at column set by the first `open`, or a gate variable P11 sets) and from then on answer soon like maintenance (503 + Retry-After, no X-Robots-Tag: noindex), or hide «اعرض صفحة قريباً» after the launch. Which of the two is the owner's call (D47 defines the three-state control); either way say it in the admin sentence.

### SOON-02 [medium] The launch sequences never set the gate's fallback MODE to open, so after «افتح الموقع» a failed state read puts «قريباً» (200, noindex) over the launched site
PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md:49 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:SOON-03
EVIDENCE: Contract line 43: 'with no answer yet it uses `MODE`, which is `soon` before the launch and `open` from the launch (`worker.js` and `deploy.cjs` accept `open`; P11 sets it)'. The three sequences an operator follows stop without that step: contract line 49 ('...then the owner opens the site with «افتح الموقع». The Worker `anas-studio-soon` is deleted once nothing routes to it'), artifacts/soon/README.md line 63 ('At launch (P11)') and PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md line 143; `grep -c MODE PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md` -> 0. artifacts/soon/wrangler.toml line 21 ships `MODE = "soon"`, and a deploy without --mode publishes it (README line 28); deploy.cjs line 17 accepts only soon|maintenance today.
IMPACT: If the launch follows the written sequence, the gate keeps MODE = soon. Every isolate whose first read of site_state fails (a Supabase incident, a response slower than the 1.5 s timeout, a wrong key after a restore) answers every gated page with a soon design, 200 and noindex, for at least 30 seconds and for as long as the reads keep failing; Googlebot visits in that window drop pages (see SOON-01). The static site would otherwise stay readable through a Supabase outage.
FIX: Add the step to all three sequences (contract line 49, README 'At launch', WORK-PACKAGES P11), after «افتح الموقع» and the domain check and never before it: redeploy the gate with MODE = "open" and commit that value to wrangler.toml; P11's hosted acceptance checks the failed-read fallback after this step.

### SOON-03 [medium] `open` is defined only as 'pass the request through to the site', but until P11 the state-reading Worker is itself the domain's origin, and the hosted acceptance switches the live domain to `open` before the launch
PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md:43 fix:small conf:medium prior:none
EVIDENCE: Line 43 makes SITE-STATE-1 change `artifacts/soon/worker.js` itself to read the stored state and says '`open` passes the request through to the site'; line 51: 'Until the hosted Supabase project has this migration, the domain keeps following `MODE`' (so the Worker is expected to read the hosted row before P11 moves the domain). artifacts/soon/wrangler.toml lines 24-32: the Worker is attached to anas.studio and www as Custom Domains. Cloudflare (https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/configuration/routing/custom-domains/): 'A Worker running on a Custom Domain is treated as an origin' and 'Custom Domains can also be invoked within the same zone via fetch()'; https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/observability/errors/ : 'A Worker cannot call itself or another Worker more than 16 times... When the count reaches zero, error code 1019 is returned.' Line 69 (acceptance): 'the three states switched from the admin and seen on the domain within half a minute', on the one hosted Supabase project (DECISIONS line 85), while P11 makes «افتح الموقع» the last launch step (line 49).
IMPACT: Between SITE-STATE-1 and P11's domain move, a stored `open` (pressed in the hosted admin, or switched by the acceptance itself) makes the custom-domain Worker fetch its own hostname: per the vendor docs that re-enters the same Worker up to 16 times (16 of the account's 100,000 daily requests per visit) and ends in error 1019 on anas.studio instead of any page. After the move, the same acceptance step opens the unlaunched site to the public. The contract gives the implementer no rule for either case.
FIX: Write in the contract that `open` is passed through only when the Worker runs on a route in front of the Pages project (a variable P11 sets, for example GATE_ORIGIN=pages) and is treated as `soon` otherwise; and run the acceptance's state switches against a separate test hostname with its own row (or define the `open` switch as the launch itself).

### SOON-04 [medium] Maintenance is the state for 'a migration, a restore', yet it leaves checkout and Moyasar payments running, and no runbook closes «الشراء» first
PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md:64 fix:trivial conf:medium prior:related: OPS-PRIVACY-19 (orders paid since the backup), ARCH-09 and VENDOR-PLAT-06 (repointing after a restore)
EVIDENCE: Contract line 64: 'This switch does not touch orders, holds or payments; the Edge Functions keep answering in every state.' D46 (PLANS/DECISIONS.md line 53): MAINT-1 is 'shown when the site is taken down on purpose (a deploy that needs it, a migration, a restore)'. The gate only hides pages: the payment callback is the Supabase function (supabase/functions/_shared/payments.ts line 211 `callback_url: ${deps.config.callbackBase}/payments/callback`, callbackBase = FUNCTIONS_PUBLIC_URL in payments/moyasar.ts line 80), the return page /checkout/return is exempt (line 43), and a cart or checkout tab opened before the switch keeps calling the checkout function. docs/operations.md 'A real restore into a new hosted project' (lines 1332-1410) has no step that closes «الشراء» or waits for open holds and unpaid invoices; docs/payments-runbook.md never mentions a restore.
IMPACT: A restore done under «صيانة» while the old project still answers (a corrupted or badly migrated database rather than a lost one): a buyer whose checkout tab was already open pays on Moyasar's page, the old project records the paid order and its webhook, and the restored project never has it. The buyer is charged and gets no order page, receipt or file from the new project; OPS-PRIVACY-19 is about finding such orders afterwards, not about not taking them.
FIX: One rule in the contract and one step in the restore and migration runbooks: before a restore or a migration, close «الشراء» on the project being replaced, wait until no hold or unpaid invoice is open (the checkout-expire job), then switch to «صيانة»; reopen «الشراء» only after the restored project is verified.

### STORE-UI-01 [medium] The store UI still sells a digital variant in quantities of 1 to 20, which the quote now refuses with a sentence that says 1 to 20 is fine
src/components/store/AddToCart.tsx:24 fix:small conf:high prior:FABLE-AUDIT wave A DB-COMMERCE-01, planned as F2b-8/F2b-12 (not applied in today's tree); lead:STORE-UI-10
EVIDENCE: M1b (supabase/migrations/20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:1079-1084, today's working tree) makes finance.checkout_price refuse `if v_row.fulfillment = 'digital' and v_qty <> 1` with INVALID_QUANTITY and drop the line. The UI was not changed: AddToCart.toQuantity clamps every variant to 1..MAX_QUANTITY (`Math.min(MAX_QUANTITY, Math.max(1, next))`, line 24; input max={MAX_QUANTITY} line 63) and addLine merges a second press into quantity 2; CartView's + button is disabled only at `line.quantity >= MAX_QUANTITY` (CartView.tsx:271); quote.ts:364-365 renders INVALID_QUANTITY as «الكمية يجب أن تكون بين 1 و20.». Rendered with the quote mocked to the new rule (p1-digital.mjs, p1.out.txt): two presses of «أضف إلى السلة» on «خلفيات من الغرف» give «أُضيف إلى السلة. في السلة الآن 2.», then the cart reads «منتج في السلة / … / الكمية يجب أن تكون بين 1 و20.» beside a quantity of 2, totals 0.00 ر.س and no checkout link (shots/p1-cart-digital-qty2-360.png). tests/e2e/cart-checkout.spec.ts:413-422 still adds «النسخة الإلكترونية» with quantity 2 and expects «24.80 ر.س», so that e2e test now fails against today's SQL (M1b was accepted on integration tests only, FABLE-AUDIT/STATE.md).
IMPACT: A buyer who presses «أضف إلى السلة» twice on the e-book or the wallpapers (or types 2) gets a cart line reading «منتج في السلة» with price «…», the error «الكمية يجب أن تكون بين 1 و20.» beside a quantity of 2, totals of 0.00 ر.س and no checkout link; the offered «إزالة غير المتاح» removes the product he wants. The after-battery's e2e run fails on the digital coupon test.
FIX: The planned F2b-8/F2b-12 (brief-F2b.md) covers AddToCart, addLine and the e2e spec; three things it does not: (1) a refused line has no quote line (the SQL `continue`s before adding it), so CartView cannot learn from the quote that a refused line is digital to hide or cap its control: let the INVALID_QUANTITY error carry `maximum: 1` (or the fulfilment) and have the cart offer «اجعل الكمية 1» for it; (2) quote.ts:364-365 must not say «بين 1 و20» when the line's maximum is 1 (e.g. «نسخة رقمية واحدة لكل طلب.»); (3) the brief's sentence «الكتاب الرقمي في سلتك.» is wrong for the demo's other digital product (wallpapers, «ملف رقمي»): use a product-neutral one such as «هذا المنتج في سلتك؛ النسخة الرقمية واحدة لكل طلب.». Pass the variant's fulfilment from the product page to VariantAction/AddToCart.

### STORE-UI-04 [medium] The hold view pays the held order without showing what it holds: a cart edited after `create`, or an edited retry after a lost reply, is paid as the old order, and `paid` then empties the whole cart
src/components/store/HoldView.tsx:206 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-02 (extended: the paid path also empties items never ordered)
EVIDENCE: Nothing ties the pending order to what the buyer last confirmed. (1) Cart edited after create: CheckoutForm keeps only `{orderNumber, accessToken}` (writePendingOrder, line 381) and, while one is stored, renders HoldView whatever the cart is (line 456); HoldView shows the number, the total and «ادفع الآن» but not the order's lines (lines 206-244), though OrderSummary carries them; PaymentReturn.spend then does `if (paid) writeCart(EMPTY_CART)` (PaymentReturn.tsx:61) for whatever the cart holds. Rendered (p5-pending.mjs, p5.out.txt): cart = paper + cup, «الإجمالي 139.00 ر.س»; /checkout shows «رقم الطلب: K7M2P9QX / الإجمالي: 94.00 ر.س / محجوز حتى 14:54 / ادفع الآن» (shots/p5-hold-old-order-360.png); after verify answers paid, `anasaq:cart:v1` is `{"version":1,"lines":[]}`: the cup, never ordered, is gone. (2) Edited retry after a lost reply: a changed field mints a new key (CheckoutForm.tsx:348-354), the SQL answers ACTIVE_HOLD and hands the session's held order back when the email matches (20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:486-497), and the form adopts it without a word (`if (mine) { writePendingOrder(mine); setStart(null); ... setPending(mine) }`, lines 425-433); rendered in p8.out.txt (hold-mine) as the plain hold view.
IMPACT: A buyer who goes back to the cart while his order is held and adds or changes items, or who corrects his address after a lost reply, is shown the earlier order's hold view and pays it; the page says «تم الدفع», the cart is emptied, and he believes he bought what he last confirmed while the order (and its delivery address) is the old one: wrong goods or a wrong address shipped, items silently dropped from the cart, support contacts.
FIX: Show the order's own lines in the hold view (and, for a physical order, that it ships to the address given when it was made). Store a digest of the ordered lines with the pending order; when the current cart differs, or ACTIVE_HOLD handed back an order for a request other than the one just sent, say «هذا الطلب أُنشئ قبل تعديلك؛ ادفعه كما هو أو ألغه لتطلب من جديد.». In spend(), empty the cart only when it still matches the ordered lines.

### A11Y-RTL-05 [low] The hold's end, «محجوز حتى 15:09», is Riyadh clock time with no zone named, so a buyer on any other clock misreads how long the order is held
src/lib/cart.ts:420 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Asia/Riyadh', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23' })` feeds HoldView.tsx:241 «محجوز حتى <span dir="ltr">…</span>» and CheckoutForm.tsx:779 «لديك طلب محجوز من هذه الجلسة حتى …». Nothing says «بتوقيت الرياض», and no duration is given (HoldView.tsx:147 deliberately shows no countdown). flows.mjs with the browser in Asia/Dubai: the device clock reads 15:49:03 while a fresh 20-minute hold reads «محجوز حتى 15:09».
IMPACT: A buyer in the UAE or Oman (UTC+4) reads a fresh hold as already over by 40 minutes and may abandon or reorder. A buyer in Egypt or Europe reads one to three extra hours and lets the 20-minute hold lapse while paying on Moyasar's page. WCAG 2.2.1 accepts a stock hold as an essential time limit (see doc claim), but only if the buyer can tell when it ends.
FIX: Format the time in the buyer's own zone (drop `timeZone`, keep `hourCycle: 'h23'` and Latin digits), or keep Riyadh time and add «بتوقيت الرياض». The device-zone version needs no new words.

### A11Y-RTL-06 [low] Without JavaScript the wrapped header stays sticky at 200px on a 320px phone, more than a third of a 568px screen on every page
src/components/site/site.module.css:99 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `@media (scripting: none) { .bar { flex-wrap: wrap } .inline { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap } }` shows all eight room links inline, but `.header` keeps `position: sticky` (line 3), and the hide-on-scroll lives in SiteHeader.tsx's script. Measured on /started at 320x568 with javaScriptEnabled:false: the header is 200px tall and sticky, and after a 900px scroll it still covers y=0..200 (shots/nojs-header-320-scrolled.png). With script the header is 68px and slides away on scroll.
IMPACT: A visitor without script (or at 400% zoom with script blocked) reads every page through a 368px slot under a 200px fixed block, and a control reached with Shift+Tab lands under it (the A11Y-RTL-02 mechanism, with a header three times as tall).
FIX: Inside the existing `@media (scripting: none)` block add `.header { position: relative; }` so the wrapped header scrolls away with the page.

### A11Y-RTL-07 [low] Pressing the reader's «التالية» onto the last page (or «السابقة» onto the first) disables the focused button and drops focus to <body> for good
src/components/book/PdfBookReader.tsx:643 fix:small conf:high
