EVIDENCE: Line 643 is `<button … onClick={() => turn(1)} disabled={lastShown === total - 1}>`; «السابقة» at line 632 has `disabled={(shown[0] ?? 0) === 0}`. reader.mjs on /book, with pdf.js and page-flip loaded from the export: focus «التالية» and press Enter until it disables (6 presses at 1440, 7 at 360). activeElement is then BODY while the live region reads «نهاية الصفحات المتاحة»; the same with motion on. Unlike A11Y-RTL-04, nothing ever restores focus, because the button stays disabled. Opening the book shows a transient form of the same thing: «افتح الكتاب» is disabled while loading (ClosedBook.tsx:42), and activeElement is BODY until the reader focuses the book.
IMPACT: A keyboard or screen-reader user paging through the preview with the buttons loses focus at the last page and has no visible position. The next Tab continues from the button's old place, and a screen reader may restart at the top. AUDIT-1 S15.3 and AUDIT-2 FIX-public-8 fixed two other focus drops in this reader.
FIX: Keep both buttons focusable at the ends with `aria-disabled` and an early return in the click handler (turn() already ignores a move past the ends), or move focus to the book itself when a button becomes disabled.

### A11Y-RTL-08 [low] The one film that keeps its soundtrack (the Raha drive-thru reel, whose slogan is spoken) has no captions track
src/components/weave/VideoTile.tsx:97 OWNER fix:small conf:medium
EVIDENCE: VideoTile renders only `<source src=… type="video/mp4">`, never a <track>. In public/media/manifest.json, raha-roaster-drivethru-slogan is the only one of 18 films with audioKept=true. ffprobe: h264 plus AAC stereo, 35.7 s. ffmpeg silencedetect (-35 dB, 0.6 s) finds no silence and a mean of -17.6 dB, so there is sound through the whole clip. scripts/prepare-media.mjs:166 keeps the audio because 'the slogan «اتسعت الدار وحيّ الله الجار» is spoken in it'. On /built at 1440, after «تشغيل», the focused <video> has textTracks.length 0; the only text is the figcaption «اتسعت الدار وحيّ الله الجار».
IMPACT: Fails WCAG 1.2.2 (Captions, Prerecorded, level A): a deaf or hard-of-hearing visitor who unmutes the film gets no synchronized text for the speech or the music. The figcaption gives the slogan's words, but not when they are said or what else is said.
FIX: Give VideoTile an optional captions file per manifest entry (`<track kind="captions" srcLang="ar" label="العربية" src=…vtt default>`) and add a WebVTT file with the clip's words. Alternatively, drop the audio track as for the other 17 films if the figcaption is meant to carry the slogan.

### A11Y-RTL-09 [low] The cart's «−» at quantity 1 and «+» at 20 disable themselves while focused, and focus falls to <body>
src/components/store/CartView.tsx:256 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-05
EVIDENCE: `disabled={line.quantity <= 1}` (line 256) and `disabled={line.quantity >= MAX_QUANTITY}` (line 271). leadcheck.mjs with a mocked quote, on a cart with quantities 2 and 19 at 360x720: Enter on «إنقاص الكمية: خوص (تجريبي): نسخة إلكترونية» brings the quantity to 1 and leaves activeElement on BODY; Enter on «زيادة الكمية: خوص (تجريبي): نسخة موقعة» brings it to 20 and leaves BODY; the next Tab lands on «حذف …» of the second line.
IMPACT: A keyboard buyer stepping a quantity down to 1, the most common correction, loses focus and its indicator, and a screen reader is left on the page body without being told the limit was reached.
FIX: Keep the steppers focusable at their limits with `aria-disabled` and an early return (setQuantity already clamps), or move focus to the line's quantity field when a stepper reaches its limit.

### A11Y-RTL-10 [low] The contact form's «أرسل» is disabled while the message is sent, so focus falls to <body> on every send, success or failure
src/components/public/contact/ContactForm.tsx:244 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:A11Y-RTL-06
EVIDENCE: Line 244: `<ActionButton type="submit" arrow disabled={!scripted || !turnstileAvailable || status.kind === 'sending'}>`. leadcheck.mjs with the contact function mocked to answer 503 after 1.2 s and Turnstile stubbed (nothing left the browser): after Enter on «أرسل», activeElement is BODY while sending and stays BODY after the reply. The always-mounted status reads «الخدمة غير متاحة الآن؛ حاول بعد قليل.».
IMPACT: After pressing «أرسل», a keyboard or screen-reader visitor has no focused element to continue from, and must find the button again to retry a failed send.
FIX: Use `aria-disabled={status.kind === 'sending' || undefined}` on the submit button (submit() already returns while sending), and keep `disabled` only for the not-scripted and no-Turnstile cases.

### A11Y-RTL-11 [low] Stepping through the scenes lightbox announces only «4 / 19»; the new photo is never named
src/components/public/scenes/SceneGallery.tsx:173 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: The dialog's only live region is `<span dir="ltr" aria-live="polite" className="t-label">{index + 1} / {shown.length}</span>`. The caption `<span className={styles.caption}>{current.caption}</span>` (line 201) and the new <img alt={current.caption}> (line 189, re-keyed for each photo) are not live, and focus stays on «إغلاق» or on the step button. dialogs.mjs at 1440 and 360: after ArrowLeft the live text changes from «3 / 19» to «4 / 19» while the image alt changes from «ذرى» to «تحميص القهوة في رحى», and nothing announces the alt.
IMPACT: A screen-reader visitor stepping through the gallery hears only a counter and has to leave the step control to find out what each photo shows, which is the whole content of المَشاهد.
FIX: Put the caption in the live text, e.g. `<span aria-live="polite"><span dir="ltr">{index + 1} / {shown.length}</span>: {current.caption}</span>`, or add a visually hidden polite region with the caption.

### A11Y-RTL-12 [low] Rich-text blocks lose their direction on the site: a Latin paragraph, heading or list item that the editor shows left to right is drawn right to left with its punctuation at the wrong end
src/lib/richtext.tsx:41 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: The renderer emits <p>, <h2>, <h3>, <blockquote> and <li> with no dir (richtext.tsx:40-65), so every block inherits the page's dir=rtl. The admin editor (Lexical 0.51.0, RichTextEditor.tsx:267) gives every top-level block dir="auto": $getReconciledDirection returns 'auto' for a root child whose __dir is null, and $setElementDirection writes it to dom.dir (node_modules/lexical/dist/Lexical.dev.js:5162-5190). So Anas sees an English line left to right while writing. Injected into the .policyBody of /policies/store: «Orders ship with SMSA Express (2-3 days).» renders as «.Orders ship with SMSA Express (2-3 days)», a heading as «?FAQ: what is included», and a list item as «.ISBN 978-603-01-1234-5». The same paragraph with dir="auto" renders correctly (shots/richtext-latin-paragraph.png).
IMPACT: Policies, product descriptions and journal posts that quote an English sentence, an ISBN or a Latin heading are published with the period or question mark at the wrong end, unlike the editor view Anas approved.
FIX: Give each rich-text block the editor's direction: set `dir="auto"` on p, h2, h3, blockquote and li in renderNode, or honour a stored `direction` of 'ltr' or 'rtl' when Lexical sets one.

### A11Y-RTL-13 [low] The header brand link shows «أنس anas.studio» but is named «أنس، الرئيسية», so the visible domain is missing from its accessible name (WCAG 2.5.3)
src/components/site/SiteHeader.tsx:19 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `<Link href="/" aria-label="أنس، الرئيسية" …><Mark /><span>أنس</span><span dir="ltr">anas.studio</span></Link>`: the aria-label replaces the visible text. The domain is visible at every width (crawl: brandDomain is 83px wide at 320; hdr-320.png) and also in the menu dialog's copy of the brand (line 139). Accessible name from CDP: «أنس، الرئيسية».
IMPACT: On every page, a speech-input user who says what they see («click anas.studio») gets no match. The name still starts with «أنس», so the impact is small.
FIX: Keep the visible words in the name, e.g. `aria-label="أنس anas.studio، الرئيسية"`, or drop the aria-label and add a visually hidden «، الرئيسية».

### A11Y-RTL-14 [low] The store settings report «تم فتح الشراء.» / «تم إغلاق الشراء.» in a status line that is inserted with its text already in it
src/components/admin/CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:275 RUNTIME fix:trivial conf:medium prior:AUDIT-1 X3.5 / S11.5 (the same pattern fixed in PublishBar and TableForm)
EVIDENCE: `{saved && (<p className={styles.message} role="status">{saved}</p>)}`: the live region and its words arrive in the same render. The project replaced this pattern elsewhere because screen readers often miss it (VideoTile.tsx:107 comment; AvailabilityForm.tsx:140, OrderPage.tsx:88 and StatsView.tsx:157-158 keep their regions mounted). The same form's controls are `disabled={busy}` (lines 192-257), so focus is not on a live control when the result arrives either. MediaUpload.tsx:417 (`{busy && (<p … role="status">`) follows the same pattern. The admin cannot be rendered without the stack, so this comes from reading the code only.
IMPACT: When the owner opens or closes the store with a screen reader (or saves the seller details, or approves the policies), the outcome of the most consequential switch in the admin may pass silently, and he has to explore the page to learn whether checkout is now open.
FIX: Keep the status and alert paragraphs mounted and empty, as StatsView does (`<p role="status" …>{saved}</p>` always rendered), and move focus to the status line after the action.
PROOF: On the local stack, with NVDA or VoiceOver running, sign in as the owner, open /admin/settings, press «افتح الشراء» and complete the step-up. Expected: «تم فتح الشراء.» is announced. Wrong result: nothing is spoken (Chrome's accessibility event log shows no live-region-changed event for the new paragraph).

### ADMIN-CMS-03 [low] «أرشفة» takes a live post or category off the site in one click with no confirmation, and is offered (claiming a site change) for documents that were never published
src/components/admin/PublishBar.tsx:102 fix:trivial conf:high prior:none in AUDIT-1, AUDIT-2 or FABLE waves A/B
EVIDENCE: `onClick={() => run('أرشفة', () => archiveDocument(collection, docId), true)}` (PublishBar.tsx:102) runs at once; the button renders whenever `canArchive` (CollectionForm.tsx:371: posts or taxonomies), whatever liveSeq is. archive_document deletes the live row, clears the schedule and calls site_build_request() even with no live row (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:204-237), and the bar then says «أرشفة: تم بنجاح. يظهر التعديل على الموقع خلال دقائق.» (PublishBar.tsx:63). The e2e clicks «أرشفة» and expects success directly (tests/e2e/cms.spec.ts:236-237). Afterwards the list shows the post as «مسودة لم تُنشر» (CollectionList.tsx:49), like a draft never published.
IMPACT: A misclick beside «نشر» removes a published article (or a category, its filter and every post's label) at the next build with no undo prompt, and the list no longer tells it apart from an unpublished draft. On a never-published draft the button claims a site change and spends a Pages build.
FIX: Show «أرشفة» only when liveSeq !== null and confirm first: `window.confirm('سيُحذف هذا المستند من الموقع حتى تنشره من جديد. هل تريد المتابعة؟')`, as TeamView does for a revoke.

### ADMIN-CMS-05 [low] AUDIT-1 S08.6 holds only for non-empty bodies: a click into an empty rich-text body still marks the document changed, turns «نشر» off and leaves a false «unsaved» copy
src/components/admin/RichTextEditor.tsx:249 RUNTIME fix:trivial conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 S08.6 (fixed for key order only)
EVIDENCE: An empty body is not handed to Lexical: `editorState: hasContent ? JSON.stringify(value) : undefined` (RichTextEditor.tsx:242-249). With undefined, LexicalComposer appends one paragraph in a history-merge update (node_modules/@lexical/react/dist/LexicalComposer.dev.js:138-155, Lexical 0.51.0). The first click is a selection update made with updateEditorSync and no tag (lexical/dist/Lexical.dev.js onSelectionChange, ~7280), and OnChangePlugin forwards it: `ignoreSelectionChange` defaults to false and `prevEditorState.isEmpty()` is now false (LexicalOnChangePlugin.dev.js). handleChange stores a root holding one empty paragraph, which equalData cannot match to the stored `{ root: { type: 'root', children: [] } }` (fields.ts:115-121, 180-181), so hasUnsavedChanges is true, canPublish false (CollectionForm.tsx:252-253) and the autosave writes a copy (205-218).
IMPACT: Anas opens a saved post or policy draft whose body is still empty, clicks into the body and leaves without typing: «نشر» and «جدولة» stay disabled until he saves an identical version, and on his next visit the form is hidden behind «يوجد تعديل غير محفوظ محليًا لهذا المستند.» for a change he never made.
FIX: Pass `ignoreSelectionChange` to OnChangePlugin, or treat a root whose only child is an empty paragraph as the empty root in equalData.
PROOF: Create a post, fill «المعرّف» and «العنوان» only, «حفظ» (expect «تم الحفظ.», «نشر» enabled). Click once in the element labelled «محرر النص المنسق», type nothing, click the page heading. Expect (wrong): «نشر» disabled and localStorage 'anasaq:draft:posts:<id>' non-null; after a reload the «يوجد تعديل غير محفوظ محليًا لهذا المستند.» heading shows.

### ADMIN-CMS-06 [low] The owner home's failed-schedule warning names a post by its raw uuid (a room by its English slug), gives no reason, and keeps showing for 7 days after the document was published
src/components/admin/AdminHome.tsx:410 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-06; AUDIT-2 4.3 added this list
EVIDENCE: The list is every `content.publish_due_failed` audit row of the last 7 days (AdminHome.tsx:227-242), deduplicated by document (84-95), with no check of the document's current state; each entry prints `COLLECTION_LABELS[...]: <bdi>{failure.docId}</bdi>` (406-411). A post's doc_id is a uuid (posts.ts:4-5), a room's is 'started' etc. publish_due records `{seq, sqlstate}` (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:327-329) but the home selects only entity and entity_id. The media library already maps ids to names (MediaLibrary.tsx:97-109, AUDIT-1 S09.10).
IMPACT: Anas reads «تعذّر نشر 1 من المحتوى المجدول في آخر 7 أيام: المقالات: 3f9c2a1e-…» in red; he cannot tell which article it was or why, and after he fixes and publishes it the red line stays up to a week, so a second real failure looks like the old one (the S10.12 class).
FIX: Select summary too; drop entries whose document is now live at or after the failed seq (one content_documents read for the listed ids); label posts with documentTitle over latest_data; add the reason for 23505 («معرّف المقال مستخدم في مقال منشور آخر»).

### ADMIN-CMS-07 [low] Validation parity: the site settings and room publish rules run only in the browser; neither the database nor the build re-checks them
src/lib/admin-publish.ts:139 fix:trivial conf:high prior:none; AUDIT-2's parity lens covered the catalog tables
EVIDENCE: publishDocument validates in the browser, then calls `rpc('publish_version', ...)` (admin-publish.ts:136-140); publish_version, granted to authenticated and limited to active owners/editors, checks only the actor and the seq (20260930120000_audit_fixes.sql:167-185); content_versions accepts any JSON object up to 256 KB (20260925120000_content_versions_and_publishing.sql:19). The build parses site settings with siteSettingsStoredSchema and the rooms with the lenient room schemas on purpose (src/lib/content.ts:166-171, 257-276). So only the browser enforces: menu label non-blank, menu href internal-or-https and unique, door href unique, contact page title lines and services title, social network and handle, WhatsApp and contact-email formats (site-settings.ts:156-212), and pull/band lines matching a paragraph (rooms.ts:300-346). Posts, policies, taxonomies and scenes are re-parsed with full schemas at build (journal.ts:69-81, policies.ts:29, content.ts:283-286).
IMPACT: Bounded: only an active owner or editor can publish and the UI path always runs the gate. One calling the Data API directly (or a future client without validateVersion) can publish a menu with a blank or duplicate item or two doors to one room; the build accepts it and every page shows it. React 19 blocks javascript: hrefs, so this is content integrity, not script injection.
FIX: Parse the strict schemas at build too (fail the build like posts do), or record the browser-only gate as an accepted decision in PLANS/DATA-AND-SECURITY.md, whose line 13 currently says the opposite.

### ADMIN-CMS-10 [low] The email problems screen shows only the newest 200 problem rows with no note; bounced 'sent' rows never leave the list, so an older exhausted mail can vanish while «تحتاج تدخل» says there is nothing to do
src/components/admin/EmailView.tsx:188 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-05
EVIDENCE: outbox_attention() returns exhausted/uncertain/suppressed rows and any row with delivery bounced/complained/failed, `order by o.created_at desc limit 200` (20261002120000_order_emails.sql:419-425), so non-actionable sent-and-bounced rows compete with replayable ones; no purge removes sent order or notify mail (20261002150000_notifications.sql:314-317, 426-429; 20260927140000_privacy_requests.sql:129-131, 167-169). EmailView loads once (75-84), filters locally (132-139) and prints «لا توجد مشكلات من هذا النوع.» (188) with no sign the list was cut; only the home marks a capped count with «+» (AdminHome.tsx:66, 187, 312-313).
IMPACT: Once 200 bounces or complaints have accumulated (availability sign-ups typed by visitors bounce), an exhausted order link or receipt older than them is listed nowhere; the operator filters «تحتاج تدخل», reads there is nothing, and the buyer's mail is never replayed.
FIX: Order replayable rows first in outbox_attention (`order by (o.status in ('exhausted','uncertain')) desc, o.created_at desc`) and print «تُعرض أحدث 200 مشكلة فقط.» when 200 rows come back.

### ADMIN-CMS-11 [low] After the server refuses a role change, the team table keeps showing the refused role in that member's row with «حفظ» still enabled
src/components/admin/TeamView.tsx:170 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-03
EVIDENCE: `const role = roleEdits[member.user_id] ?? member.role` (170); roleEdits is written only by the select (186-188) and never cleared, not after a refusal (runAction sets actionError and reloads, 93-96) nor after the step-up retry (99-113). staff-admin refuses the last owner's demotion with «يجب أن يبقى مالك نشط واحد على الأقل.» (staff-admin/index.ts:23-26), printed at the bottom under the invite form (293-297).
IMPACT: The owner tries to demote the only owner, or the network drops: the message appears far below while the row still reads «محرر» with «حفظ» enabled, so a later look at the team table states a role the member does not have.
FIX: Drop the member's roleEdits entry whenever a set_role call for that member returns (`setRoleEdits(({ [userId]: _, ...rest }) => rest)`), and show the refusal next to that row.

### ADMIN-CMS-12 [low] A rich-text body over a size limit cannot be published, and the form explains it in English after a raw schema path
src/admin/richtext.ts:19 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-07; AUDIT-1 X3.21 / AUDIT-2 FIX-admin-3 fixed labels outside rich text
EVIDENCE: The limits carry no message: `text: z.string().max(20000)` (19), `z.array(inlineOrBlock).max(2000)` (15), root `.max(1000)` (53), list `.max(500)` (38). Importing the repo's own src/admin/richtext.ts under Node 24 (SCRATCH/ADMIN-CMS/rt-real.mjs): a 20,001-character text node gives `{"path":["root","children",0,"children",0,"text"],"message":"Too big: expected string to have <=20000 characters"}`, 1,001 blocks `Too big: expected array to have <=1000 items`. CollectionForm prints `{where}: {issue.message}` (340-346) and fieldPathLabel stops resolving inside a richtext field (fields.ts:251-253): «النص › root › children › 1 › children › 1 › text: Too big: ...».
IMPACT: Anas pastes a long essay as plain text (one paragraph per line, blank lines included) or a chapter without paragraph breaks: saving works, «نشر» and «جدولة» stay disabled, and the only explanation is English after a path of English words.
FIX: Give each limit an Arabic message («الفقرة أطول من 20000 حرف؛ قسّمها.», «النص أطول من 1000 فقرة؛ قسّمه أو احذف الأسطر الفارغة.») and let fieldPathLabel print only the richtext field's label.

### ADMIN-CMS-13 [low] AUDIT-2 ADMIN-editor-11 half fixed: «حذف» on a list item or paragraph still removes it at once with no confirm or undo, and the autosave stores the deletion
src/components/admin/FieldInput.tsx:737 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-2 ADMIN-editor-11 / X-A11Y-RTL-13 (naming half fixed); lead:ADMIN-CMS-08
EVIDENCE: `onClick={() => onChange(items.filter((_, itemIndex) => itemIndex !== index))}` (737; paragraphs 663), in one row beside «أعلى»/«أسفل» (712-741, 638-667). The autosave writes the deletion into the local copy on the same change (CollectionForm.tsx:205-218), so a reload offers the copy without the item and «تجاهل» drops every other unsaved edit. ADMIN-editor-11 named both halves («... no per-item name, and delete has no undo»); only the aria-labels were added (643-663, 717-737).
IMPACT: On a phone Anas mis-taps «حذف» beside «أسفل» on a book excerpt, a stage with new paragraphs or a scene caption he just wrote: the item and its unsaved text are gone, with no way back except discarding all his other unsaved edits.
FIX: Keep the last removed item and index and offer «تراجع» for one step (re-insert at the index), or window.confirm('حذف هذا العنصر؟') when the item holds text.

### ADMIN-CMS-14 [low] Text typed while the editor reloads after «نشر», «جدولة», «إلغاء الجدولة», «أرشفة» or «استعادة» is silently replaced: the form stays editable but autosave is off until the reload lands
src/components/admin/CollectionForm.tsx:138 RUNTIME fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-09
EVIDENCE: PublishBar calls onChanged after every successful action (PublishBar.tsx:62-64) and VersionHistory calls onRestored (VersionHistory.tsx:82); both run `void load()` (CollectionForm.tsx:373, 392). A reload keeps the form mounted and editable (143) but sets `hydratedRef.current = false` (138), so the autosave effect returns early (206); after two awaited reads (146-151, 158) it runs `setInitialData(loadedData); setData(loadedData)` (172-173) and bumps loadGeneration (183), remounting every field (323). The e2e suite waits around the same window ('interacting mid-load races the CollectionForm remount and the loaded values', tests/e2e/media.spec.ts:376-377).
IMPACT: Anas presses «جدولة» or «نشر» and keeps writing while the action and the reload run (one to several seconds on a phone connection): what he typed during the reload disappears when it lands, with no local copy; what he typed during the action itself comes back only behind a «يوجد تعديل غير محفوظ محليًا» banner.
FIX: Disable the fields (fieldset disabled) while a reload after an action is in flight, or keep a ref of the latest data and write it as the local copy before replacing the form when it differs from the data the reload started from.
PROOF: Playwright with a 2 s route delay on /rest/v1/content_documents: open a saved post, click «إلغاء الجدولة» or «نشر», then type 'abc' into «مقتطف» while the delayed request is pending. Expect (wrong): after the response the field lacks 'abc' and localStorage 'anasaq:draft:posts:<id>' does not contain it.

### ADMIN-CMS-15 [low] On a slow uplink a large photo can never be uploaded: the original and every derivative must arrive within the ticket's 5 minutes, and «إعادة المحاولة» repeats the same race
src/components/admin/MediaUpload.tsx:252 fix:small conf:medium prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-11
EVIDENCE: runUpload takes one ticket (252), uploads the original (up to 15 MiB, line 26) and up to four derivatives (up to 4 MiB each, line 28) one after another (262-268), then calls media-complete (269). The ticket expires `now() + interval '5 minutes'` (20260926090000_media_library.sql:107) and media_claim refuses an expired one with 55000 (253-255), mapped to «انتهت صلاحية التذكرة أو استُخدمت من قبل.» (supabase/functions/_shared/media.ts:125-126); the signed upload URLs themselves 'are valid for 2 hours' (https://supabase.com/docs/reference/javascript/storage-from-createsigneduploadurl). The busy line shows «جارٍ رفع 1 من N» (263) with no progress; the retry (427-429) starts another 5-minute ticket.
IMPACT: Uploading a 12-15 MB phone photo over weak mobile data (below roughly 0.5 Mbit/s upstream) fails after minutes with a message about an expired 'ticket', every retry fails the same way, and each attempt leaves parts in quarantine until the daily sweep.
FIX: Size the ticket window to the declared bytes (5 minutes plus total bytes at about 50 kB/s, capped well below the URL's 2 hours) and map GONE at completion to «طال الرفع أكثر من المسموح؛ جرّب اتصالًا أسرع أو صورة أصغر.».

### ADMIN-CMS-16 [low] Changing a published article's «المعرّف» silently moves it to a new address: the old /journal/<slug> page disappears at the next build with no warning
src/admin/collections/posts.ts:8 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-12
EVIDENCE: The slug is an ordinary editable field (`{ name: 'slug', label: 'المعرّف', type: 'slug' }`, posts.ts:8) rendered as a plain input (FieldInput.tsx:367-391); nothing compares it with the live slug. Article pages come only from published slugs (`(await getPosts()).map((post) => ({ slug: post.slug }))`, src/app/(public)/journal/[slug]/page.tsx:21-24) in a static export, and public/ has `_headers` but no `_redirects`.
IMPACT: Anas tidies the Latin slug of an article he already shared on X or WhatsApp and republishes: minutes later every shared link answers the 404 page, and nothing told him the address would change.
FIX: When a post has a live version and the slug differs from the live slug, show «تغيير المعرّف يغيّر رابط المقال المنشور، والروابط القديمة لن تعمل.» beside the field; optionally keep old slugs and emit `_redirects` lines at build.

### ADMIN-CMS-17 [low] A schedule whose time has passed is still shown as waiting («مجدول في …», «مستندات تنتظر موعد النشر: 1»), so a stopped publish job never shows in the admin
src/components/admin/AdminHome.tsx:222 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-15; complements FABLE wave B ARCH-13 (cron health over job_run_details, which shows nothing for a job that was never re-scheduled)
EVIDENCE: The home counts `.not('scheduled_at', 'is', null)` (AdminHome.tsx:217-225) and prints «مستندات تنتظر موعد النشر: {count}» (396) without comparing with now; PublishBar prints «مجدول: نسخة N في …» (PublishBar.tsx:146-151) and CollectionList «مجدول في …» (CollectionList.tsx:43-48), past or not. A schedule stays pending until pg_cron's content-publish-due runs publish_due() (20260925120000_content_versions_and_publishing.sql:299), which records no job_runs row (KNOWN_JOBS, AdminHome.tsx:33).
IMPACT: If the minute job stops (a restore without the cron jobs, AUDIT-2 4.11; pg_cron disabled), an article scheduled for 9:00 still reads «مجدول في 9:00» at noon and for days, and the home calls it waiting.
FIX: Treat `scheduled_at < now() - 5 minutes` as overdue: a red «نشر مجدول متأخر: N» on the home, and «تأخّر النشر المجدول (كان في …)» on the bar and the list.

### ADMIN-CMS-18 [low] The documents list has no loading, failure or empty state: until the query answers, and for good when it fails, every room, policy and the settings read «مسودة لم تُنشر» and «لم يُحفظ بعد», and the articles list is a bare table
src/components/admin/CollectionList.tsx:168 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-16
EVIDENCE: rows starts null (56) and stays null when the query fails (68-71). Fixed collections are listed regardless (81-89) and each row renders statusFor(row) and `row ? formatRiyadh(row.latest_at) : 'لم يُحفظ بعد'` (168-169), where statusFor(undefined) is «مسودة لم تُنشر» (41-42). For posts and taxonomies docIds is [] while rows is null (90-92): only the header shows, no «يحمّل...» and no empty-state sentence, against the admin's own D21 rule (AdminHome.tsx:5-6).
IMPACT: On a slow connection or a failed read Anas sees his rooms, policies and settings described as never-saved drafts and an empty articles table that looks as if his articles were gone; he may press «جديد» and start rewriting one.
FIX: Render «يحمّل...» while rows is null without error, hide status and date cells when the load failed, and show «لا توجد مقالات بعد.» / «لا توجد تصنيفات بعد.» for an empty open collection.

### ADMIN-CMS-19 [low] The book photo's «نقطة التركيز» is free text written into an inline style; a value with ';' adds any CSS declaration to the public /book page
src/admin/collections/rooms.ts:244 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-18
EVIDENCE: `{ name: 'focus', label: 'نقطة التركيز', type: 'text', required: false }` (rooms.ts:244) has no pattern in the form, the gate or the build schema (bookRoomSchema, 289). BookView passes `focus={photo.focus || undefined}` (BookView.tsx:194) and Figure writes `style={{ ... '--focus-point': focus }}` (weave/Figure.tsx:42). The repo's React server renderer copies a custom property's value verbatim (HTML-escaped only): SCRATCH/ADMIN-CMS/ssr-style.cjs renders '50% 36%; position: fixed; inset: 0; background: url(https://example.invalid/x.png)' as `<figure style="--focus-point:50% 36%; position: fixed; inset: 0; background: url(https://example.invalid/x.png)">`. Stored values are '50% 36%' and '50% 25%'.
IMPACT: Anyone with an editor session (a lent laptop, a phished editor) can publish a page-covering overlay or an outside background image on /book: defacement and, with no CSP yet (I46), a third-party request from every visitor. No script runs.
FIX: Give the field `pattern: { regex: /^\d{1,3}% \d{1,3}%$/, message: 'اكتب موضعًا مثل 50% 36%.' }` (applies to form, gate and build; both stored values match) and drop a non-matching focus in Figure.

EVIDENCE: jobProblem returns a red sentence only for a waiting email job, a NO_HOOK skip and a stale run (AdminHome.tsx:145-152); a 'failed' or 'partial' run falls through to `${JOB_STATUS_LABEL[run.status] ?? run.status} (${formatRiyadh(run.finished_at)})` (381) inside ul.metaList (371), styled `color: var(--aub-muted); font-size: 0.875rem` (admin.module.css:438-446) like «سليم». site_build_trigger records 'failed' for every non-2xx, timeout or lost call and stops after the fifth (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:735-769); docs/operations.md:38-42 says that «فاشل» after five tries means the hook is broken.

The preview reads the newest content_versions row (RoomPreview.tsx:113-120), never the open form, and its banner says only «معاينة المسودة (نسخة N)…» (RoomPreview.tsx:90-94).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-02 [low] A retry of a refund whose first answer was unknown drops its idempotency key when the retry meets 503 PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE, although the replay never reached the duplicate check
src/lib/admin-money.ts:226 fix:trivial conf:high prior:R11B-1 fixed the key loss on a failed re-read; this path (a retry answered 503) was not covered.
EVIDENCE: admin-money.ts:226 `const UNKNOWN_CODES = new Set(['UNKNOWN', 'FAILED'])` and :302 keep the key only for those (and NOT_ENROLLED); RefundView.tsx:300-304 `if (!reading.keep) { kept.current = null; setFrozen(null) }`. On the server the provider fetch runs BEFORE the SQL that recognises a replay: refunds.ts:228-232 (`providerPaymentId`, then `fetchPayment`, `if (!fetched.ok) return fetchFailed(fetched)` -> 503 PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE «... لم يتغيّر شيء؛ حاول بعد قليل.»), while the same-key lookup is inside refund_request (20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:857-870). tests/unit/admin-money.test.ts:383 pins PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE as keep:false, so the test that says 'keeps the key only when the request is unknown' asserts the opposite for this sequence.
IMPACT: Sequence: the owner confirms a refund, the reply is lost (UNKNOWN: network, a 546/502 from the gateway), the refund was in fact made; he presses «تأكيد الاسترداد» again with the kept key while Moyasar is briefly unreachable -> 503, the confirmation and key are discarded and the screen says nothing changed and to try again. His next confirmation is a new key, so the server cannot answer the stored refund; only REFUND_IN_FLIGHT (while the first is unsettled) or the reduced remainders on the re-read stand between him and a second refund of the same money. Bounded, but it is exactly the case the kept key exists for.
FIX: Treat PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE (and any 5xx the function answers before the SQL ran) as an unknown-request answer: add it to UNKNOWN_CODES (keep the frozen confirmation and the key; the server wrote nothing on that path, so keeping them is always safe) and flip the unit test's expectation for it.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-03 [low] The statistics line «دفعات قيد المراجعة: مفتوحة N، مبالغها X» shows as X the amounts of every review payment that arrived in the range, closed ones included, next to the count of open ones
src/lib/admin-commerce.ts:267 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-04 (confirmed); R11C-6 added the covered-days line, the review line's wording was not examined.
EVIDENCE: admin-commerce.ts:267 renders `دفعات قيد المراجعة: مفتوحة ${review.open}، مبالغها ${review.captured}، المسترد منها ${review.refunded}`. owner_commerce_stats (20261002170000_stats_disputes.sql:341-351) computes `'open', count(*) filter (where pr.closed_at is null)` but `'captured', coalesce(sum(pr.amount_halalas), 0)` and the refunded sum over ALL review payments created in the range (`where pr.environment = p_environment and pr.created_at >= p_from and pr.created_at < p_to`), open or closed. The screen shows no count of all of them, so X cannot be related to any number drawn. The e2e (store-admin.spec.ts:1242) only pins the format.
IMPACT: With three review payments this month, two already closed (refunded or settled by hand) and one open, the owner reads «مفتوحة 1، مبالغها 600.00 ر.س» and believes 600 SAR of customer money is still waiting in review when one payment's amount is. A figure on the one screen meant to say what the numbers are misleads about money held.
FIX: Word the line as what it is: `دفعات وصلت للمراجعة في المدة: مبالغها X، المسترد منها Y، المفتوح منها الآن N` (or have the SQL return `count` and `openAmount` and show both). Update the e2e expectation.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-06 [low] Review payments are drawn, refunded and summed as riyals whatever their currency; no screen shows `currency`
src/components/admin/ReconciliationView.tsx:324 fix:small conf:medium prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-05 (confirmed); related to wave A EF-MONEY-01.
EVIDENCE: apply_verified_payment stores an unmapped invoice's charged payment with the payment's own minor units and currency (`values (v_pay_id, p_invoice_id, p_mode, v_amount, v_currency, v_status, 'UNMAPPED_INVOICE', v_refunded)`, 20261007100000_fable_audit_payments.sql:320-324), and a CURRENCY_MISMATCH review the same way (:395, :427-430). reviewOf parses `currency` (admin-orders.ts:310) but nothing draws it: ReconciliationView.tsx:324-325 and OrderView.tsx:554-557 use `formatMoney(...)` (« ر.س» appended), and the review's refund field is labelled «ر.س» and read with parseRiyals (RefundView.tsx:381, admin-money.ts:146). owner_commerce_stats sums `pr.amount_halalas` with no currency filter (20261002170000_stats_disputes.sql:343).
IMPACT: When the Moyasar account carries anything else (a dashboard invoice or payment link in USD or KWD becomes an UNMAPPED_INVOICE review, wave A EF-MONEY-01), a USD 10.00 payment shows as «10.00 ر.س» and a KWD 10.000 one as «100.00 ر.س»; the owner judges and refunds it as riyals and the statistics add its minor units to riyal figures. The reason label «عملة مختلفة» exists for exactly this case, yet the amounts beside it are mislabelled.
FIX: Show `review.currency` (dir=ltr) beside the amount on both screens and, when it is not SAR, replace the refund form with «دفعة بعملة أخرى؛ أعدها من لوحة Moyasar ثم سجّلها هنا» plus the existing «تسجيل استرداد خارجي»; filter the stats' review sums to SAR.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-07 [low] When a refund's reply cannot be read the screen keeps the key for a safe repeat but tells the owner «حدّث الصفحة», the one action that throws the key away
src/lib/admin-money.ts:27 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-06 (confirmed); R11B-6 introduced BAD_REPLY with keep:true.
EVIDENCE: `export const BAD_REPLY = 'تعذّر قراءة الرد؛ حدّث الصفحة.'` is what readRefundReply answers for an unreadable reply, with `keep: true` 'because the refund may exist' (admin-money.ts:299 and :309). The frozen body and the key live only in component state and a ref (RefundView.tsx:246-248), so a reload loses both (round 11b's recorded risk). The safe repeat is pressing «تأكيد الاسترداد» again with the same key (keyFor, :221-223; refund_request's duplicate branch).
IMPACT: After an unreadable reply (a proxy body, a 2xx without the shape) the owner follows the sentence and reloads; the key is gone, the page may list the refund, and if he reads the sentence as 'it failed' and confirms again a new key is minted and a second partial refund goes through (ADMIN-COMMERCE-05's path).
FIX: Give the refund readers their own sentence for this case, e.g. «تعذّر قراءة الرد وقد يكون الاسترداد تمّ. اضغط «تأكيد الاسترداد» مرة أخرى فلن يتكرر، أو راجع جدول الاستردادات قبل أي استرداد جديد.», and keep «حدّث الصفحة» for readers that keep nothing.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-08 [low] After the switch to live, every test attempt the job parked as MODE_CHANGED stays on «المطابقة» and in «تحتاج مطابقة» for good: its only control, «أعد الفحص», answers «لم نجد محاولة الدفع هذه.»
src/components/admin/ReconciliationView.tsx:289 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-14 (confirmed); attempt counterpart of wave A CONTRACT-04 (other-mode refunds, open); introduced by FABLE-AUDIT M1a-6.
EVIDENCE: payment_reconcile_claim (working tree, M1a-6) marks every due attempt of the other mode `last_error = 'MODE_CHANGED'`, next_check_at null (20261007100000_fable_audit_payments.sql:638-641); reconciliation_list lists it with reason MODE_CHANGED while `a.status not in ('paid','review') and a.provider_invoice_id is not null` (:786-788) and orders_alerts counts it in unverifiedAttempts (20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:424). The mark clears only on an answered prompt (payment_attempt_checked, :1968). The screen's only control is «أعد الفحص» (ReconciliationView.tsx:285-292) -> payment-recheck -> payment_attempt_ref, which reads `where a.id = p_attempt and a.environment = p_mode` (20261002100000_payment_core.sql:1387) and answers NOT_FOUND, worded «لم نجد محاولة الدفع هذه.» (admin.ts:541). Nothing else can clear or file it (the migration's comment says 'a person settles it from the dashboard', but no screen can record that).
IMPACT: Every sandbox attempt still due when PAYMENTS_MODE becomes live (E02's last runs, invoices expired within the last 24 hours) stays on the owner's reconciliation screen and in the home's «تحتاج مطابقة» count permanently; the count never returns to zero, so a real unverified payment or external refund later hides among them, and the recheck tells the owner the attempt does not exist although it is on the screen.
FIX: Give the owner a way to file a parked attempt after checking the dashboard (an `attempt_dismiss` like `event_dismiss`: clears MODE_CHANGED with an audit row) and offer it instead of «أعد الفحص» on MODE_CHANGED rows of the other mode; word the reason «من وضع الدفع الآخر؛ راجعها في لوحة Moyasar» (F2b-11 adds a label only).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-09 [low] The order view offers «إعادة المبلغ» on a paying payment Moyasar already shows voided; the refund is sent, refused, and worded as a gateway refusal with no pointer to «تسجيل استرداد خارجي»
src/components/admin/OrderView.tsx:396 fix:small conf:medium prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-18 (confirmed); related to wave A EF-MONEY-09 and EF-MONEY-15.
EVIDENCE: `const refundTarget = owner && order.status !== 'refunded' ? (d.attempts.find((attempt) => attempt.status === 'paid') ?? null) : null` (OrderView.tsx:396) ignores `attempt.providerStatus`, which the card draws raw («حالة المزوّد: voided», :505). refund_request does not read the provider status (20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:888-952), so after the step-up the row is reserved and the provider is called; Moyasar refunds only `paid` and `captured` payments (https://docs.moyasar.com/docs/guides/payment-operations/index.md), its 4xx is recorded `failed` (refunds.ts:185-189) and the owner reads REFUND_FAILED «رفضت بوابة الدفع الاسترداد؛ لم يُخصم شيء.» (admin-money.ts:31). The reconciliation screen offers the external record on the same attempt (PROVIDER_STATUS, ReconciliationView.tsx:294), the order view says nothing.
IMPACT: After a void in Moyasar's dashboard the owner who opens the order to refund the buyer types his TOTP, gets a refusal that blames the gateway, and the void stays unrecorded (order «مدفوع», files and shipping still live) until he finds the reconciliation screen.
FIX: When the paying attempt's providerStatus is 'voided', replace the refund form with «أُلغيت هذه الدفعة لدى Moyasar؛ سجّل الإلغاء هنا.» and open «تسجيل استرداد خارجي» (refund_record_external records a void as the whole unrefunded amount).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-10 [low] «إكمال الطلب» puts the focus on «تأكيد إكمال الطلب», so a double or held Enter completes the order: R11B-4's rule was applied to the refund confirmation only
src/components/admin/OrderView.tsx:131 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-13 (confirmed); P08 R11B-4 (same pattern, fixed for refunds only).
EVIDENCE: `useEffect(() => { if (confirming) confirmRef.current?.focus() }, [confirming])` (OrderView.tsx:130-132) focuses the button that calls order_resolve (:926-930) as soon as «إكمال الطلب» (:917-925) toggles `confirming`. Enter activates a focused button on keydown and repeats while held. R11B-4 moved the refund confirmation's initial focus to its group for this reason (RefundView.tsx:257-258); this two-step was not changed.
IMPACT: An owner who presses Enter twice, or holds it, on «إكمال الطلب» commits the order without reading «يُخصم المخزون للعناصر غير المعادة، وتُمنح الملفات، ويُرسل إيصال.»: stock is taken, files granted and the receipt mailed, none of which the admin can undo.
FIX: Focus the section's sentence (a tabIndex=-1 element) or a labelled group when `confirming` turns on, as RefundView does, and update the e2e that asserts the focus on «تأكيد إكمال الطلب».

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-11 [low] The refund confirmation never says what a refund does besides moving money: a line refunded in full loses its file for good (and stops shipping), and an unshipped unit does not go back to stock
src/components/admin/RefundView.tsx:339 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-08 (confirmed).
EVIDENCE: The confirmation shows the total, the lines and REFUND_WARNING «سيُعاد المبلغ إلى وسيلة دفع العميل عبر Moyasar، ولا يمكن التراجع عنه.» only (RefundView.tsx:327-339; admin-money.ts:32), and the success sentence is «تمت إعادة …» (admin-money.ts:312). On success the ledger revokes the entitlement of an item whose refunded total equals what it cost and of every item once the attempt is fully refunded, and marks such a line not shippable (finance.item_fully_refunded, 20261002100000_payment_core.sql:475-494; refund_succeed effects, 20261002130000_refunds.sql:186-200), and 'Stock is never touched: an unshipped refunded unit goes back on sale only when the owner edits it' (same comment). A revocation by refund cannot be undone from the admin (dispute «إبقاء الملفات» restores only dispute revocations, fable_audit_operations.sql:1894-1907).
IMPACT: A goodwill refund of a digital book's full price (a typo in the PDF, a late delivery) silently ends the buyer's downloads for good; a refund of a physical copy that never shipped leaves the copy on the shelf but out of stock until the owner remembers the runbook, so it is unsellable meanwhile.
FIX: From the frozen lines, list the consequences in the confirmation: «سيُسحب الملف: <line>» for a digital line whose amount equals its remainder (every digital line when the total empties the payment), «لن يُشحن: <line>» for a physical line still being prepared that is refunded in full, and «لا يعود المخزون تلقائيًا؛ عدّل مخزون <SKU> بعد الاسترداد». Whether a full goodwill refund should keep the file is the owner's policy (see plan gap).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-12 [low] The reconciliation screen's dispute form silently loses «سحب الملفات» and «إيقاف الشحن» when the order's lines cannot be read, and an earlier form's late answer leaves a newer form 'loading' for good
src/components/admin/ReconciliationView.tsx:202 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-07 (confirmed); R11B's uncovered list names this read as untested.
EVIDENCE: orderLines turns any failure into null (ReconciliationView.tsx:68-82) and toggleForm stores whatever answer comes back last, with no check that the form that asked is still open: `const lines = await orderLines(order); setFormLines({ order, lines })` (:199-204). linesOf answers 'loading' whenever formLines names another order (:230-231). DisputeForm maps 'loading' and null alike to `known = null` and offers only ['none', 'entitlement_kept'] (DisputeForm.tsx:73-76; admin-money.ts:383-386), with no sentence saying the lines are loading or could not be read.
IMPACT: The owner opens «إضافة متابعة» on a lost chargeback to stop a parcel: if the read failed, or he had opened another order's form just before and its read answered last, the decision list has no «إيقاف الشحن» and nothing says why; he records «بلا إجراء» (which, since M1b, also lifts an earlier stop: ADMIN-COMMERCE-01) or gives up.
FIX: Keep the open form's key in a ref and drop an answer for a form no longer open; in DisputeForm say «يحمّل بنود الطلب…» while 'loading' and «تعذّر قراءة بنود الطلب؛ أغلق النموذج وافتحه من جديد أو سجّل من صفحة الطلب.» when the order has a number but its lines are null.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-13 [low] The commerce settings screen tells an enrolled owner to enable his authenticator when the factor list merely failed to load
src/components/admin/CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:111 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-17 (confirmed).
EVIDENCE: On STEP_UP_REQUIRED: `const { data } = await supabase.auth.mfa.listFactors(); const verified = data?.totp.find((factor) => factor.status === 'verified'); if (!verified) { setNeedsEnrollment(true); return }` (CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:110-116): the call's `error` is ignored, so a failed read is 'no factor'. The money screens' useStepUp tells them apart ('failed' becomes «تعذّر الاتصال بالخادم.», RefundView.tsx:91-92, admin-money.ts:259).
IMPACT: On a dropped connection the owner pressing «افتح الشراء», «اعتماد السياسات المنشورة» or «حفظ» reads «يلزم تفعيل تطبيق المصادقة أولًا. اذهب إلى صفحة الأمان.», finds it already enabled there, and does not learn why checkout did not open.
FIX: Read `error` from listFactors and, when set, show «تعذّر الاتصال بالخادم. حاول مرة أخرى.» instead of the enrolment sentence (or reuse useStepUp from RefundView).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-14 [low] Changing a digital variant that has a paid file to «ورقي» or «موقّع» fails with the bare «تحقق من القيم.», and no admin action can remove the file
src/admin/tables/variants.ts:73 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-11 (confirmed); AUDIT-1 S11.3 (validation parity).
EVIDENCE: product_variants has `check (fulfillment = 'digital' or digital_asset is null)` (20260927160000_catalog_and_checkout.sql:149). variants.ts `validate` checks only the preorder fields (:73-85) and toRow sends the new fulfillment (:86-103); the update fails with 23514, which TableForm words as CHECK_MESSAGE «تحقق من القيم.», naming no field (TableForm.tsx:230). The owner can no longer write digital_asset (`revoke insert (digital_asset), update (digital_asset) on public.product_variants from authenticated`, 20261002100000_payment_core.sql:69) and only paid_asset_set sets it, so nothing clears it.
IMPACT: The owner who mis-set a variant's type after uploading its file, or wants the printed edition under the same SKU, gets a generic refusal he cannot act on and no way out but a new variant he is not told about.
FIX: In the variant form, when the saved variant is digital and has a file (VariantCommerce already reads it), refuse a change of «نوع التنفيذ» before the call with «لهذا الخيار ملف مدفوع؛ أنشئ خيارًا جديدًا للنسخة الورقية.»; at least map this constraint's 23514 to that sentence.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-15 [low] A digital variant can be put on sale with no paid file and the variant form neither warns nor refuses: buyers pay for a download that does not exist yet
src/components/admin/VariantCommerce.tsx:246 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-10 (confirmed).
EVIDENCE: The panel says only NO_FILE «لا يوجد ملف بعد» (VariantCommerce.tsx:246-247) and appears only after the variant's first save (TableForm.tsx:407-416), while «معروض للبيع» can be ticked in that save. Checkout never looks at the file: finance.availability answers null (unlimited) for a digital variant that is not a preorder (20261002100000_payment_core.sql:457-469) and no checkout function reads digital_asset. The buyer learns it from the receipt: «الملف الرقمي: سنضيفه إلى صفحة طلبك عند توفره.» (supabase/functions/_shared/email.ts:402).
IMPACT: The owner creates the e-book variant, ticks «معروض للبيع» and saves, then comes back to upload (or forgets): every sale meanwhile is a paid order whose buyer expected an instant download and sees «بانتظار الملف»; the waiting path was built for digital preorders, whose product page says so before payment, not for an ordinary digital edition.
FIX: In VariantCommerce, when the saved variant is digital, not a preorder, enabled and has no file, show on the alert line «هذا الخيار معروض للبيع بلا ملف: من يشتريه ينتظر حتى ترفع الملف. ارفع الملف أو أوقف عرضه.»; refusing to save `enabled` without a file would be the owner's call.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-16 [low] Store forms still never mark the offending input invalid: AUDIT-2 X-A11Y-RTL-11 holds for the summary and the Save button only
src/components/admin/TableForm.tsx:349 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-2 X-A11Y-RTL-11 (holds only partly); AUDIT-1 X3.4; lead:ADMIN-COMMERCE-09 (confirmed).
EVIDENCE: TableForm now renders the problems as an always-mounted role=status list and focuses it from an aria-disabled Save (TableForm.tsx:361-396), but the text, slug, number and date inputs it draws through FieldInput (:349-357) get no aria-invalid and no aria-describedby to their message; the only aria-invalid in the store's form code is the money field's own (FieldInput.tsx:349). The AUDIT-2 fix text asked for 'aria-invalid and aria-describedby to the offending input for text fields as MoneyFieldInput does' (artifacts/acceptance/AUDIT-2/findings.json, X-A11Y-RTL-11).
IMPACT: A screen-reader user who blanks a variant's title or types a SKU with a space hears the list once on «حفظ», then tabs back through a long variant form (preorder fields, stock, threshold) with no field announced as invalid and no message read with it.
FIX: Pass TableForm's issues down keyed by field name and render each under its input with an id, setting aria-invalid and aria-describedby on that input (the money field's pattern).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-17 [low] Once the site runs live, every money control on a sandbox order («أعد الفحص», «إعادة المبلغ», «تسجيل استرداد خارجي», «تسجيل اعتراض») is still offered and each answers that the payment does not exist
src/components/admin/OrderView.tsx:515 fix:small conf:high prior:Related to wave A P08-CKPT-05 (fixed by F1-11 as a 404 wording) and CONTRACT-04; neither covers the screens offering the controls.
EVIDENCE: OrderView draws the owner's controls for every attempt (`{owner && (... أعد الفحص ... {attempt.status === 'paid' && ... تسجيل اعتراض})}`, OrderView.tsx:515-536) and the refund form for the paid one (:396, :1058), whatever `attempt.environment` (shown only as «تجريبي», :500). Every one of those actions resolves the attempt with the configured mode: payment_attempt_ref `where a.id = p_attempt and a.environment = p_mode` (20261002100000_payment_core.sql:1387) -> «لم نجد محاولة الدفع هذه.» (admin.ts:541 for the recheck, refunds.ts:167 for refund-create and refund-record-external), and dispute_record `where a.id = p_attempt and a.environment = p_environment` (20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:1813) -> «لم نجد الدفعة المقصودة.». The screen could know the mode: the owner-only `status` action returns `payments.mode` (admin.ts:661, :673), which CommerceSettingsForm already reads.
IMPACT: Sandbox orders paid during E02 stay in the hosted database after go-live (kept as accounting retention, wave B OPS-PRIVACY-20) and keep their lines in «للشحن». The owner who opens one to clean it up is offered a refund (after his TOTP code) and a recheck that both say the payment he is looking at was not found, with no hint that it belongs to the other mode and can only be handled in Moyasar's test dashboard.
FIX: Read `payments.mode` once (the `status` action) and, on an attempt or review payment whose environment differs, replace the money controls with «دفعة من وضع الدفع الآخر؛ تُدار من لوحة Moyasar التجريبية.»; the same sentence on the reconciliation screen's rows of the other mode.

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-19 [low] The refund form stays open while a refund of the same payment is in flight: the owner types his authenticator code and the server then refuses REFUND_IN_FLIGHT, which the screen already knew
src/components/admin/RefundView.tsx:357 fix:trivial conf:high prior:Round 11b accepted that 'an in-flight refund blocks a new one with REFUND_IN_FLIGHT anyway'; the step-up cost of that path was not weighed.
EVIDENCE: RefundView renders the form whenever a field has a remainder (RefundView.tsx:357-430); the item remainders count only succeeded refunds (`item.total - item.refunded`, admin-money.ts:105; order_detail's per-item `refunded` sums `r.status = 'succeeded'`, 20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:240-247), so an in-flight refund leaves the full amount on offer. The screen does know: OrderView.tsx:398 `const rechecks = owner && d.refunds.some((refund) => refundInFlight(refund.status))` draws the «أعد الفحص» column for it. The server asks for the TOTP first (admin.ts:243), then fetches the payment from Moyasar (refunds.ts:231), then refund_request refuses `REFUND_IN_FLIGHT` (fable_audit_operations.sql:898-900). The same holds for a review payment's form (its `refunded` counts succeeded refunds only, 20261002145000_order_operations.sql:133-137).
IMPACT: While a refund is uncertain or still submitting (minutes, or up to 24 hours for an unsettled one), the owner can fill the form, confirm, open his authenticator and type a code, only to read «يوجد استرداد قيد المعالجة لهذه الدفعة...». No money moves, but a step-up and a provider call are spent and the owner learns the rule only by being refused.
FIX: In RefundView, when the subject's payment has a refund in flight (the order's `refunds` with this attemptId, or the review payment's, in submitting/uncertain), show «يوجد استرداد قيد المعالجة لهذه الدفعة؛ أعد فحصه من جدول الاستردادات قبل استرداد جديد.» instead of the form (keep «تسجيل استرداد خارجي»).

### ADMIN-COMMERCE-20 [low] The order's «الاستردادات» table mixes the refunds of its review payments (a duplicate payment, an amount mismatch) with the order's own, with nothing saying which payment each one returned
src/components/admin/OrderView.tsx:1017 fix:trivial conf:high prior:New.
EVIDENCE: order_detail lists every refund whose order_id is the order (`from finance.refunds r where r.order_id = v_order.id`, 20261007110000_fable_audit_operations.sql:308-311), and refund_request stores a review payment's refund with the review's order (`v_order_id := v_review.order_id` ... `values (v_order_id, p_attempt, p_review_payment, ...)`, :854 and :960-961). The table draws status, amount, reason, source and times only (OrderView.tsx:1004-1053); `refund.attemptId` and `refund.reviewPaymentId` are parsed (admin-orders.ts:365-367) but never shown. The totals' «المُعاد» counts only the paying attempt's confirmed refunds (finance.order_refunded_total, 20261002145000_order_operations.sql:68-77).
IMPACT: An order paid twice (SECOND_PAYMENT): the owner refunds the duplicate 150 SAR from its review card. The order's «الاستردادات» table now shows «تم 150.00 ر.س» while «المُعاد: 0.00 ر.س» above it and the buyer's order is untouched; the owner (or a later reader of the screen) cannot tell from the table whether the order itself was refunded, and may refund it again or answer the buyer wrongly.
FIX: Add a «الدفعة» column: «الطلب» for a refund with attemptId, «دفعة قيد المراجعة <paymentId>» (ltr) for one with reviewPaymentId; or draw a review payment's refunds under its own card and keep the table to the paying attempt's.

### DSN-HOME-03 [low] بدأتُ من هنا: the seventh family film is stretched alone on its row, up to twice the size of the other six (768-1920px)
src/components/public/rooms/rooms.module.css:30 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-03
EVIDENCE: `.films > li { flex: 1 1 280px; max-inline-size: 560px }` in a wrapping flex row, so a short last row grows to the 560px cap. grids.mjs (7 visible reels): 768px rows [2x339][2x339][2x339][1x560]; 1024 [3x296][3x296][1x560]; 1280 [3x370][3x370][1x560]; 1440 [4x307][3x417]; 1920 [5x336][2x560]. Screenshot shots/el-started-films-1024.png: the last film is 560x315 beside six 296x167 tiles, pushed to the right edge.
IMPACT: The family-films band, the room's one gallery, looks broken at every tablet and desktop width: whichever film is last in the admin's order is promoted for no reason and the grid has no common column (DESIGN-AUDIT 44).
FIX: Make the films an equal-tile grid: `.films { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, 280px), 1fr)) }` and drop the flex sizing on the li, or use `layout.lattice` with `--tile: 280px`.

### DSN-HOME-04 [low] The gap-free photo mosaic leaves empty cells: ذرى at 1280px is 5 photos and one alone beside four sand holes; مررتُ من هنا's products are 3+3+2 at 1024-1359px and 5+3 at 1920px
src/components/weave/layout.module.css:69 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-05 (mosaic part; AUDIT-2 PUBLIC-style-10 made lattice holes show the band)
EVIDENCE: `.mosaic { display:grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(min(50%, var(--tile, 260px)), 1fr)) }`: the column count follows the width, not the photo count. grids.mjs: /shelf mosaic (6 photos, --tile 220px) 1024px [4][2], 1280px [5][1]; /passed products (8 photos, --tile 340px) 1024 and 1280 [3][3][2], 1920 [5][3]. Screenshots shots/el-shelf-mosaic-1280.png (one photo on row two, four empty cells) and shots/el-passed-products-1280.png (an empty cell at the bottom left). The على الرف doors lattice (3 doors, --tile 280px) splits 2 + 1 from about 630 to 930px (shots/el-shelf-doors-768.png, an iPad in portrait); a lattice may show the band in its last row (layout.module.css:49-53), but three doors could take 1 or 3 columns.
IMPACT: At common laptop widths (1280x800, 1366, a 1024 iPad in landscape) the edge-to-edge photo grids DESIGN.md calls gap-free show holes of bare sand in the two rooms that carry Anas's product photos.
FIX: Choose the column count from the photo count, known at build time: the view sets `--cols` to a divisor of the count (6 photos: 2/3/6; 8 photos: 2/4) and `.mosaic` uses `repeat(var(--cols), 1fr)`, taking the larger count only where tiles stay above the minimum; or let the last row's tiles span the remaining columns.

### DSN-HOME-05 [low] The committed image derivatives lack the source-width step prepare-media.mjs promises: the Moonlight Cup render is drawn 1000px wide from a 720px file (2.8x upscale on retina); I49(6) records this as optional and misstates the cause
public/images/manifest.json:953 fix:trivial conf:high prior:I49(6); lead:DSN-HOME-06
EVIDENCE: scripts/prepare-media.mjs:246 pushes the source's own width when no breakpoint reaches it («round 3 audit fix 1»), but the committed manifest has 40 of 62 image entries whose last derivative is below sourceWidth: render-83 sourceWidth 1134 with derivatives 360/720 only, render-84 1134 -> 720, factory-mug-inside_original-ONLY 960 -> 720, the product photos 1206 -> 1200. The sources are present (BOOK_ASSETS/SORTED_2026-09-21/04-on-the-shelf/moonlight-cup/render-83.jpg is 1134x660). pix.mjs: /shelf at 1440 draws render-83-720.webp at 1000 CSS px (0.72 file px per CSS px at 1x, 0.36 at 2x); render-84 0.56 at 2x; 0.69 at 390px 3x. Crop shots/hd-render83-1440x2.png is visibly soft. PLANS/ISSUES.md I49(6) defers a full run to the P10 media pass «because some local sources are now wider than when the committed derivatives were made»; the manifest disproves that cause here (render-83's recorded sourceWidth 1134 equals the source today) and the record does not mention the upscaled render.
IMPACT: On الرف the main picture of كوب ضوء القمر, a product that will be sold, is soft on every desktop (1.39x at DPR 1) and blurry on retina (2.78x); the rule the P01 audit fixed never reached the shipped files, the build does not regenerate them, and the issue record presents the run as an optional refresh.
FIX: Re-run `node scripts/prepare-media.mjs` (at least render-83, render-84, factory-mug-inside_original-ONLY, 24-boxed-loaf-cake) with BOOK_ASSETS present, commit the derivatives and manifest, and correct I49(6); a unit test can assert every manifest entry's last derivative equals its sourceWidth.

### DSN-HOME-06 [low] The two photo mosaics declare `sizes` smaller than the tiles they fill: مررتُ من هنا's products are drawn up to 1.84x their file at 1280px, ذرى's 1.42x on a 1024px retina screen
src/components/public/rooms/PassedRoomView.tsx:118 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-07
EVIDENCE: PassedRoomView.tsx:118 `sizes="(min-width: 1024px) 25vw, 50vw"` while the products mosaic has 3 columns (33vw) from 1020 to 1359px and the 1:1 cover crop scales landscape photos past the tile; ShelfRoomView.tsx:138 `sizes="(min-width: 1024px) 17vw, 50vw"` while the ذرى mosaic has 4 columns at 1024. pix2.mjs (drawn width incl. object-fit cover x DPR / file width): 1280@1x all eight products load the 360w file for 427px tiles, 1.19x, 23-caramel 1.45x, 26-murady 1.84x; 1440@1x 1.22-1.55x for the two landscape photos; 360@2x 1.22-1.55x; 1024@2x thura-67..81 load 360w for 256px tiles, 1.42x.

### DSN-HOME-07 [low] Seven coloured bands in the rooms open on a raw straight seam, against DESIGN.md's edge rule and the D39 recheck that says «never a raw seam»
PLANS/DESIGN-AUDIT.md:194 fix:small conf:high
EVIDENCE: seams.mjs (a uniform pixel row of one band colour directly followed by another, reduced-motion full-page shots at 1440): /started 4142 sand->aub (films band, StartedRoomView.tsx:31 `<Band tone="aub" pad="s">` with no edge) and 6543 sand->coral (band line, StartedRoomView.tsx:99 `edge={false}`); /built 4344 sand->aub (team band, BuiltRoomView.tsx:141); /passed 3408 sand->aub (ارم films, PassedRoomView.tsx:78); /shelf 3778 sand->coral (inspiration, ShelfRoomView.tsx:162 `edge={false}`), 6679 saffron->paper (moon render band, :231) and 10540 sand->aub (boutique band line, :278). The home page has none. Crops shots/c-started-1440-band.png, c-passed-1440-films.png, c-shelf-1440-moon.png. DESIGN.md:110 «Use a crenel to open a band of a new colour»; DESIGN-AUDIT.md:194 «bands meet at a triangle or weave edge, never a raw seam».
IMPACT: The rooms lose B's signature exactly where a new colour starts mid-story and read flatter than the home page; the acceptance record tells an operator the seams were checked when seven are not, so a P10 design pass would skip them.
FIX: Give those bands their opening crenel (StatementBand's default `edge`, `edge="crenel"` on the films/team/boutique bands, a weave before the moon render band), or, if a plain seam is wanted inside a movement, write that exception into DESIGN.md §4 and correct row 54/75.

### DSN-HOME-08 [low] كوب ضوء القمر's render wipes in as a stark white rectangle, then snaps to the paper tint when the reveal ends
src/components/public/rooms/shelf.module.css:172 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `.render img { mix-blend-mode: multiply }` on a figure with `data-reveal data-fx="media"` (ShelfRoomView.tsx:232). The reveal animates `clip-path: inset(...)` on the figure, which makes it a stacking context and so an isolated group: the image multiplies against the figure's transparent backdrop, not the paper band. frames.mjs at 1440, motion on: at 600ms (clip inset(35% 0 0)) the render shows its white JPEG background (shots/fr-moon-600.png); at 1400ms (clip none) the same box is paper-tinted (shots/fr-moon-1400.png). .renderSmall does not flash because its figure has its own data-tone="paper" background.
IMPACT: Every visitor with motion allowed sees the Moonlight Cup's main picture arrive as a white box on the paper band and change colour at the end: a visible glitch on the room's product image.
FIX: Give the figure the band's colour so the blend has a backdrop inside the clip: `.render { background: var(--bg); }` (or `data-tone="paper"` on the figure, as .renderSmall does).

### DSN-HOME-09 [low] Home: the book cover's solid ink shadow (B's only depth) is clipped during the cover's 1.1s wipe and pops in when it ends
src/components/public/home/HomeView.tsx:121 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: The cover link has `data-reveal data-fx="media"` and `.cover { box-shadow: calc(var(--offset-shadow) * -1) var(--offset-shadow) 0 var(--saffron-ink) }` (home.module.css:143). The media reveal animates clip-path inset() on the link itself, which clips all painting outside its border box, the shadow included. frames.mjs at 1440: at 900ms (clip inset(3% 0 0)) the cover stands with no shadow (shots/fr-cover-900.png); at 1400ms (clip none) the shadow appears at once (shots/fr-cover-1400.png).
IMPACT: The book, the home page's closing image, lands flat and then jumps as its shadow snaps on; small, but it is the one depth effect the design allows.
FIX: Let the shadow travel with the book: drop `data-fx="media"` on the cover link so it takes the default rise reveal (opacity and translate carry the shadow), or move the clip-path reveal to a wrapper that does not draw the shadow.

### DSN-HOME-10 [low] Without JavaScript the header wraps to 200px on a phone and stays sticky, covering about a third of the screen on every page
src/components/site/site.module.css:99 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-HOME-02
EVIDENCE: `@media (scripting: none)` wraps .bar and .inline (8 room links in three rows) but leaves `.header { position: sticky; inset-block-start: 0 }`, and the tuck-away on scroll is script. nojs.mjs/nojs3.mjs (javaScriptEnabled:false): header 200px tall at 320 and 360px (108px at 768); at 360x640 after scrolling 2500px it is still at top 0, 200px tall (shots/nojs-360x640-started-scrolled.png).
IMPACT: A phone reader without JavaScript (blocked scripts, a failed bundle) reads every room through the bottom two thirds of the screen; DESIGN.md promises the pages work without script.
FIX: Inside the existing `@media (scripting: none)` block add `.header { position: static; }` (the rooms are listed inline, nothing needs to stay pinned).

### DSN-HOME-11 [low] The «band» wipe clips every glyph part outside the line box, so tails and dots of large display lines are cut flat during the wipe and drop in when it ends («ذرى», «خوص», the band lines, «عبدالله», «القرني»)
src/components/weave/motion.ts:20 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: FROM.band/TO.band are `inset(0 0 0 100%)` -> `inset(0 0 0 0)` (motion.css:63-70 has the same keyframes for entrances): top and bottom insets are 0, so ink beyond the border box stays clipped until the clip is removed. Display lines at line-height 1-1.25 carry ink outside their box (ink.mjs, canvas metrics): «خوص» 31px below at 1440, «ذرى» 32px, «مررتُ بها… ومرّت بي» 27px, «بوتيك أنس القرني» 26px, the «عبدالله» band 28px above. frames.mjs on /shelf at 1440: at 600ms the tails of «ر» and «ى» end in a flat cut (shots/fr-thura-600.png), at 1400ms they are whole (shots/fr-thura-1400.png); inline `clip-path: inset(0)` on the «القرني» band halves the dots of «ي» (shots/qarni-1440-clipped.png).
IMPACT: With motion allowed (most visitors), every big Arabic line that wipes in arrives with its descenders and dots sliced off and then snaps them on, the opposite of DESIGN.md's «No mark may be hidden» for the length of the wipe, on the home page's name and book title and each room's headings.
FIX: Clip only horizontally: `band: { clipPath: 'inset(-0.6em 0 -0.6em 100%)' }` -> `'inset(-0.6em 0 -0.6em 0)'` in motion.ts and the same in motion.css `@keyframes motion-band` (negative insets extend the clip past the box; 0.6em covers the measured overhangs of 0.11-0.22em below and 0.15em above).

### DSN-HOME-12 [low] Home name band: the dots of «ي» in «القرني» hang over the weave strip from about 1024px up (half of each dot drawn on the pattern)
src/components/public/home/home.module.css:14 fix:trivial conf:high

### DSN-HOME-19 [low] بدأتُ من هنا: a year typed with Arabic-Indic digits («٢٠٢٤», the default on an Arabic phone keyboard) is not recognised as a year: it renders as a paper label band in Arabic-Indic digits

EVIDENCE: `const isYear = /^\d{4}$/.test(movement.year)`; JavaScript's `\d` matches 0-9 only, so «٢٠٢٤» takes the non-year branch (weave + `t-band-xl` paper band, StartedRoomView.tsx:89-96) and prints the digits as typed. The «السنة» field (rooms.ts:48) is free text with no folding, while the codebase already folds Arabic-Indic digits (src/lib/digits.ts, foldDigits in saudi-mobile.ts).

FIX: Fold before the test and the display: `const year = movement.year.trim().replace(/[٠-٩۰-۹]/g, (d) => String(d.charCodeAt(0) & 15))`, then `isYear = /^[0-9]{4}$/.test(year)` and render `{year}`; or fold in the admin field on save.

### DSN-HOME-23 [low] A film started with the native controls before hydration keeps playing afterwards but loses its controls and gets the «تشغيل» overlay on top
src/components/weave/VideoTile.tsx:77 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: Before hydration the video has `controls` (useHydrated() false, so `waiting` false), the documented no-JS path. After hydration `waiting = hydrated && state !== 'playing'` and `state` is still 'idle': the component learns of playback only through its own start(), with no onPlay listener and no check of ref.current.paused on mount. hydrace.mjs (/passed, Next chunks held back, video.play() while unhydrated, chunks then released): before {controls: true, buttons: 0}; playing {paused: false, t: 1.51}; after hydration {controls: false, paused: false, t: 3.83, overlay button «تشغيل: تحلية مكعب أخضر من آرم مودرن»} (shots/hydrace-passed.png).
IMPACT: On a slow connection a visitor who taps play before the scripts finish sees the film keep running under a «تشغيل» label with no pause or sound control; the only way back to the controls is pressing play on a playing film.
FIX: Follow the element: add `onPlay={() => setState('playing')}` to the <video>, and on mount `useEffect(() => { if (ref.current && !ref.current.paused) setState('playing') }, [])` for a play that happened before hydration.

EVIDENCE: The print rule resets only the band itself: `@media print { [data-tone] { background: none !important; color: #000 !important; } }`. Descendants with their own colour keep it: `.solid` actions (`color: var(--action-fg)`, action.module.css:41-45), `.t-muted`, the footer row (`color: var(--muted)`, site.module.css:225). print3.mjs under print media after the entrances: «فلنتحدث» rgb(234, 218, 200), «إلى الكتاب» rgb(239, 160, 50) with their fills dropped (browsers print without background graphics by default); «رحى المكان» and «ذرى · كوب ضوء القمر · بوتيك أنس القرني» rgb(205, 179, 156); footer «المَشاهد», «تواصل», «@anasa.aq» rgb(205, 179, 156): about 1.3:1 and 2.0:1 on white. shots/print3-home.pdf pages 2 and 4.

FIX: Extend the reset to what sits inside a band: `@media print { [data-tone], [data-tone] * { color: #000 !important; } .solid { border: 2px solid #000; } }`.

### DSN-PAGES-11 [low] The rich-text renderer forces every block right-to-left, while the editor shows each top-level block with dir=auto: an English paragraph or quote in a post (or a policy) prints its full stop or question mark on the wrong side

EVIDENCE: renderNode returns <p>, <h2>/<h3>, <blockquote> and <li> with no dir (richtext.tsx:39-48, 63), so every block inherits the page's rtl. The admin editor is Lexical 0.51.0 (node_modules/lexical/package.json): $getReconciledDirection (Lexical.dev.js:5161-5181) gives a top-level block whose stored direction is null dir="auto", and typed text leaves direction null (setDirection is only called from a DOM paste or when splitting a block), so what Anas sees in the editor is per-block auto direction while the stored JSON carries no direction at all. bidi.mjs on the site CSS: «Good food is a promise kept.» renders as «.Good food is a promise kept» (G at x=597, the period at x=590, i.e. left of the text) and «Is it worth it?» as «?Is it worth it»; with dir="auto" the period sits at the end (shots/bidi.png).
IMPACT: A quotation or sentence in English inside an Arabic post, and any Latin-script paragraph in a policy, shows its punctuation at the wrong end on the public page, unlike the editor preview Anas approved; readers see a typo the author never typed.
FIX: Render top-level blocks with the direction the editor used: dir={node.direction ?? 'auto'} on paragraph, heading, quote and list (or at least unicode-bidi: plaintext on .prose and .policyBody blocks, which keeps right alignment but places punctuation by the paragraph's own first strong letter). Reading node.direction alone, as the earlier lead proposed, would not help: it is null for typed text.

IMPACT: A visitor without JavaScript (blocked scripts, a failed bundle, some reader modes) reads every page on a phone through a window two thirds high; DESIGN.md section 6 promises every page readable without JavaScript.
FIX: Inside the existing @media (scripting: none) block add .header { position: static } (or relative), so the wrapped link rows scroll away with the page.

### DSN-PAGES-13 [low] No global-error file: an error in the public root layout's client code falls through to Next's built-in English page without lang or dir
src/app/(public)/error.tsx:11 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-11 (confirmed)
EVIDENCE: src/app holds global-not-found.tsx but no global-error.tsx. Next.js docs (https://nextjs.org/docs/app/api-reference/file-conventions/error, v16.4.0): error.js «does not wrap the layout.js or template.js above it in the same segment. To handle errors in the root layout, use global-error.js». (public)/layout.tsx renders the client components SiteHeader (matchMedia, scroll and focus listeners, dialog.showModal) and MotionLayer outside the (public)/error.tsx boundary. The installed next 16.3.5 falls back to node_modules/next/dist/client/components/builtin/global-error.js: <html id="__next_error__"> with no lang/dir, «This page couldn’t load», «Reload to try again, or go back.», buttons «Reload» and «Back». error.tsx also calls reset(); the same docs make retry() the stable recovery from 16.3 («In most cases, you should use retry() instead»).
IMPACT: If the header or the motion layer ever throws in a visitor's browser (an old engine, an extension, a future change), the Arabic site is replaced by an English, left-to-right error card; rare, but it is the one failure the public error page was written to cover.
FIX: Add src/app/global-error.tsx ('use client', its own <html lang="ar" dir="rtl"> and minimal inline styles, the wording of error.tsx and a retry()), and switch error.tsx to retry().

### DSN-PAGES-14 [low] The contact form's two stop notices send visitors to «القنوات المجاورة» even in the designed state with no channels at all
src/components/public/contact/ContactForm.tsx:219 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:DSN-PAGES-13 (confirmed)
EVIDENCE: ContactForm.tsx:219 «التحقق من المرسل غير مُعدّ بعد، فالإرسال متوقف مؤقتاً. راسلني عبر القنوات المجاورة.» and :223 (noscript) «الإرسال يحتاج JavaScript؛ راسلني عبر القنوات المجاورة.» are unconditional, while contact/page.tsx:47 renders the channel list only when WhatsApp or a social link is set, and DESIGN.md section 8 names the state with no channels at all (also the first hosted build per I41). The form gets no prop telling it whether channels exist; the failed-widget notice (line 221) offers no way out at all. Related: when the widget fails to load, the button stays enabled and a send answers «أكمل التحقق من أنك لست آلياً، ثم أرسل.» (line 100) under the notice «تعذّر تحميل التحقق من المرسل. أعد تحميل الصفحة.» (line 221), asking for a check that is not on the page (shots/contact-1440.png shows that state with the script blocked).
IMPACT: A visitor without JavaScript, or on a build without the Turnstile key, is told to use channels that are not on the page: a dead end on the one page meant to reach Anas.
FIX: Pass hasChannels from the page and point to the channels («راسلني عبر القنوات أدناه») only when they exist; otherwise drop the pointer (or show the published contact e-mail once I45 (3) is decided). When turnstileFailed is true, make the status repeat the reload advice instead of asking to complete the check.

EVIDENCE: .ways (lost.module.css:17-24) is a wrapping flex row with gap 1px, padding 1px and background var(--aub-800), so any row the links do not fill shows the background. At 360 the seven links wrap 3-3-1: a dark strip beside «بنيتُ هنا» and a large dark slab beside «المجلس» (shots/no-such-page-360.png). AUDIT-2 PUBLIC-style-10 fixed the same pattern in layout.lattice by drawing the lines on the tiles (layout.module.css:49-66); Lost.tsx was not changed.
