### STORE-UI-05 [low] A refunded or under-review order kept by the tab locks /checkout for the rest of the tab's life
src/components/store/PaymentReturn.tsx:109 fix:small conf:high prior:FABLE-AUDIT wave A CONTRACT-08, planned as F2b-10 (not applied in today's tree)
EVIDENCE: The return page spends the stored order only on paid, expired and cancelled (lines 108-109); HoldView calls onEnd only for cancelled/expired and shows `closed === 'received'` (paid, paid_needs_resolution, refunded, review, or reason UNDER_REVIEW: lines 55, 61) as «وصلتنا دفعة هذا الطلب.» with one link to the return page (lines 210-218); CheckoutForm renders HoldView whenever a pending order is stored (line 456). Rendered (p5.out.txt, case B): verify answers refunded → «أُعيد مبلغ هذا الطلب.», `anasaq:pending-order` kept; /checkout then shows only «رقم الطلب… الإجمالي: 94.00 ر.س / وصلتنا دفعة هذا الطلب. / عرض حالة الطلب» (shots/p5-refunded-tab-checkout-360.png) although the cart holds another item.
IMPACT: After a late payment that was refunded (or while a payment waits for the owner's review, which can take days), the buyer cannot order anything in that tab: every visit to /checkout shows the old order, says a payment arrived even when the money went back, and offers no way on; only closing the tab frees it.
FIX: As planned in F2b-10, release the stored pending order and its idempotency key for refunded (and review/needs_resolution) on the return page, and give HoldView's received state a way to start over; but do not reuse the paid path's `writeCart(EMPTY_CART)` for refunded: the money went back, so the buyer's cart must stay (brief-F2b.md says 'the same clearing the paid state does', which would empty it).

### STORE-UI-06 [low] «تطبيق» sits 18px below the coupon field it belongs to (cart and checkout, wide screens)
src/components/store/store.module.css:383 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-05
EVIDENCE: `.coupon { display:flex; align-items:flex-end }` (lines 380-386) aligns the bottom of the button with the bottom of the coupon's `<label className={styles.field}>`, whose `.field { margin-block-end: 18px }` (line 396) is part of its margin box. Measured in the export at 1440 (p6b-zoom.mjs): input 483-539px, button 501-557px, so the button hangs 18px lower; the same in the checkout summary (p2-checkout-1440.png: input 610-664, button 628-682). Screenshot shots/p6b-coupon-1440.png. The `.coupon input { inline-size: 12rem }` rule (line 387) is dead: `.field input` (line 400, same specificity, later) sets inline-size 100%.
IMPACT: The one control pair in the order summary panel is visibly misaligned on every tablet and desktop, on the pages the owner wants to look official.
FIX: `.coupon .field { margin-block-end: 0 }` (the .coupon block already has its own margins).

### STORE-UI-07 [low] An unbreakable run in a product or variant title still widens the checkout summary and the order page's return form past a phone screen
src/components/store/store.module.css:488 fix:trivial conf:high prior:P08 round 12a (the spec changed its seeded title instead of the page)
EVIDENCE: The store fixed this input for the product head (`.head h1 { overflow-wrap: anywhere }`, line 24, 'an ISBN, a Latin edition name') and the cart and order line titles (.lineTitle, .wrap), but `.summaryLines li` (lines 488-495, the checkout summary's `{productTitle}: {variantTitle} × n` text) and `.quantityLabel` (lines 196-202, the return form's «الكمية: {title}: {variant} (حتى n)», OrderPage.tsx:245-247) have no overflow-wrap. Rendered with the title «خوص ISBN9786039123456SPECIALEDITIONSIGNED» and the variant «SignedFirstEditionNumbered0001of0500» (p7b-run.mjs, p7b.out.txt): at 360 the checkout summary's <li> is 368px wide in a 278px box and the page scrolls 49px sideways; the order page scrolls 80px and the return quantity input sits at x -80..-8, off screen (shots/p7b-run-orders-360.png). With hyphenated runs nothing overflows (p7.out.txt).
IMPACT: A product named with a long Latin or numeric run (an edition code, a SKU-like name) makes the checkout page pan sideways on phones and pushes the return quantity field out of view, on the two pages a buyer must complete.
FIX: Add `overflow-wrap: anywhere` to `.summaryLines li` and to `.quantityLabel > span` (or apply styles.wrap there), matching the product head and the line cards.

### STORE-UI-08 [low] After a reload, a `pay` that fails (throttle, lost connection, unreadable reply) leaves «نجهّز صفحة الدفع…» on screen beside the failure
src/components/store/HoldView.tsx:44 fix:small conf:high prior:P08 round 4b 'uncovered' (a network failure of pay after a reload)
EVIDENCE: A view opened from storage starts as `WAITING = { total: null, holdEnds: null, payment: { state: 'preparing' }, closed: null }` (line 44, used at line 87) and loadPayment's failure branches only set submitError (lines 125-129), so the placeholder `preparing` is rendered as if the function had said so (lines 245-249). Rendered (p9-hold-return.mjs, p9.out.txt) with a stored order and `pay` answering 429: «رقم الطلب: K7M2P9QX / نجهّز صفحة الدفع… / أعد المحاولة / إلغاء الطلب / … / أرسلت طلبات كثيرة؛ حاول لاحقًا.»; with a 502 HTML reply: «نجهّز صفحة الدفع…» then «تعذّر الاتصال بالخدمة؛ أعد المحاولة.». With 503 CHECKOUT_DISABLED it says both «تعذّر تجهيز الدفع؛ حاول بعد لحظات.» and «الشراء غير متاح حاليًا، ويفتح قريبًا.».
IMPACT: The buyer coming back to a held order is told the payment page is being prepared while nothing is being prepared (the request failed), next to a sentence saying it failed; with the store closed he gets two contradictory instructions, at the moment he decides whether to wait or retry.
FIX: Give WAITING a neutral payment state (e.g. `{ state: 'unknown' }`) that renders no sentence (or «تعذّر تحميل حالة الدفع.» once a pay attempt failed), and on CHECKOUT_DISABLED keep only the store-closed sentence.

### STORE-UI-09 [low] «نجهّز صفحة الدفع…» never moves on by itself: the hold view asks `pay` again only when pressed or at the hold's end
src/components/store/HoldView.tsx:141 fix:small conf:high
EVIDENCE: The mount effect asks `pay` only when the view has no payment (`start === null || (start.payment === null && ...)`, line 141); a `create` reply carrying `{state:'preparing'}` (checkout.ts:480, when startPayment threw or the creation is uncertain or being made) sets `start.payment`, so nothing is asked; the only timer fires at the hold's end (lines 149-153). Server side, an uncertain creation is resolved only by a later `pay`, and one younger than 60 s answers `preparing` again (payments.ts:43 YOUNG_MS, 194). tests/e2e/cart-checkout.spec.ts:597-610 passes only because the test presses «أعد المحاولة».
IMPACT: When Moyasar is slow (the case `preparing` exists for), a buyer who waits for «نجهّز صفحة الدفع…» to finish waits until his 20-minute hold runs out; one who presses at once may get the same sentence again with no hint to wait a minute. The order holds stock meanwhile.
FIX: While the payment is `preparing`, ask `pay` again on a short bounded schedule (e.g. 5, 15, 45 and 75 s, nothing counting on screen, like the return page's verify schedule), then leave «أعد المحاولة».

### STORE-UI-10 [low] Reloading a notification link page says «الرابط غير مكتمل.»: the page removed the token from the address and kept it nowhere
src/components/store/NotifyAction.tsx:106 fix:small conf:high
EVIDENCE: The mount effect takes the fragment out of the address (`takeFragment()` → history.replaceState, src/lib/orders.ts:65-74) and keeps the token in React state only (NotifyAction.tsx:52-61); after a reload the fragment is gone, `token` becomes null and the text is INCOMPLETE = «الرابط غير مكتمل.» (lines 37 and 106). Rendered (p10-orders.mjs, p10.out.txt): /notify/unsubscribe#<token> → press → 429 «حاول بعد قليل.» with the button kept → reload → «إلغاء الإشعار / الرابط غير مكتمل.»; the same after a successful confirm. The order page solves the same problem by keeping its pair in sessionStorage (ORDER_ACCESS_KEY, src/lib/orders.ts:24, 76-81).
IMPACT: A visitor who reloads the unsubscribe page to try again after «حاول بعد قليل.» (or whose phone reloads a discarded tab) is told the link he followed is incomplete, which is false, and has no way on but to find the mail again; for an unsubscribe request this reads as the site refusing to stop mailing him.
FIX: Keep the token in sessionStorage for the tab (as /orders does) until a final answer, or at least say «افتح الرابط من الرسالة مرة أخرى.» when the page has no token after a reload instead of claiming the link is incomplete.

### STORE-UI-11 [low] A name with a pasted TAB is refused by the function and the form answers «أدخل الاسم.» under the filled name (GAP-G4-4 only half fixed)
src/components/store/CheckoutForm.tsx:640 fix:trivial conf:high prior:AUDIT-2 GAP-G4-4 (partial)
EVIDENCE: The name input stores the raw value (`setName(e.target.value)`, line 640) and the client only checks `!name.trim()` (line 297); the function refuses control characters (`.refine(noControlCharacters, 'لا يُقبل اسم فيه رموز تحكم.')`, checkout.ts:94; noControlCharacters, commerce-settings.ts:18, refuses U+0000-U+001F and U+007F-U+009F) and the form maps any fieldErrors.name to FIELD_ERRORS.name = «أدخل الاسم.» (lines 109, 408-410). A real clipboard paste of «أنس<TAB>القرني» (two spreadsheet cells) keeps the TAB in an <input type=text> (p11-tab.mjs: `pasted value has TAB: true`), is sent as is, and the answer shows «أدخل الاسم.» under the field that visibly holds «أنس القرني» (p11.out.txt, shots/p11-name-tab-360.png). The dedication field already folds control characters to spaces (cart.ts:135).
IMPACT: A buyer who pasted his name is told to enter a name he can see he entered; nothing shows the invisible TAB, so he may give up or retype blindly. AUDIT-2 GAP-G4-4's fix put the error under the field but left the words and the cause.
FIX: Fold control characters to spaces as the buyer types or before sending (the same Unicode Cc replacement setDedication uses, cart.ts:135), and give the refine failure its own sentence if it can still happen.

### STORE-UI-12 [low] The cart still swallows a failed city list: an empty select and «اختر مدينة التوصيل.» with no failure note or retry (STORE-checkout-5 fixed in the checkout only)
src/components/store/CartView.tsx:116 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-2 STORE-checkout-5 (partial); lead:STORE-UI-08; same defect as lead QUALITY-07
EVIDENCE: `fetchCities().then(setCities).catch(() => setCities([]))` (line 116) turns a failed read into an empty list; the select (lines 318-336) then has only «اختر المدينة», and the quote's CITY_REQUIRED says «اختر مدينة التوصيل.». CheckoutForm got the fix (citiesState, «تعذّر تحميل قائمة المدن.» and «أعد المحاولة», lines 187-195 and 690-705); the cart did not. Rendered with shipping_rates answering 500 (p11.out.txt): options [«اختر المدينة»] and «اختر مدينة التوصيل.» beside it.
IMPACT: A buyer of a physical item is told to choose a delivery city from a list that offers none and is given no reason or retry; only continuing to the checkout (which reloads the list) gets him out.
FIX: Reuse the checkout's pattern in the cart: a 'failed' state that shows «تعذّر تحميل قائمة المدن.» with «أعد المحاولة», and «لا نوصل إلى أي مدينة حاليًا.» when the list loads empty.

### STORE-UI-13 [low] After its 30-second schedule the return page still says «جارٍ التحقق من الدفع…» when nothing is being checked
src/components/store/PaymentReturn.tsx:138 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-04
EVIDENCE: `let headline = 'جارٍ التحقق من الدفع…'` stays for state `pending` whether or not anything is still being asked; `done` only adds «تحديث» (lines 132-149, 189-194). Rendered with verify answering pending and a fake clock run through the schedule (p13-leads.mjs (a), p13.out.txt): 6 verify calls, then «جارٍ التحقق من الدفع… / متابعة الدفع / تحديث» (shots/p13a-return-after-schedule-360.png). The invoice's back_url is this page (payments.ts:206-213), so a buyer who leaves the invoice without paying, or whose card was declined, lands here. (Same line as STORE-UI-03, a different state.)
IMPACT: A buyer who has not paid is told his payment is being verified, indefinitely; he may wait for a confirmation that will not come instead of pressing «متابعة الدفع» before his hold ends.
FIX: Once the schedule has run out (or a throttle stopped it) with the state still pending, replace the line with a settled sentence such as the order page's «بانتظار الدفع.» (or «لم يتأكد الدفع بعد.»), keeping «متابعة الدفع» and «تحديث».

### STORE-UI-14 [low] «تأكيد الطلب» and the hold view's buttons are `disabled` while their request runs, so every refusal or failure drops keyboard focus to the document
src/components/store/CheckoutForm.tsx:790 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-03; also lead A11Y-RTL-04
EVIDENCE: `disabled={!turnstileAvailable || submitting || waiting || stale || noCities || policiesStale}` on the submit (line 790) and `disabled={submitting}` on «إلغاء الطلب» and «أعد المحاولة» (HoldView.tsx:224, 262, 267); a focused button that becomes disabled loses focus. Rendered (p8-create.mjs, p8.out.txt): after create answered TURNSTILE, QUOTE_CHANGED, ACTIVE_HOLD (another email), RATE_LIMITED and IDEMPOTENCY_CONFLICT, document.activeElement is BODY. The order page's own buttons use `aria-disabled` for exactly this reason (OrderPage.tsx:159, 290, 387, 508).
IMPACT: A keyboard or screen-reader buyer who presses «تأكيد الطلب» and gets a refusal is thrown to the top of the page; the alert is announced but he must find the form again, on the page that takes his money.
FIX: Use the order page's pattern: keep the buttons enabled with `aria-disabled={submitting || waiting || undefined}` (submit() already returns early on `submitting`; add the same guard to loadPayment), and disable only for the static reasons (no Turnstile, stale quote, no cities, stale policies).

### STORE-UI-15 [low] A tracking number with hyphens is broken across lines on a phone and reads «-SMSA-290012345678» then «RUH»
src/components/store/OrderPage.tsx:146 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-06
EVIDENCE: `<p className={`${styles.note} ${styles.wrap}`}>رقم التتبع: <span dir="ltr">{item.tracking}</span></p>` with `.wrap { overflow-wrap: anywhere }`: the isolated LTR run is broken at a hyphen like any text. Measured at 360 (p13-leads.mjs (b)): the span's client rects are x 70-249 on the first line and x 288-323 on the second; the screenshot shows «رقم التتبع: -SMSA-290012345678» with «RUH» alone at the start of the next line (shots/p13b-tracking-360.png); the same at 320.
IMPACT: The one value a buyer copies or reads to a courier is shown with a leading hyphen and its suffix detached, so it is easily misread or mistyped.
FIX: Render the code as a unit: `<bdi dir="ltr" class=code>` with `.code { display: inline-block; max-inline-size: 100%; overflow-wrap: anywhere }`, so it moves whole to its own line and breaks inside only when wider than the line.

### STORE-UI-16 [low] With storage blocked, leaving the checkout and coming back forgets the held order and mints a new checkout session, so the same cart is ordered again while the first order holds its stock
src/lib/cart.ts:474 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-07
EVIDENCE: `checkoutSession()` (lines 474-480) reads sessionStorage and otherwise mints a new UUID whose write is dropped; CheckoutForm caches it per mount only (`sessionRef`, CheckoutForm.tsx:160, 355), and `readPendingOrder()` returns null, while the cart survives client navigations in the module's memoryCart (cart.ts:233). Rendered with both storages throwing (p13-leads.mjs (c), p13.out.txt): add the e-book, cart, checkout, create → hold view «رقم الطلب: K7M2P9QX»; back to /cart; «المتابعة لإتمام الطلب» → the form again; a second create goes out with a new key and a new session (`ac3f606c…` then `6a22ff3c…`), so ACTIVE_HOLD cannot catch it.
IMPACT: In a browser that blocks site storage, a buyer who steps back to the cart and returns creates a second order; the first keeps its stock for 20 minutes and cannot be paid or cancelled from the tab (its token is gone), and each round trip spends the IP's order allowance.
FIX: Give the checkout session and the pending order the same in-memory fallback the cart has (module-level values in cart.ts used when sessionStorage throws), so they survive client navigations within the tab.

### STORE-UI-17 [low] The hold's end is a bare Riyadh clock time: a buyer in another time zone reads «محجوز حتى 14:54» against his own clock
src/lib/cart.ts:420 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:STORE-UI-09
EVIDENCE: formatRiyadhTime formats with `timeZone: 'Asia/Riyadh'` (lines 419-423) and HoldView.tsx:241 and CheckoutForm.tsx:779 print it with no zone. Rendered with the same hold (11:54Z) in browsers set to other zones (p13-leads.mjs (d)): Asia/Dubai shows «محجوز حتى 14:54» while that instant is 15:54 on the device; Europe/London shows 14:54 for a device time of 12:54. P08-CONTRACT.md section 10 asks for «a plain clock time in Riyadh»; digital-only carts need no Saudi address or phone.
IMPACT: A Gulf buyer an hour ahead sees a hold that seems already over; a buyer in Europe believes he has two more hours than he does and finds the order expired.
FIX: Say which clock it is («محجوز حتى 14:54 بتوقيت السعودية»), or show the instant in the device's own zone (the timer already works on the instant).

### STORE-UI-18 [low] A press made before Turnstile has a token turns «تأكيد الطلب» into a disabled «جارٍ الإرسال…» while nothing is sent and the visitor may still have a check to complete
src/components/store/CheckoutForm.tsx:792 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: With no token the press only sets `awaitingToken` (lines 324-331), `waiting` disables the button (line 790) and its label becomes `submitting || waiting ? 'جارٍ الإرسال…' : 'تأكيد الطلب'` (line 792); no sentence asks the visitor to complete the check, unlike the order recovery and availability forms («أكمل التحقق من أنك لست آلياً، ثم أرسل.», OrderPage.tsx:320, AvailabilityForm.tsx:113). Rendered with a widget that never answers (p17-wait.mjs): 5 s after the press the button reads «جارٍ الإرسال…», disabled, and no create was sent (shots/p17-waiting-token-360.png). In managed mode Turnstile can present an interactive challenge that waits for a click before calling back (developers.cloudflare.com/turnstile/get-started/client-side-rendering/widget-configurations/: timeout-callback, refresh-timeout).
IMPACT: A buyer whose network or browser gets an interactive challenge sees the order «being sent» and a dead button, with no hint that the box above the button wants a click; he may wait, reload (losing the typed details) or leave.
FIX: While waiting, keep the label «تأكيد الطلب» (or «بانتظار التحقق…») and, if no token arrives within a few seconds, show «أكمل التحقق أعلاه ليُرسل الطلب.» next to the widget.

### ADMIN-CMS-21 [info] Note for the in-flight F1 staff-admin change: its revoke failure replies no longer match what TeamView then shows
supabase/functions/staff-admin/index.ts:146 fix:trivial conf:medium prior:lead:ADMIN-CMS-20; FABLE F1 (unaudited)
EVIDENCE: Uncommitted diff: `if (banError) await admin.from('staff').update({ active: true }).eq('user_id', userId)` (137) now precedes the unchanged `fail(500, 'BAN_FAILED', 'حُدّثت الحالة لكن تعذّر إيقاف الدخول. حاول مرة أخرى.')` (146), and a new SESSIONS_FAILED «أُوقف الدخول لكن تعذّر إنهاء الجلسات المفتوحة. حاول مرة أخرى.» returns after a successful ban (147). TeamView prints the message, reloads and offers one toggle per row («إيقاف» / «استعادة», TeamView.tsx:93-95, 214-226); its comment still says 'BAN_FAILED commits `active` first' (94).
IMPACT: Once F1 lands: after a ban failure the owner reads that the status was updated while the row says «نشط»; after SESSIONS_FAILED he is told to retry while the row says «موقوف» and its only control restores the member.
FIX: Word BAN_FAILED «تعذّر إيقاف الدخول، وبقي العضو نشطًا. حاول مرة أخرى.»; after SESSIONS_FAILED offer «أعد الإيقاف» on that row; update the TeamView comment.

### DSN-HOME-22 [info] The «three small triangles» mark renders as a crown: its 12px square tiles make the conic wedge spill into a solid base, unlike the crenel edges' separate triangles
src/components/weave/action.module.css:97 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `.mark { inline-size: 36px; block-size: 12px; background: conic-gradient(from 135deg at 50% 0, var(--coral) 90deg, transparent 0) 0 0 / 12px 12px repeat-x }`: in a square tile the 90deg wedge from the top centre meets the sides at half height, so the three tiles fill pentagons joined by a 6px solid strip; the menu button's version (site.module.css:85-88, 9x12 tiles) does the same with two peaks. The crenel edges use 2:1 tiles (edge.module.css:9-10) where the wedge ends at the tile's corners. DPR-4 crop shots/mark-x4.png: three peaks on a solid base. Action.tsx:102-104 and DESIGN.md call it the three-triangle mark.
IMPACT: Brand detail only: the mark before «أنس» in every header, on «القائمة» and on the home's coral addition reads as a crown rather than three Najdi triangles.
FIX: If separate triangles are meant: `conic-gradient(from 153.43deg at 50% 0, var(--coral) 53.13deg, transparent 0)` on the 12px tile (same in .markInk; the menu button with its own ratio); if the crown is intended, say so in DESIGN.md §5.

### DSN-HOME-26 [info] Dead CSS: `.splitCenter` in the shared layout module is used nowhere
src/components/weave/layout.module.css:14 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `.splitCenter { align-items: center; }`; `grep -rn splitCenter src` finds only the definition; deadcss.cjs over the slice's 17 CSS modules found no other unused class once dynamic `styles[variant]`/`styles[size]` lookups are counted.
IMPACT: None at runtime.
FIX: Delete the three lines.

### DSN-HOME-27 [info] The skip link's focus ring is saffron drawn outside it on the sand header (1.32:1), against DESIGN.md's rule that a self-toned control draws its ring inside
src/styles/globals.css:362 fix:trivial conf:high
EVIDENCE: `.skip-link:focus-visible { transform: none; outline-color: var(--saffron); }` keeps the global `outline-offset: 3px`, so the ring lies outside the aubergine link, over the sand header. focus.mjs (first Tab on every slice page, 360 and 1440): ring rgb(239, 160, 50) against rgb(224, 198, 173), 1.32:1.
IMPACT: Small: the link appearing is itself a strong focus cue (WCAG 2.4.7 holds), but the ring adds nothing on sand and breaks the documented rule on the first stop of every page.
FIX: Add `outline-offset: -6px;` to `.skip-link:focus-visible` (saffron on aubergine, 5.0:1).

### DSN-PAGES-19 [info] Proposal for the article page inside direction B: at desktop the 33em column hugs the start edge under a full-width title, and the meta line runs author, date and reading time together
src/components/public/journal/journal.module.css:188 OWNER fix:small conf:medium prior:none
EVIDENCE: article.mjs at 1440: the title spans 72-1368px in three lines of 178px type, then .prose (max-inline-size: var(--measure-article), no margin-inline) sits at x 678-1368 and the signature under it, so the end half of the sand band (about 600px) stays empty for the whole article (shots/article-1440.png). .meta (lines 169-176) is a flex row with only a 20px gap: «أنس عبدالله القرني  7 أكتوبر 2026  دقيقتان للقراءة» (shots/article-360-head.png, where it wraps after the date).
IMPACT: No defect; the long-form page reads as a narrow strip at the right of a wide band, and the author name and the date can read as one run.
FIX: Inside B: give the article a two-column frame at >= 1100px (the text column at the start, the end column carrying the cover caption, the categories and the signature, or the pull lines), or centre the measure under the title; separate the meta items with « · » (typeset() already keeps that separator off a line start).

### PERF-09 [info] The film tile bundles the whole video manifest into the room pages' JavaScript
src/components/weave/VideoTile.tsx:5 fix:small conf:high
EVIDENCE: VideoTile.tsx:1 `'use client'` and :5 `import videoManifestRaw from '../../../public/media/manifest.json'`. The chunk loaded by /started, /built and /passed (out/_next/static/chunks/420oqbivl5yj7.js, 5,519 B raw, 1,771 B gzip) carries all 18 entries with room, bytes, audioKept and totalBytes (e.g. `"arm-modern-red-drink_HD":{room:"passed",file:"media/arm-modern-red-drink_HD.mp4",bytes:232170,...}`). images.ts:7-8 records the rule this skips: a client component gets its strings as props instead of importing a manifest (P01 audit fix 10).
IMPACT: About 1.2 KB gzip of every film room's initial JavaScript is data no page uses, in a budget with 1-3 KiB of headroom.
FIX: Resolve file, width, height and poster in the server components that render VideoTile and pass them as props, as Picture/ImageSources does.

### QUALITY-22 [info] rooms.ts builds eight Zod schemas nobody uses, one of them a hand copy of the jewel options
src/admin/collections/rooms.ts:22 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:QUALITY-06 (confirmed, first half); same class as AUDIT-1 X6.x; S-8 (FIX-A1-01/-03) covers .splitCenter and FieldType, not these
EVIDENCE: `grep -rn` over src, tests, scripts and supabase/functions finds only the declarations of jewelSchema (22), reelMediaSchema (39), galleryPhotoSchema (45), builtMovementSchema (81), thuraFlavourSchema (170), thuraItemSchema (187), moonlightCupItemSchema (196), boutiqueItemSchema (205). Seven are `schemaFromFields(...)` calls run on every import of the collection registry (the admin bundle and the build); `jewelSchema = z.enum(['coral', 'aub', 'saffron', 'paper'])` repeats JEWEL_FIELD.options (26-27), from which the real schemas derive.
IMPACT: No runtime defect: dead exports a maintainer may edit or trust (a new room colour added to jewelSchema changes nothing), and eight schemas constructed for nothing on each admin load.
FIX: Delete the eight unused `*Schema` exports and keep the `*Fields` lists.

### QUALITY-23 [info] Two helpers survive only for their unit tests: cart.ts removeLine and money-input.ts riyadhLocalToIso
src/lib/cart.ts:140 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:QUALITY-06 (removeLine half); AUDIT-1 X6.6 left riyadhLocalToIso behind when the OrNull variant replaced it
EVIDENCE: removeLine (cart.ts:140) is imported only by tests/unit/cart.test.ts (41, 141, 339); the cart removes lines with removeLines (CartView.tsx:181). riyadhLocalToIso (money-input.ts:54) is used only by tests/unit/money-input.test.ts; PublishBar and FieldInput call riyadhLocalToIsoOrNull, and tests/e2e/helpers.ts:59 still calls it 'the format PublishBar's riyadhLocalToIso expects'.
IMPACT: No runtime defect; the tests of these two functions prove nothing about the code the screens run.
FIX: Delete removeLine (and its test cases, or point them at removeLines) and riyadhLocalToIso (keep riyadhLocalToIsoOrNull's tests); fix the e2e helper comment.

### QUALITY-24 [info] The Riyadh day is written three times and the dispute form's day check is weaker than the catalog's: it accepts 2026-02-30
src/lib/admin-money.ts:422 fix:small conf:high prior:lead:QUALITY-13 (confirmed and extended with the day-check drift)
EVIDENCE: riyadhToday: admin-money.ts:389, admin-commerce.ts:55 and supabase/functions/_shared/disputes.ts:44 (identical). The dispute form checks `!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(input.day) || Number.isNaN(Date.parse(input.day))` (422), but V8 parses '2026-02-30' as 2026-03-02 (`node -e "Date.parse('2026-02-30')"` → 1772409600000), while admin-commerce.ts:60-64 isDay refuses it by round-tripping, and the server's `z.iso.date()` refuses it too (zod 4.6.5: '2026-02-30' → false). Same family: MediaUpload.tsx:25-28 restates media.ts:16-25's limits, admin-commerce.ts:102 restates PAID_FILE_MAX_BYTES, MediaLibrary.tsx:111 formatBytes equals admin-commerce.ts:136 formatFileSize, OrderPage.tsx:135 inlines preorderSentence (quote.ts:60).
IMPACT: Not reachable through the date picker today (the server refuses an impossible day with 422 «بيانات غير صالحة.»); each pair is a place where a change to one side leaves the other refusing or saying something else.
FIX: Use isDay in buildDispute and one exported riyadhToday (a pure _shared module both libs and disputes.ts import); move the media and paid-file limits into pure modules both sides import.

### QUALITY-25 [info] Comments and branches left from earlier architectures point maintainers at code that no longer exists
supabase/functions/_shared/outbox.ts:121 fix:trivial conf:high prior:lead:QUALITY-14 (partly; its CollectionForm token item is QUALITY-12 here)
EVIDENCE: outbox.ts renderFor types `created_at: Date | string` and branches on `contact.created_at instanceof Date` (121-125), but the row is JSON from the Data API (processRow types it `created_at: string`, 205-210). media.ts:168-170 explains dropping file-type because 'the Worker bundle cannot resolve' strtok3 (no Worker since D32). content.ts:288-292 says Picture.tsx reads its own manifest JSON (it goes through images.ts). SettingsView.tsx:7 names `settingsStatusAction` (now the admin `status` action). StepUp.tsx:12-13 says the caller retries 'the original staff-admin call' (the money screens and the settings use it too). TeamView.tsx:94 says 'BAN_FAILED commits active first' (F1-9 now reverts it). db.ts:1-4 calls named SQL functions 'the Edge Functions' only database path' (staff-admin and staff.ts use table access).
IMPACT: No runtime defect; misleading pointers for whoever maintains the functions and the admin next.
FIX: Drop the Date branch and correct the six comments.

### QUALITY-26 [info] staff-admin is the last function that reads its whole body before the size check, and measures it in UTF-16 units
supabase/functions/staff-admin/index.ts:59 fix:trivial conf:high prior:AUDIT-1 X6.1 named three other handlers; EF-ADMIN-03 / F1-13 fixed admin.ts only
EVIDENCE: `const text = await req.text(); if (text.length > 4096) return fail(413, ...)` (59-60). Every other handler now uses http.ts boundedText (byte count, declared length refused before reading, stream cancelled at the limit), admin.ts included since F1-13 (uncommitted).
IMPACT: Bounded: the read happens only after the owner, fresh-TOTP check. Kept for consistency so the one pattern the audits converged on is the only one.
FIX: `const text = await boundedText(req, 4096); if (text === null) return fail(413, ...)`.

### READER-01 [info] The whole book-source manifest, with the owner's internal approval note and the BOOK_ASSETS file names, ships in /book's initial JS
src/lib/book-preview.ts:1 fix:small conf:high prior:AUDIT-2 PUBLIC-data-1 (same defect, refuted by both AUDIT-2 runs); still present in the 2026-10-07 export; recorded as info so the recheck is on file, not as a new finding
EVIDENCE: book-preview.ts imports the full JSON (`import manifest from '../../content/book-source-manifest.json'`) to read only output.url and pages[].label; ClosedBook/BookPreview are client components, so the whole object is bundled. `grep -o "ANAS provide..." out/_next/static/chunks/2667kept7dvnv.js` prints `ANAS provide two or three PDF texts !! we only want to let users see these then had to buy t`; the same chunk holds `BOOK_ASSETS/ (8).pdf`, `(11).pdf`, `(9).pdf` and the three input sha256 values. 2667kept7dvnv.js is one of the ten <script src> tags of out/book.html (initial load, before the book is opened); 2uzaovfm1pqub.js (admin preview) carries the same.
IMPACT: Every visitor of /book downloads the owner's private, informal instruction about Anas's manuscript and the internal names and hashes of the unpublished source files; anyone reading the page source can quote it. No secret or email is exposed (the manifest has none), so the harm is reputational and a small information leak about the unpublished BOOK_ASSETS.
FIX: Import only what the client needs: have scripts/prepare-preview.py also write a small public JSON (url plus [{page,label}]) and import that in src/lib/book-preview.ts, keeping book-source-manifest.json (approval, inputs, hashes) for the script and the tests only.


# Prior-fix checks not holding
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] AUDIT-2 X-A11Y-RTL-11: Store forms announce why Save cannot proceed and mark the offending inputs invalid — Partly: TableForm.tsx:361-396 has the always-mounted role=status summary and an aria-disabled Save that focuses it; but no input other than the money field carries aria-invalid/aria-describedby (FieldInput.tsx:349 is the only one). Reported as ADMIN-COMMERCE-16.
- [ADMIN-CMS] AUDIT-1 S08.6: A click into a rich-text body does not mark the document changed — Holds for non-empty bodies (key-order-insensitive equalData, fields.ts:115-121). For an empty stored body Lexical adds a paragraph at init and the first selection update reaches the form, so the document reads as changed (ADMIN-CMS-05).
- [ADMIN-CMS] AUDIT-1 S10.8: Owner cannot revoke or demote himself with one click — Only the revoke has a confirm (TeamView.tsx:219-222); the role «حفظ» (196-204) has none. Already found in this audit (wave B FIX-A1-02), so not re-reported.
- [ADMIN-CMS] AUDIT-2 ADMIN-editor-11 / X-A11Y-RTL-13: List item buttons are named per item; delete has an undo — aria-labels exist (FieldInput.tsx:643-663, 717-737); «حذف» still removes at once with no confirm or undo (663, 737): ADMIN-CMS-13.
- [QUALITY] AUDIT-2 STORE-checkout-5: a failed or empty city list is reported with a retry instead of a dead select — Holds in CheckoutForm.tsx (citiesState, 188-195, 690-710) but the identical swallowing line AUDIT-2 quoted survives in CartView.tsx:116 `fetchCities().then(setCities).catch(() => setCities([]))` with no message or retry (QUALITY-08).
- [QUALITY] FABLE-AUDIT EF-ADMIN-11 / F1-6 (in flight): the contact notice drops the visitor's own bidi controls the way one() does — F1-6 added VISITOR_BIDI (U+202A-U+202E, U+2066-U+2069 only; email.ts:232), narrower than the Bidi_Control and Cc cleanup of one() (346-352) that EF-ADMIN-11's own fix asked for; the name keeps U+000B, U+000C, U+001C-U+001E, U+200E, U+200F and U+061C (QUALITY-03).
- [QUALITY] FABLE-AUDIT EF-ADMIN-16 / F1-9 (in flight): after BAN_FAILED the member is revocable again and the screen says what happened — staff-admin/index.ts:137 now puts the member back to active, but line 146 still says «حُدّثت الحالة لكن تعذّر إيقاف الدخول»; the revert's result is unchecked (QUALITY-01).
- [SOON] MAINT-1 review: design B fits short screens: Design B (and /m/b/) stays on one screen on short screens — Holds for short portrait phones (320x568, 360x640, 375x667: pageH = viewport). Not in landscape: 667x375 pageH 492 (+117), 844x390 pageH 492 (+102), 720x450 (200% zoom of 1440x900) pageH 497 (+47), links and domain below the fold (SCRATCH shots/v-b-844x390-extra.png); the word size floors at 96px (`--F: max(96px, ...)`, v/b/style.css line 49) so heights under about 530px cannot fit. FIX-A2-01 found 4-17px at 520-700px portrait widths; same root.
- [SOON] SOON-1 row: one screen from 360 wide, no horizontal overflow from 320 to 1920: All designs are one screen from 360 wide; none overflows horizontally — Horizontal overflow: 0 at every size 280 to 3440 wide (holds). One screen: holds in portrait from 360 wide; design C scrolls 52px at 320x568 (as recorded); in landscape phones every design scrolls (A +35/+40, B +102/+117, C +132/+145 at 844x390/667x375), design C by its own comment (v/c/style.css line 273).
- [STORE-UI] AUDIT-2 STORE-checkout-5: A failed or empty city list is reported with a retry — Holds in CheckoutForm (citiesState, lines 187-195, 690-710); CartView.tsx:116 still swallows the failure (`.catch(() => setCities([]))`), rendered in p11.out.txt. Reported as STORE-UI-12.
- [STORE-UI] AUDIT-2 GAP-G4-4: Control characters in the buyer's name produce an error named on the field — The function now refuses them with fieldErrors.name (checkout.ts:94) and the form puts it under the name, but as «أدخل الاسم.» while the field is filled; the client never strips the character (p11.out.txt). Reported as STORE-UI-11.
- [STORE-UI] P08 R10A-5: No static page bakes a loading line that only script could end — Holds for /orders and /notify/* (no-js.spec.ts:106-114); out/checkout/return.html, out/cart.html and out/checkout.html still carry «جارٍ التحقق من الدفع…», «جارٍ تحميل السلة…», «جارٍ تحميل الطلب…» (p4 run). Reported as STORE-UI-03.
- [A11Y-RTL] AUDIT-1 S14.5: Hold and cancel results are announced and focus moves into the order box — It holds on success: a cancel that succeeds focuses the order box and announces «أُلغي الطلب.» (flows.mjs G). It does not hold on failure: a create or cancel that fails leaves activeElement on BODY (flows.mjs D and F). See A11Y-RTL-04.
- [DSN-HOME] P01 round 3 audit fix 1 (scripts/prepare-media.mjs:240-246): The largest derivative is never smaller than the source, so the browser never upscales — The script has the rule, but the committed public/images/manifest.json and derivatives predate it: 40 of 62 entries end below sourceWidth (render-83 1134 -> 720, render-84 1134 -> 720, factory-mug-inside 960 -> 720, raha-logo 793 -> 720, products 1206 -> 1200); the build does not run the script. See DSN-HOME-05.
- [DSN-HOME] AUDIT-2 PUBLIC-style-8: The film VideoTile focuses after «تشغيل» has a visible focus ring — video.module.css:14-16 draws the ring inside, so it is no longer clipped, but it is one colour over the moving picture: aubergine on /built, barely visible on a dark frame (shots/vfocus-built.png). Partly holds; DSN-HOME-14.

# Plan gaps
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] before_launch OWNER | P08 residual / P10 | Entitlement administration: re-point buyers to a replaced paid file, and restore a file a refund revoked
  WHY: paid_asset_set gives a new file only to entitlements with no file (20261002140000_delivery.sql: 'An entitlement that already has a file keeps it'), and the variant panel says so (REPLACE_NOTE), so a wrong first upload or a corrected edition never reaches the buyers who already received the old one; a refund that empties a digital line revokes its entitlement for good (only dispute revocations can be undone by «إبقاء الملفات», fable_audit_operations.sql:1894-1907). The admin has no action for either, and the owner learns it only from a buyer.
  TOUCHES: supabase/migrations (a new owner function: re-point a variant's entitlements to an asset, restore an entitlement), supabase/functions/_shared/admin.ts (step-up), src/components/admin/VariantCommerce.tsx and OrderView.tsx (the controls)
- [ADMIN-CMS] before_launch | P10 (monitoring and admin error flows) or P11 (hosted setup) | An owner control to rebuild the site after a failed or broken build
  WHY: site_build_trigger gives one publish five tries, then keeps triggered_at so nothing more is built until the next publish (20260930140000_audit2_fixes.sql:750-769); the home shows only a grey «بناء الموقع: فاشل» (AdminHome.tsx:371-386, ADMIN-CMS-22). No admin control or callable function requests a rebuild (site_build_request is granted to no API role; grep of src and supabase/functions finds no caller), and docs/operations.md 'Site rebuilds' (383-406) never says the owner must publish something again after fixing the hook. An owner who repairs the deploy hook and waits keeps a stale site with his last publishes missing.
  TOUCHES: an owner-only SQL function wrapping site_build_request() (role checked inside, granted to authenticated); a «أعد بناء الموقع» button on the home's «بناء الموقع» line when the last run failed or was skipped; docs/operations.md 'Site rebuilds'; one integration test
- [QUALITY] before_P10 | P10 | P10's Sentry for the Edge Functions has nothing to capture: every function catches its errors and answers 5xx without rethrow or log
  WHY: D23 and WORK-PACKAGES P10 plan Sentry Free for checkout, webhook and scheduler errors in the Edge Functions, with only `supabase/functions/_shared/` named. All 51 5xx paths catch the error (or meet an unrecognised SQL reply) and return a reply (QUALITY-04), and the three jobs (outbox, payments_reconcile, media_sweep) swallow row and run failures by design. A Sentry init alone captures uncaught exceptions only, so the P10 proof 'Sentry redacted synthetic checkout/webhook/scheduler error ... actually observed' could pass with a thrown test error while real failures stay invisible.
  TOUCHES: supabase/functions/_shared/http.ts (fail), the rpc failure helpers in checkout.ts, orders.ts, download.ts, notify.ts, contact.ts, admin.ts, refunds.ts, disputes.ts, paid-files.ts, payments.ts, outbox.ts, media-sweep.ts; PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md P10 file list and proof
- [DSN-PAGES] before_checkout_opens | P08 (before acceptance) or a named P09 item | Book purchase wiring: P07 listed it, nobody built it, and no later package owns it
  WHY: WORK-PACKAGES P07 names «book purchase wiring» among its files, P07 was accepted without it, and neither P08-CONTRACT nor P09-P11 mention /book. Today /book ends in a hard-coded «قريباً» / «السعر يُعلن قريباً» with no link (DSN-PAGES-08), no public page links /store, and the book page editions (CMS) and the store variants (catalog) are edited in two places with nothing tying them. Before the owner turns the «الشراء» switch on, the book page must show the real editions, prices and a way to buy, and the nav needs a decision about «المتجر».
  TOUCHES: src/components/public/book/BookView.tsx, src/app/(public)/book/page.tsx, src/admin/collections/rooms.ts (an edition product slug), src/lib/store.ts (read at build), site_settings.nav (owner)
- [DSN-PAGES] before_checkout_opens OWNER | P08 / E08 with the owner | Store policies are reachable only from the checkout, the availability form and the order page
  WHY: grep of src outside the admin: /policies/* is linked only by CheckoutForm, AvailabilityForm and OrderPage; the footer (Footer.tsx) links المَشاهد, تواصل and one social handle. A buyer who wants the refund or delivery terms before adding to the cart, or a visitor of /contact (which collects name, e-mail and message, I43), has no path to them. Moyasar says it reviews «pricing and refund policy» and «how customers can contact you» before live payments (https://moyasar.com/en/resources/faqs/). The footer is Anas's poem and signature by design (DESIGN-AUDIT 98), so where the links go is his call.

  WHY: Checkout binds each order to a policy seq (P08-CONTRACT section 6, src/lib/policies.ts), but /policies/<slug> shows only the title and body (policies/[slug]/page.tsx:39-53) and the order page links the current /policies/refund (OrderPage.tsx:285). After Anas edits a policy, a buyer cannot tell which text they accepted or read it again. Whether to show «آخر تحديث» and archive old revisions is a legal-wording choice (E08).

  WHY: The export has no published post (out/journal/ holds only the placeholder _), so the visual suite and every design audit so far never rendered the article page: its title size, quotation style and bold (DSN-PAGES-05/06/07) went unseen. tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts runs 360/768/1024/1440 only (the header overflow at 320, DSN-PAGES-01, and WCAG 1.4.10 sit at 320), and the lightbox test (public.spec.ts:109-122) checks the counter, never that the photo fits (DSN-PAGES-10).
  TOUCHES: scripts/seed-demo-catalog.mjs or db:import (a demo post with h2, h3, quote, lists, bold, a long title), tests/e2e/visual.spec.ts, tests/e2e/public.spec.ts
- [SOON] before_launch | P11 | The Worker-route option for the gate has no rule to keep static files off the script; measured 18-21 same-origin requests per first page view
  WHY: Contract line 51 asks for `_routes.json` 'so that only pages, not files, call it' only for the Pages Function option. A Worker route on anas.studio/* runs the script for every request: one first view of the real export (out/ on :4173, Chromium) made 21 same-origin requests on / (11 _next/static, 5 fonts, 4 images), 18 on /store and /orders, 21 on /admin, so the 100,000-a-day allowance is about 5,000 first views, and with fail-closed the whole site (and /admin) shows error 1027 past it. Cloudflare allows more specific routes with no Worker to exclude paths.
  TOUCHES: PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md line 51; P11 traffic check
- [SOON] before_P11 | SITE-STATE-1 / P11 | Where SITE-STATE-1's hosted acceptance switches the three states without touching the public domain
  WHY: Contract line 69 switches 'the domain' through all three states on the single hosted project before the launch step «افتح الموقع» (line 49); `open` there either opens the unlaunched site or, on the custom-domain Worker, loops (SOON-03). The acceptance needs its own hostname and row, or must be folded into the launch.
  TOUCHES: PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md lines 49, 69; PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md line 143
- [SOON] before_P11 | SITE-STATE-1 | What the gate does when its own code throws: an uncaught exception answers Cloudflare error 1101 on every path, /admin included
  WHY: The gate runs in front of /admin and the buyer pages it promises to keep reachable (contract lines 43, 63), but the contract only specifies failed reads. Cloudflare: an unhandled exception is error 1101 for that request; ctx.passThroughOnException() would instead fail open to the origin, which in soon/maintenance shows the hidden site; neither covers CPU-limit errors (https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/runtime-apis/context/ , https://developers.cloudflare.com/workers/observability/errors/). A top-level try/catch that passes the exempt paths through and answers the rest from MODE keeps the owner able to switch back.
  TOUCHES: PLANS/SITE-STATE-CONTRACT.md lines 43-44 (the pure function and its node --test file); artifacts/soon/worker.js
- [STORE-UI] before_checkout_opens OWNER | P11 (E08) / FABLE-AUDIT D | Checkout and the availability sign-up can collect personal data with no privacy policy shown or agreed
  WHY: commerce_policies_approve builds the approved set from store, delivery and refund and adds privacy only when one is published (`case when v_privacy is null then '{}'`), so checkout opens without it; the consent sentence lists only the approved policies (CheckoutForm.tsx:511-526), and the sign-up shows no privacy link and sends consentRevision null while none is published (AvailabilityForm.tsx:192-198; the current export's privacyRevision is null). E08 requires approved privacy text before live data collection, but nothing in the code or the opening checklist enforces it (I43 covers the contact form only).
  TOUCHES: supabase/migrations (commerce_policies_approve, or the checkout readiness rule), src/components/store/AvailabilityForm.tsx, the go-live checklist
- [STORE-UI] before_launch | FABLE-AUDIT F3 or P10 | One buyer's two checkout sessions make two payable orders for the same cart, and the earlier tab keeps inviting payment after the other was paid
  WHY: ACTIVE_HOLD is per checkout session (20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:482-497) and the per-email hold was dropped (X-SEC-4), while the session id and the pending order live in each tab's sessionStorage; after paying in tab B (whose return page empties the shared cart), tab A still shows «ادفع الآن» for its own order until its hold ends, with nothing saying these items were just bought. A confused buyer can pay twice; the owner must then refund. No plan item weighs this residual or a mitigation (e.g. the hold view re-asking `pay` when the shared cart is emptied by a paid return, or a server-side note when the same email paid the same lines minutes earlier).
  TOUCHES: src/components/store/HoldView.tsx, src/lib/cart.ts (a cross-tab signal in localStorage), possibly supabase/functions/_shared/checkout.ts
- [STORE-UI] before_launch | P10 / P11 launch checklist | Size the per-IP checkout throttles for buyers who share one IPv4 address on Saudi mobile networks before the launch peak
  WHY: checkout_create allows 10 orders per hour per salted IP hash and checkout_quote 300 quotes per hour (20261002110000_checkout_payment.sql:346 for the quote and 479 for create; the create hit is taken before the session-hold check and the pricing, so a refused create spends it too); the cart quotes on every edit (a dedication typed slowly makes one per pause), and a buyer behind a throttled address reads «تعذّر تحديث الأسعار؛ أعد المحاولة بعد لحظات.» or «أرسلت طلبات كثيرة؛ حاول لاحقًا.». DATA-AND-SECURITY.md:69 asks that IP throttling stay secondary 'so shared networks remain usable', but no plan item measures the thresholds against carrier NAT: about a third of Saudi users still reach sites over IPv4 only (pulse.internetsociety.org/en/reports/SA/: 66% can use IPv6), and a book launch concentrates many buyers on few carrier addresses in one hour.
  TOUCHES: supabase/migrations (rate_limit_take keys and limits for checkout:ip and checkout-quote:ip), docs/operations.md (launch-day watch), a P10 load check

# Vendor claims false or unverifiable
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] false: After a partial refund Moyasar sets the payment status to 'refunded' (the code assumes 'refunded' means fully reversed) (supabase/migrations/20261007100000_fable_audit_payments.sql:1002 (order_resolve treats provider_status 'refunded' as a reversal); tests/integration/order-operations.test.ts:1384-1389 models a partial refund as status 'paid') — Moyasar's guide, under «Partial Refund»: 'The payment status changes to `refunded`.' https://docs.moyasar.com/docs/guides/payment-operations/index.md (fetched 2026-10-07); the emulator default partialRefundKeepsPaid:false agrees (tests/support/moyasar-emulator.ts:220, :753). The code's assumption is false; filed as ADMIN-COMMERCE-04.
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] false: «تحتاج مطابقة» is the sum of what the reconciliation screen lists (src/lib/admin-orders.ts:231-233) — orders_alerts counts uncertain/parked refunds, UNVERIFIED/MODE_CHANGED attempts, exhausted events and external refunds (fable_audit_operations.sql:418-430), while reconciliation_list also lists uncertain creations, PROVIDER_STATUS attempts, every in-flight refund and unprocessed events (fable_audit_payments.sql:770-830). Already recorded as wave A CONTRACT-13 (open).
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] false: After an action 'the order is read again whatever the answer (a refusal may mean it changed)' (src/components/admin/OrderView.tsx:164-168) — act() re-reads only in the non-error branch (OrderView.tsx:181-192): an RPC error (a lost reply, code '') says «تعذّر الحفظ. حاول مرة أخرى.» and leaves the old state drawn although the change may have committed; the retry then meets a repeat (changed 0) or BAD_TRANSITION. Low impact, not filed.
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] false: A STEP_UP_REQUIRED reply opens the dialog and the same save retries exactly once (src/components/admin/CommerceSettingsForm.tsx:7-8) — onStepUpVerified (:133-142) sends again and applyResult (:102-121) reopens the dialog on another STEP_UP_REQUIRED, so the retry is not bounded to one (harmless in practice).
- [ADMIN-COMMERCE] false: The refund key is kept for a retry after a network failure and dropped only on a final answer (src/components/admin/RefundView.tsx:9-12; src/lib/admin-money.ts:216-226) — A retry answered 503 PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE (refunds.ts:232, before the SQL duplicate check) drops the key (admin-money.ts:226, :302). Filed as ADMIN-COMMERCE-02.
- [ADMIN-CMS] false: Content is 'Validated with Zod in the admin before publishing and again when the site is built; an invalid published document fails the build' (PLANS/DATA-AND-SECURITY.md:13) — True for posts, policies, taxonomies and scenes (journal.ts:77-78, policies.ts:29, content.ts:283-286). False for site settings and rooms: the build parses siteSettingsStoredSchema and the lenient room schemas (content.ts:166-171, 257-276), so siteSettingsSchema's and roomPublishSchemas' rules are never re-checked (ADMIN-CMS-07).
- [ADMIN-CMS] false: «Autosave keeps an unsaved local copy in localStorage so a conflict or a closed tab never loses text» (src/components/admin/CollectionForm.tsx:7-8) — A second tab of the same document removes or overwrites the copy (ADMIN-CMS-01), a never-saved post's copy cannot be reached (ADMIN-CMS-02), and text typed during a post-action reload is not stored (ADMIN-CMS-14).
- [DSN-PAGES] false: The contact form leaves room for the Turnstile widget (.turnstile min-block-size 70px) and the page does not overflow at 320 (src/components/public/contact/contact.module.css:57) — Cloudflare widget-configurations page: normal 300x65, flexible 100% (min 300px) x 65, compact 150x140. The contact form column at 320 is 280px, so a normal or flexible widget forces the form to 300px (measured with a 300x65 stand-in: form [0,300] at vw 320, left gutter gone).
- [DSN-PAGES] false: The article page shows no date yet (it is added in PostView.tsx) (PLANS/ARCHITECTURE.md:82 and PLANS/WORK-PACKAGES.md P01) — PostView.tsx:45 renders <time dateTime={post.publishedAt}>{formatDate(post.publishedAt)}</time> in the meta line; the plan text is stale.
- [DSN-PAGES] false: A size that belongs to one place is still a token (--size-post-title); change a type size everywhere by editing its --size-* token (DESIGN.md:81 and :212) — --size-post-title never applies: .t-title (globals.css, loaded after the CSS-module chunk) wins over .postTitle at equal specificity; the post title is 68-200px (--size-title). See DSN-PAGES-05.
- [DSN-PAGES] false: No clipped glyph or overflow at 360, 768, 1024 or 1440 (DESIGN-AUDIT D39 recheck 43/45/59/60) (PLANS/DESIGN-AUDIT.md:192) — True for the exported pages at 360+, but the export never contained a journal post: at 360 a post title with «الاستراتيجيات» is 336px in a 320px column (titlewords.mjs); at 320 every page overflows by 22px (DSN-PAGES-01), outside the claim's widths but inside WCAG 1.4.10.
- [DSN-PAGES] unverifiable: In the export global-not-found becomes 404.html, which Pages serves for unknown paths (I27: «renders the Arabic page with status 404») (next.config.ts:33-36; PLANS/ISSUES.md:93) — https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/configuration/serving-pages/: «Pages will then attempt to find the closest 404 page ... ending in /404.html», and without a top-level 404.html Pages falls back to SPA behaviour; the page does not state the status code. out/404.html is the Arabic page (lang ar, dir rtl, theme-color); the 404 status was only seen on the local static server, so the hosted status needs a P10/P11 check.
- [SOON] false: Always over HTTPS: a page asked for in plain HTTP is sent to its secure address (artifacts/soon/worker.js line 16) — Only for addresses the script handles; assets are served first (same doc as above). Live http://anas.studio/v/c/ -> 200 text/html over HTTP. Recorded open item (zone Always Use HTTPS, EXECUTION-STATUS next work 8).
- [SOON] false: Nothing else leaves the domain (artifacts/soon/README.md line 59) — Every live response carries Cloudflare's `Report-To: {"group":"cf-nel",...,"url":"https://a.nel.cloudflare.com/report/v4?s=..."}` and `Nel: {"report_to":"cf-nel","success_fraction":0.0,...}`, so browsers that support Network Error Logging send failure reports to a.nel.cloudflare.com. Minor; not a page request.
- [READER] false: A key pressed while a page is still turning is ignored (docs/book-preview.md:64) — PdfBookReader.tsx go()/turn() store the last move in pendingRef while page-flip's state is 'flipping' and run it at 'read'. t4.mjs: two ArrowLeft presses 80 ms apart from «الإهداء 1 / 5» end on «3–4 / 5» (READER-15).
- [READER] unverifiable: The pdf.js worker (/_next/static/media/pdf.worker.min.*.mjs) is served by Cloudflare Pages with a JavaScript MIME type, as a module worker requires (src/components/book/pdf.ts:20 (workerSrc); deployment) — The local static server is not Cloudflare. Cloudflare's 2025-08-25 changelog (https://developers.cloudflare.com/changelog/2025-08-25-workers-assets-javascript-content-type/) says JavaScript asset responses use text/javascript but names Workers Assets, not Pages, and lists no extensions. One request against the Pages preview settles it: `curl -sI https://<preview>.pages.dev/_next/static/media/pdf.worker.min.3bl-ygmetel-a.mjs` must show text/javascript (or application/javascript); anything else would fail every book open at launch.
- [DSN-HOME] false: Bands meet at a triangle or weave edge, never a raw seam (D39 recheck rows 54, 75) (PLANS/DESIGN-AUDIT.md:194) — seams.mjs at 1440: seven band openings with no edge (/started 2, /built 1, /passed 1, /shelf 3); DSN-HOME-07.
- [DSN-HOME] false: The home's first screen is the name bands and the portrait (row 66) (PLANS/DESIGN-AUDIT.md:197) — fold.mjs: true at 360x640; at 1366x657 only «أنس» and «عبدالله» show, at 1440x789 and 1280x720 «القرني» is half below the fold, at 1536x730 47px of it show (DSN-HOME-17).
- [DSN-HOME] false: Everything is visible without motion, without JavaScript, when printed and in a background tab (DESIGN.md:136) — Reduced motion and JS off: true for the slice (survey.mjs, nojs.mjs). Printed: the two actions, muted door facts and footer links print pale on white (print3.mjs; DSN-HOME-24).
- [PERF] false: The two preloaded faces are the ones every page's first screen is set in (src/app/(public)/layout.tsx:33) — document.fonts after load shows Thmanyah Sans 400 (not preloaded) on all 22 public pages measured and on /admin; it sets the body text (globals.css:141-146). See PERF-05.
- [PERF] false: pnpm check:budgets is the proof of the public page budget (HTML+CSS+fonts+hero <1MiB and initial JS <150KiB) (PLANS/VERIFICATION.md:55-57) — check-budgets.mjs checks the JavaScript budget only (line 22, lines 54-94). See PERF-06.
- [PERF] false: Lazy PDF/editor/board chunks are measured separately (PLANS/VERIFICATION.md:55) — No script or test measures them; measured here: reader chunks 148 KB + worker 307 KB br + PDF 365 KB (PERF-12).
- [PERF] false: The largest page's initial JavaScript is 147.4 of its 150 KiB budget (PLANS/ISSUES.md I49 (5)) — Stale since P08 round 10: the largest is checkout.html at 148.9 KiB (I50 (2)); the largest room page is book.html at 141.8 KiB, the largest product page 144.5 KiB.
- [PERF] false: A full prepare-media run would add about 40 new image sizes because some local sources are now wider than when the committed derivatives were made (PLANS/ISSUES.md I49 (6)) — The committed manifest has recorded sourceWidth 2048/2160/2688 for 16 entries since 70be5c9 with derivatives up to 1800; prepare-media.mjs:246 adds the source width for any source wider than the last breakpoint. See PERF-08.
- [PERF] unverifiable: Pages serves the reader's module worker (out/_next/static/media/pdf.worker.min.*.mjs) with a JavaScript MIME type and compressed, so pdf.js can start it (PLANS/VERIFICATION.md:45 (the PDF.js worker is to be checked on the hosted preview); src/components/book/pdf.ts) — https://developers.cloudflare.com/pages/configuration/serving-pages/ lists "Content-Type: $CONTENT_TYPE" without an extension table; the 2025-08-25 changelog (text/javascript for JS assets) is about Workers static assets. A module worker needs a JavaScript MIME type, and uncompressed the worker is 1,265,413 B instead of ~307 KB br. Proof on the P10 preview: `curl -sI -H "accept-encoding: br" https://<preview>.pages.dev/_next/static/media/pdf.worker.min.<hash>.mjs` must show content-type text/javascript (or application/javascript) and content-encoding br or gzip.

Checked and found not to be defects:
- The footer poem blank in full-page screenshots: a capture artifact; it shows after scrolling.

- Weave fills anchored at 0 0: the pattern is seamless.
- Soon-page contrast and ring flags: checker artifacts from gradient backgrounds; the real text sits on aubergine.
- A service request leaving focus off-screen: a race in my test harness, not reproduced when the scroll was allowed to finish.
- Long Arabic post titles at 320: the longest real words measured are 277px or less against 280px available.
